# Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra

ברטנורא על משנה בבא בתרא

Link: [https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra)

חיבור התשתית הקדום של חז״ל, המתעד מגוון דעות הלכתיות ממסורת התורה שבעל פה. נחתם בשנת 200 לספירה לערך.

16th-century commentary printed in most editions of the Mishnah, including summaries of talmudic discussions and legal conclusions.

Sections: Chapter-Mishnah-Comment

Location: Mishnah/Rishonim on Mishnah/Bartenura/Seder Nezikin

## Chapter 1


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 1:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:1:1)


**הַשֻּׁתָּפִין שֶׁרָצוּ לַעֲשׂוֹת מְחִצָּה.** בְּחָצֵר שֶׁאֵין בָּהּ דִּין חֲלֻקָּה מַיְרֵי, שֶׁאֵין בָּהּ כְּדֵי אַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת לְכָל אֶחָד מִן הַשֻּׁתָּפִין. לְהָכִי קָתָנֵי שֶׁרָצוּ, דְּמַשְׁמַע דַּוְקָא שְׁנֵיהֶם רוֹצִים, אֲבָל אֵין אֶחָד מֵהֶם יָכוֹל לָכוֹף אֶת חֲבֵרוֹ. דְּאִלּוּ בְּחָצֵר שֶׁיֵּשׁ בָּהּ דִּין חֲלֻקָּה, כָּל אֶחָד יָכוֹל לָכוֹף אֶת חֲבֵרוֹ לַחֲלֹק. וְהַשְׁתָּא אַשְׁמוֹעִינַן דְּכֵיוָן שֶׁרָצוּ לַחֲלֹק וְהֶחֱזִיק כָּל אֶחָד מֵהֶם בָּרוּחַ הַמַּגִּיעַ לוֹ כְּפִי מַה שֶּׁנִּתְפַּשְּׁרוּ, בּוֹנִין אֶת הַכֹּתֶל בָּאֶמְצַע. שֶׁזֶּה נוֹתֵן מֵחֶלְקוֹ חֲצִי מָקוֹם עֳבִי הַכֹּתֶל וְכֵן זֶה:


השותפין שרצו לעשות מחיצה – we are speaking of a courtyard where there is no law of division in it, where there lacks by the way four cubits for each one of the partners. For this reason, it (i.e., the Mishnah) taught " "שרצו/that they wanted, which implies that especially, two of them want [to make a partition] but neither one of them is able to force his fellow, for if in a courtyard which has a law of division, each one of them is able to force his fellow to divide, but now, it comes to teach us that since they wanted to divide it, and each one of them had taken possession of the direction that is entitled for him according to what they had compromised, they build the wall in the middle, and this one gives from his portion one-half of the place the thickness of the wall and also this one.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 1:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:1:2)


**גָּוִיל.** אֲבָנִים שֶׁאֵינָם מְשֻׁפּוֹת וּמְתֻקָּנוֹת:


גויל – stones which are not trimmed or fixed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 1:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:1:3)


**גָּזִית.** אֲבָנִים מְשֻׁפּוֹת וּמְגֹרָרוֹת בִּמְגֵרָה:


גזית – stones which are trimmed and planed with a saw.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 1:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:1:4)


**כְּפִיסִין.** אָרִיחַ. שֶׁהוּא חֲצִי לְבֵנָה, וְהוּא טֶפַח וּמֶחֱצָה. וְהַלְּבֵנָה שְׁלֹשָׁה טְפָחִים:


כפיסין – a small brick/girder which is a one-half brick which is a handbreadth-and-a-half (i.e., one handbreadth equals 3.65 inches or 9.34 cm) and a brick is three handbreadths.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 1:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:1:5)


**הַכֹּל כְּמִנְהַג הַמְּדִינָה.** הַכֹּל לַאֲתוֹיֵי אַתְרָא דִּנְהִיגֵי לַעֲשׂוֹת מְחִצָּה בְּלוּלְבֵי גְּפָנִים וְעַנְפֵי עֵצִים, שֶׁעוֹשִׂים כְּפִי הַמִּנְהָג. וּבִלְבַד שֶׁתְּהֵא הַמְּחִצָּה עָבָה שֶׁלֹּא יוּכַל אֶחָד מֵהֶם לְהַבִּיט בְּשֶׁל חֲבֵרוֹ, דְּהֶזֵּק רְאִיָּה שְׁמֵיהּ הֶזֵּק. וְגֹבַהּ הַכֹּתֶל וְהַמְּחִצָּה אֵין פָּחוּת מֵאַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת:


הכל כמנהג המדינה – "הכל"/everything includes a place where they have the practice to make a partition with the sprouts of vine and the branches of trees that they make according to the custom, as long as the partition is wide enough so that neither one of them cis able to see that of his fellow for visual damage is called damage, and the height of the wall and the partition is not less than four cubits.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 1:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:1:6)


**זֶה נוֹתֵן שְׁלֹשָׁה טְפָחִים.** שֶׁכֹּתֶל הַגָּוִיל צָרִיךְ טֶפַח יוֹתֵר מִכֹּתֶל הַגָּזִית, בִּשְׁבִיל רָאשֵׁי הָאֲבָנִים שֶׁבּוֹלְטוֹת לַחוּץ שֶׁאֵינָן חֲלָקוֹת:


זה נותן שלשה טפחים – that the wall of untrimmed stones requires an additional handbreadth more than the wall of hewn stones because of the tops of the stones that protrude outward which are not smooth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 1:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:1:7)


**וּבִכְפִיסִים זֶה נוֹתֵן טְפָחַיִם.** שֶׁעֳבִי כֹּתֶל הַכְּפִיסִים טֶפַח יוֹתֵר מֵעֳבִי כֹּתֶל הַלְּבֵנִים, לְפִי שֶׁמֵּשִׂים אָרִיחַ מִכָּאן וְאָרִיחַ מִכָּאן, שֶׁהֵם שְׁלֹשָׁה טְפָחִים, וְטֶפַח בָּאֶמְצַע שֶׁנּוֹתֵן שָׁם טִיט לְחַבְּרָן. וְכֹתֶל הֶעָשׂוּי מִלְּבֵנִים נוֹתֵן לְבֵנָה שְׁלֵמָה לָעֳבִי הַכֹּתֶל, שֶׁהִיא שְׁלֹשָׁה טְפָחִים, וְאֵין צָרִיךְ טִיט בָּאֶמְצַע:


ובכפסים זה נותן טפחי – that the thickness of the wall of the one-half/small/girder brick is a handbreadth greater than the width of a wall of full brings because he places a small brick/bond-timber on one side and a small brick/bond-timber on the other side, which are three handbreadths and a handbreadth in the middle in which they place there plaster to attach them, but the wall made from bricks, one places a full brick along the thickness of the wall which is three handbreadths and there is no need for plaster in the middle.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 1:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:1:8)


**הַמָּקוֹם וְהָאֲבָנִים שֶׁל שְׁנֵיהֶם.** הָא קָמַשְׁמַע לָן דְּאַף עַל גַּב דְּנָפְלוּ הָאֲבָנִים לִרְשׁוּתָא דְּחַד מִנַּיְהוּ, אִי נַמִּי דְּקָדֵים חַד וּפָנִינְהוּ לִרְשׁוּתֵיהּ, מַהוּ דְּתֵימָא לֶהֱוֵי אִידָךְ הַמּוֹצִיא מֵחֲבֵרוֹ עָלָיו הָרְאָיָה, קָא מַשְׁמַע לָן:


המקום והאבנים של שניהם – this tells us that even though the stones fell into the domain of one them, or alternatively, that one of them first removed them into his domain, you might have thought that it belongs to the other – for the claimant must produce the evidence. But this comes to tell us that this is not the case.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 1:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:2:1)


**וְכֵן בְּגִנָּה.** הָכִי קָאָמַר, וְכֵן בְּגִנָּה סְתָם כִּמְקוֹם שֶׁנָּהֲגוּ לִגְדֹּר הוּא, וּמְחַיְּבִים אוֹתוֹ שֶׁלָּקַח מָקוֹם שָׁם לִגְדֹּר:


וכן בגנה – this is what he said, "וכן בגנה"/and likewise in the case of a garden, undefined, as a place where it was they had the custom to fence it in.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 1:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:2:2)


**אֲבָל בְּבִקְעָה.** סְתָם, הֲרֵי הוּא כִּמְקוֹם שֶׁנָּהֲגוּ שֶׁלֹּא לִגְדֹּר, וְאֵין מְחַיְּבִים אוֹתוֹ. אֶלָּא אִם רָצָה חֲבֵרוֹ לִגְדֹּר, כּוֹנֵס בְּתוֹךְ שֶׁלּוֹ:


אבל בבקעה – undefined, it is like a place where they had the custom to not fence it in, and we don’t require it, other than if his fellow wanted to fence it in, he moves it back to his own part


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 1:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:2:3)


**וְעוֹשֶׂה לוֹ חֲזִית.** סִימָן, לְהֶכֵּר שֶׁהַכֹּתֶל שֶׁלּוֹ. וְהַסִּימָן מְפָרֵשׁ בַּגְּמָרָא, שֶׁטָּח רֹאשׁ הַכֹּתֶל אַמָּה בְּסִיד לְצַד חֲבֵרוֹ. וְלֹא לַצַּד שֶׁלּוֹ, שֶׁמָּא יָטוּחַ גַּם חֲבֵרוֹ מִן הַצַּד שֶׁלּוֹ, וְיֹאמַר שֶׁהַכֹּתֶל שֶׁל שְׁנֵיהֶם הוּא. אֲבָל כְּשֶׁטָּח לְצַד חֲבֵרוֹ וְאֵינוֹ טָח לְתוֹךְ שֶׁלּוֹ, הָוֵי סִימָן שֶׁהַכֹּתֶל שֶׁלּוֹ. וְאִם יְקַלְּפֶנּוּ חֲבֵרוֹ, קִלּוּפָהּ מֵידָע יְדִיעַ:


ועושה לו חזית – a sign for recognition that the wall is his. And the sign is explained in the Gemara (Talmud Bava Batra 4b): he spreads the top of the wall a cubit with plaster to the side of his fellow, but not to his side lest his fellow all spread from his side. And he says that the wall belongs to both. But when he spreads to the side of his fellow, he doesn’t plaster on to his side. It is a sign that the wall is his, but if his fellow peels/scrapes It off, it is known as peeled.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 1:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:2:4)


**וְעוֹשִׂין חֲזִית מִכָּאן וּמִכָּאן.** לְהוֹדִיעַ שֶׁשְּׁנֵיהֶם עָשׂוּ אוֹתוֹ:


ועושין חזית מכאן ומכאן – to announce that both of them made it.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 1:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:3:1)


**אֵין מְחַיְּבִין אוֹתוֹ.** דְּהָא לֹא אַהֲנֵי לֵיהּ מִידֵי, שֶׁעֲדַיִן קַרְקַע שֶׁלּוֹ פְּתוּחָה. אֲבָל אִם גָּדַר אֶת הָרְבִיעִית, דְּהַשְׁתָּא וַדַּאי אַהֲנֵי לֵיהּ, מְחַיְּבִין אוֹתוֹ לְשַׁלֵּם מַחֲצִית דְּמֵי קָנִים בְּזוֹל. אֲבָל לֹא חֲצִי יְצִיאוֹתָיו, דְּאָמַר לֵיהּ לְדִידִי סַגִּי לִי בִּמְחִצָּה שֶׁל קָנִים וְאִי אֶפְשִׁי בִּיצִיאָה שֶׁל גֶּדֶר אֲבָנִים:


אין מחייבין אותו – for it doesn’t benefit him at all, for his land is still open. But, if he (i.e., the other partner) fenced in the fourth side, for now, it absolutely benefits him, we require him to pay one-half the cost of the purchase at the cheapest rate. But not half of his outlay, for he says to him: For me it is enough for me with a partition of reeds, and I don’t need an outlay of a stone fence.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 1:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:3:2)


**מְגַלְגְּלִין עָלָיו אֶת הַכֹּל.** מַחֲצִית יְצִיאוֹתָיו כְּפִי מַה שֶּׁגָּדַר. וְהַיְנוּ דְּאִיכָּא בֵּין תַּנָּא קַמָּא לְרַבִּי יוֹסֵי. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי. וְאֵין צָרִיךְ לוֹמַר אִם הַנִּקָּף הוּא שֶּׁגָּדַר רוּחַ רְבִיעִית, דְּגַלֵּי דַּעְתֵּיהּ דְּנִיחָא לֵיהּ בְּמַה שֶּׁגָּדַר חֲבֵרוֹ, שֶׁמְּגַלְגְּלִין עָלָיו אֶת הַכֹּל וּמְשַׁלֵּם לַחֲבֵרוֹ חֲצִי יְצִיאוֹתָיו:


מגלגלין עליו את הכל – one-half of his outlay according to what he fenced in. And there is the difference between the first Tanna/teacher and Rabbi Yosi. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yosi. And we don’t have to say if he himself is the one who fenced in the fourth side, for he revealed his intention that he is pleased with what his fellow fenced in that we make him bear the expenses for the fencing in of everything, and he pays his fellow half of his outlays.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 1:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:4:1)


**כֹּתֶל חָצֵר.** שֶׁל שֻׁתָּפִין:


כותל חצר – of partners.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 1:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:4:2)


**עַד אַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת.** דִּבְהָכִי סַגִּי לֵיהּ לְהֶזֵּק רְאִיָּה:


עד ארבע אמות – for with this, it is sufficient for him for proof of damages.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 1:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:4:3)


**בְּחֶזְקַת שֶׁנָּתַן.** כְּשֶׁזֶּה תּוֹבְעוֹ חֲצִי הַיְצִיאָה וְזֶה אוֹמֵר כְּבָר נָתַתִּי חֶלְקִי, נֶאֱמָן, עַד שֶׁיָּבִיא הַתּוֹבֵעַ עֵדִים שֶׁתְּבָעוֹ וְלֹא נָתַן. דְּמִשְׁפָּט זֶה גָּלוּי לַכֹּל שֶׁחוֹבָה עָלָיו לְסַיְּעוֹ, וְלֹא הָיָה זֶה בּוֹנֵהוּ מִשֶּׁלּוֹ אֶלָּא הָיָה מְבִיאוֹ לְבֵית דִּין:


בחזקת שנתן – when this one claims half the outlay, and the other one says, I have already given my portion, he is believed, until the claimant brings witnesses who claimed that he had not given. For this judgment is revealed to all that the obligation is upon him to assist him for he would not have built it on his own but rather, he would bring him to the Jewish court.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 1:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:4:4)


**סָמַךְ לוֹ כֹתֶל אַחֵר.** לְאַחַר שֶׁבָּנָה הָאֶחָד לְמַעְלָה מֵאַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת הַרְבֵּה וְלֹא רָצָה לְסַיְּעוֹ בְּהַגְבָּהָתוֹ, סָמַךְ הַשֵּׁנִי כֹּתֶל אַחֵר כְּנֶגֶד כֹּתֶל זֶה כְּדֵי לְסַכֵּךְ וְלָתֵת תִּקְרָה מִכֹּתֶל לְכֹתֶל, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא נָתַן עָלָיו עֲדַיִן הַתִּקְרָה: **מְגַלְגְּלִין עָלָיו אֶת הַכֹּל.** דְּגַלֵּי דַּעְתֵּיהּ דְּנִיחָא לֵיהּ בַּהַגְבָּהָה דְּהַאִיךְ:


סמך לו כותל אחר – after he built the first [wall] a great deal higher than four cubits (i.e. a cubit = 22.08 inches or 56.1 cm.), and he did not want to assist him in raising it higher, he built another wall opposite this wall in order to put cover it with boughs and to place a roof from wall to wall, even though he did not as yet place upon it a roof, we make him bear the expenses for everything, for he has revealed his intention that it is pleasing to him for the raising of this one.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 1:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:4:5)


**בְּחֶזְקַת שֶׁלֹּא נָתַן.** אִם בָּא הָרִאשׁוֹן וּתְבָעוֹ לַדִּין לְאַחַר שֶׁסָּמַךְ אֶת כָּתְלוֹ לְכֹתֶל רִאשׁוֹן, וְאוֹמֵר לוֹ תֵּן חֶלְקְךָ בְּמַה שֶּׁהִגְבַּהְתִּי אוֹתוֹ, וְזֶה אוֹמֵר נָתַתִּי חֶלְקִי, אֵינוֹ נֶאֱמָן אֶלָּא בְּעֵדִים. שֶׁאֵין מִשְׁפָּט זֶה גָּלוּי, וְעַד שֶׁחִיְּבוּהוּ בֵּית דִּין אֵינוֹ עָשׂוּי לִתֵּן:


בחזקת שלא נתן – if the first person came and made a claim against him to judgment after he built his other wall placing it near the first wall. And he said to him (i.e., his partner): “Give your part for what I raised it.” But the other responded: “I gave my part.” But he is not believed other than with witnesses, for this judgment was not visible to all, and until the Jewish court made him liable, he would not have given.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 1:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:5:1)


**כּוֹפִין אוֹתוֹ.** אֶת בֶּן הֶחָצֵר שֶׁאֵינוֹ רוֹצֶה לְסַיֵּעַ לִבְנֵי הֶחָצֵר:


כופין אותו – [we force] the member resident of the courtyard who doesn’t want to assist the members of the courtyard.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 1:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:5:2)


**לִבְנוֹת.** לֶחָצֵר בֵּית שַׁעַר לִהְיוֹת שׁוֹמֵר הַפֶּתַח יוֹשֵׁב שָׁם בַּצֵּל וּמַרְחִיק אֶת בְּנֵי רְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים מִלְּהָצִיץ בֶּחָצֵר:


לבנות – [to build] for the courtyard a gate house to be a guard for the opening who sits there in the shade and to distance the members of the public domain from looking into the courtyard.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 1:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:5:3)


**וְדֶלֶת.** לְשַׁעַר הֶחָצֵר:


ודלת – for the gate of the courtyard.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 1:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:5:4)


**לֹא כָל הַחֲצֵרוֹת רְאוּיוֹת לְבֵית שָׁעַר.** חָצֵר שֶׁאֵינָהּ סְמוּכָה לִרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים אֵינָהּ רְאוּיָה לְבֵית שַׁעַר. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבָּן שִׁמְעוֹן בֶּן גַּמְלִיאֵל. דַּאֲפִלּוּ שֶׁאֵינָהּ סְמוּכָה לִרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים זִמְנִין דְּדַחְקֵי רַבִּים וְעָיְלוּ וְאָתוּ:


לא כל החצרות ראויות לבית שער – a courtyard which is not close by to the public domain is not worthy of havng a gate house. But the Halakha is not according to Rabban Shimon ben Gamaliel for even if it is not close to the public domain, there are times when the masses pressure and enter [and come in].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 1:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:5:5)


**לֹא כָל הָעֲיָרוֹת רְאוּיוֹת לְחוֹמָה.** עִיר שֶׁאֵינָהּ סְמוּכָה לִגְבוּל הָאוֹיְבִים אֵינָהּ צְרִיכָה לְחוֹמָה. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבָּן שִׁמְעוֹן בֶּן גַּמְלִיאֵל. דַּאֲפִלּוּ אֵינָהּ סְמוּכָה לִגְבוּל הָאוֹיְבִים צְרִיכָה לְחוֹמָה, דְּזִמְנִין דְּמִקְרוּ וְאָתוּ גְּיָסוֹת:


לא כל העיירות ראויות לחומה – a city which is not close by to the border of enemies does not require a wall. But the Halakha is not in accordance with Rabban Shimon ben Gamaliel, for even if it is not close by the border of enemies, it needs a wall, for sometimes, it happens that invaders come.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 1:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:5:6)


**וִיהֵא כְאַנְשֵׁי הָעִיר.** לָשֵׂאת עִמָּהֶם בָּעֹל:


ויהא כאנשי העיר – to bear with them the burden/yoke.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 1:5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:5:7)


**שְׁנֵים עָשָׂר חֹדֶשׁ.** וְהָאִידְנָא דְּנַיְדֵי, נָהוּג עָלְמָא שְׁלֹשִׁים יוֹם:


שנים עשר חדש – but now that they are unsteady, the general practice is thirty days.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 1:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:6:1)


**אֵין חוֹלְקִין אֶת הֶחָצֵר.** אֵין אֶחָד מִן הַשֻּׁתָּפִין יָכוֹל לָכוֹף אֶת חֲבֵרוֹ לַחְלֹק הֶחָצֵר עַד שֶׁיְּהֵא בּוֹ אַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת לְכָל אֶחָד מִן הַשֻּׁתָּפִין חוּץ מִן הַפְּתָחִים, שֶׁכָּל בַּיִת שֶׁבֶּחָצֵר צָרִיךְ שֶׁיִּהְיֶה לִפְנֵי פִּתְחוֹ אַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת לְפָרֵק מַשָּׂאוֹ מֵעַל חֲמוֹרוֹ, וְחוּץ מֵאוֹתָן אַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת צָרִיךְ שֶׁיִּהְיֶה בֶּחָצֵר אַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת אֲחֵרוֹת לְכָל אֶחָד מִן הַשֻּׁתָּפִין לִשְׁאָר תַּשְׁמִישִׁים, וְאָז יִהְיֶה בֶּחָצֵר דִּין חֲלֻקָּה:


אין חולקין את החצר – one of the partners cannot force his fellow to divide the courtyard until there are four cubits to each one of the partners, other than the openings, for every house in the courtyard requires [that there will be] in front of his door four cubits in order to unload the burden that is upon his donkey. And outside of those four cubits, one needs in the courtyard another four cubits to each one of the partners for his other uses. And then there will be in the courtyard the law of division.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 1:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:6:2)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר תִּשְׁעָה חֲצָאֵי קַבִּין.** מָר כִּי אַתְרֵיהּ וּמָר כִּי אַתְרֵיהּ. וְלֹא פְּלִיגֵי. בְּאַתְרֵיהּ דְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה הָיְתָה עוֹשָׂה תְּבוּאָה שָׂדֶה בַּת תִּשְׁעָה חֲצָאֵי קַבִּין כְּשָׂדֶה בַּת תִּשְׁעָה קַבִּין בְּאַתְרַיְהוּ דְּרַבָּנָן:


רבי יהודה אומר ט' חצאי קבין – this person like his place, and that person like his place. But they don’t dispute that in the places of Rabbi Yehuda they would make grain – a field that has nine half-kabs, is like the field of nine kabs in the place of the Rabbis.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 1:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:6:3)


**בֵּית רֹבַע.** מָקוֹם לִזְרֹעַ בּוֹ רֹבַע הַקַּב. וּפְסַק הַהֲלָכָה, אֵין דִּין חֲלֻקָּה בְּקַרְקָעוֹת הָעוֹמְדוֹת לַחֲרִישָׁה וְלִזְרִיעָה אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן יִשָּׁאֵר לְכָל אֶחָד מִן הַשֻּׁתָּפִים קַרְקַע כְּדֵי עֲבוֹדַת יוֹם אֶחָד בַּחֲרִישָׁה [אוֹ בִּזְרִיעָה] וְכַיּוֹצֵא בָהֶן:


בית רובע – a place to seed in it a square kab. And the Jewish legal decision is that the law of division on lands that stand for plough and sewing, other than if there remains for each one of the partners enough land in order that there is the work of one day of ploughing or sewing, and similar things.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 1:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:6:4)


**מוֹרָן וּטְרַקְלִין.** מִינֵי פְּלָטִין הֵן:


מורן וטרקלין – kinds of palaces.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 1:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:6:5)


**כְּדֵי לָזֶה וּכְדֵי לָזֶה.** כְּדִמְפָרֵשׁ וְאָזִיל שֶׁלְּאַחַר שֶׁיַּחֲלֹקוּ וְיִטֹּל כָּל אֶחָד חֶלְקוֹ יִהְיֶה בְּחֵלֶק כָּל אֶחָד מֵהֶם מָקוֹם רָאוּי שֶׁיִּקָּרֵא טְרַקְלִין אוֹ מוֹרָן, שֶׁיִּשָּׁאֵר בִּקְצָתוֹ הַשֵּׁם שֶׁהָיָה בְּכֻלּוֹ:


כדי לזה וכדי לזה – as it is explained further on, and after they would divide it up and each one takes his part, there would be in the part of each of them a place worthy of being called a reception/dining room and a compartment of the house/peristyle, that would remain on part of it the name that was for all of it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 1:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:6:6)


**בִּזְמַן שֶׁאֵין שְׁנֵיהֶם רוֹצִין.** אֵין יָכוֹל הָאֶחָד לָכוֹף אֶת חֲבֵרוֹ לַחְלֹק עַל כָּרְחוֹ. אֲבָל יָכוֹל לְכוֹפוֹ לְעִנְיַן גּוֹד אוֹ אֱגוֹד, כְּלוֹמַר, קְנֵה חֶלְקִי אוֹ מְכֹר לִי חֶלְקְךָ בְּמַה שֶּׁהוּא שָׁוֶה:


בזמן שין שניהם רוצים – one cannot force his fellow to divide it by force, but he can force hi regarding the manner of "גוד או אגוד"/either fix you a price for my share, or I shall do so (and buy your share (see Talmud Bava Batra 13a), meaning to say, purchase my portion or sell me your portion for what it is worth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 1:6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:6:7)


**וְכִתְבֵי הַקֹּדֶשׁ.** תּוֹרָה נְבִיאִים וּכְתוּבִים:


ובכתבי הקודש – Torah, Prophets and Writings.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 1:6:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:6:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/1:6:8)


**לֹא יַחֲלֹקוּ.** וְדַוְקָא כְּשֶׁהֵם בְּכֶרֶךְ אֶחָד. אֲבָל בִּכְרָכִים הַרְבֵּה כָּל סֵפֶר בִּפְנֵי עַצְמוֹ, אִם רָצוּ חוֹלְקִים:


לא יחלקו – and specifically when they are in one volume, but in many volumes, each book separately, if they want, they can divide it.



## Chapter 2


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:1:1)


**לֹא יַחְפֹּר.** בּוֹר. עָגֹל:


לא יחפור. בור – round.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:1:2)


**שִׁיחַ.** אָרֹךְ וְקָצָר:


שיח – long or short


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:1:3)


**מְעָרָה.** מְקֹרָה בְּקֵרוּי:


מערה – it is covered with an arched ceiling.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:1:4)


**נִבְרֶכֶת הַכּוֹבְסִין.** חֲפִירָה מְרֻבַּעַת וּמֵי גְּשָׁמִים מִתְקַבְּצִים בָּהּ. עֲשׂוּיָה לְכַבֵּס בְּגָדִים:


נברכת הכובסין – a square pit. And rain waters gather in it as it is made to launder clothing.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:1:5)


**אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן הִרְחִיק מִכָּתְלוֹ.** מִכֹּתֶל בּוֹרוֹ קָאָמַר. וְכֹתֶל הַבּוֹר אֵין עָבְיוֹ פָּחוֹת מִשְּׁלֹשָׁה טְפָחִים. נִמְצָא מֵחֲלַל בּוֹרוֹ לַחֲלַל בּוֹר שֶׁל חֲבֵרוֹ שִׁשָּׁה טְפָחִים. וְאָסוּר לָאָדָם לִסְמֹךְ אֶחָד מִן הַנְּזָקִים לְסוֹף הַמֵּצַר שֶׁלּוֹ בְּשָׁוֶה אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן הִרְחִיק הַשִּׁעוּר הָרָאוּי, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵין לַחֲבֵרוֹ שָׁם כֹּתֶל, שֶׁמָּא יִמָּלֵךְ חֲבֵרוֹ וְיִבְנֶה בְּצַד הַמֵּצַר שֶׁלּוֹ וְנִמְצָא זֶה מַזִּיקוֹ:


אלא אם כן הרחיק מכותלו – it is saying from the wall of his cistern. And the thickness of the wall of his cistern is not less than three handbreadths, it is found that from the cavity/space of his cistern to the cavity/space of his fellow’s cistern is six handbreadths. But it is prohibited for a person to rely upon one of the damages to the end of his boundary equally, other than if he distanced it the appropriate measurement, even though his fellow lacks a wall there, lest his fellow decide that he will build on the side of his boundary, and it is found that this causes damage.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:1:6)


**וְסָד בְּסִיד.** אֶת כָּתְלֵי חֲפִירָתוֹ:


וסד בסיד – the walls of his pit.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:1:7)


**הַגֶּפֶת.** פְּסֹלֶת הַזֵּיתִים לְאַחַר שֶׁנֶּעֶצְרוּ בְּבֵית הַבַּד:


הגפת – the refuse of the olives after they were gathered together in the olive press.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:1:8)


**הַסְּלָעִים.** אֲבָנִים שֶׁהָאוּר יוֹצֵא מֵהֶן. שֶׁכָּל אֵלּוּ קָשִׁים לַחוֹמָה וּמְרַפִּין אֶת הַכֹּתֶל. וְלֹא בְּכֹתֶל דְּבוֹר קָאֵי הַשְׁתָּא:


הסלעים – stones where the light comes out from them, for all of these are hard for the fortification and weaken the wall, and we are not referring to the wall of the cistern now.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:1:9)


**זְרָעִים.** קָשִׁים לַכֹּתֶל, שֶׁמַּחְלִידִים אֶת הַקַּרְקַע וּמַעֲלִין עָפָר תִּחוֹחַ:


זרעים – they are hard for the wall for they undermine the grounds (through their roots – see Talmud Bava Batra 19b) and bring up crushed, loose earth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:1:10)


**וְאֶת הַמַּחֲרֵשָׁה.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵין זְרָעִים, כְּגוֹן שֶׁחוֹרֵשׁ לָאִילָנוֹת, קָשֶׁה לַכֹּתֶל:


ואת המחרישה – even though there are no seeds, as, for example, when they plow for trees, it is hard for the wall.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:1:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:1:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:1:11)


**מֵי רַגְלַיִם.** מְמַסְמְסִים אֶת הַלְּבֵנִים שֶׁהֵם שֶׁל טִיט יָבֵשׁ. לְכָךְ הַמַּטִּיל מַיִם סָמוּךְ לְכֹתֶל עָשׂוּי מִלְּבֵנִים צָרִיךְ לְהַרְחִיק שְׁלֹשָׁה טְפָחִים. וּבְכֹתֶל שֶׁל אֲבָנִים מַרְחִיק טֶפַח. וְשֶׁל אֶבֶן קָשָׁה כְּגוֹן צוּנְמָא, אֵינוֹ צָרִיךְ לְהַרְחִיק כְּלָל:


מי רגלים – they make loathsome the bricks which are of dry plaster. Therefore, someone who urinates close by the wall that is made of bricks needs to distance himself three handbreadths. But with a wall of stones, he distances himself a handbreadth, and of a hard stone such as granite, he does not have to distance himself at all.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:1:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:1:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:1:12)


**הָרֵחַיִם.** קָשִׁים לַכֹּתֶל, שֶׁמְּנִידִים אֶת הַקַּרְקַע בְּגִלְגּוּל חֲבִיטָתָן:


הריחים – they are hard on the wall because they shake the ground with the force of its rolling.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:1:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:1:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:1:13)


**מִן הַשֶּׁכֶב.** הִיא הָרֵחַיִם הַתַּחְתּוֹנָה:


מן השכב – from the lower millstone.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:1:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:1:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:1:14)


**הָרָכֶב.** הִיא הָעֶלְיוֹנָה הָרוֹכֶבֶת עַל זוֹ. וְהִיא קְצָרָה טֶפַח מִן הַתַּחְתּוֹנָה:


הרכב – this is the upper one that rides on this. But it is shorter by a handbreadth of the lower one.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:1:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:1:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:1:15)


**שְׁלֹשָׁה מִן הַכִּלְיָא.** בָּסִיס שֶׁבּוֹנִים מִטִּיט וַאֲבָנִים שֶׁמּוֹשִׁיבִים הַתַּנּוּר עָלָיו, וְהוּא רָחָב מִלְּמַטָּה וְצַר מִלְּמַעְלָה, וְשָׂפָה הָעֶלְיוֹנָה שֶׁל כִּלְיָא שֶׁעָלֶיהָ הַתַּנּוּר יוֹשֵׁב הִיא כְּמִדַּת הַתַּנּוּר. וְצָרִיךְ שֶׁיַּרְחִיק בָּסִיס זֶה מִן הַכֹּתֶל שְׁלֹשָׁה טְפָחִים מִתַּחְתִּיתוֹ, שֶׁהֵן אַרְבָּעָה מִשְּׂפָתוֹ הָעֶלְיוֹנָה, לְפִי שֶׁהֶבֶל הַתַּנּוּר מַזִּיק לַכֹּתֶל:


שלשה מן הכליא – the base upon which they build from plaster/clay and stones that they place the oven upon it, and It is wide from the bottom and narrow from the top, and upper rim of the belly of the stove is where the stove sits. It is like the measurement of the oven and one must distance the base from the wall three handbreadths from its underbelly, which are four [handbreadths] from the its upper rim, because the vapor of the oven damages the wall.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:2:1)


**גֹּבַהּ אַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת.** חָלָל מִפִּי הַתַּנּוּר עַד הַתִּקְרָה, כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יֹאחַז הָאוּר בַּתִּקְרָה:


גובה דא אמות – the space from the lip of the oven until the ceiling, in order that the fire not ignite the ceiling.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:2:2)


**עַד שֶׁיְּהֵא תַחְתָּיו מַעֲזִיבָה.** טִיחַ שֶׁל טִיט שְׁלֹשָׁה טְפָחִים, שֶׁלֹּא תִּבְעַר תִּקְרָה תַּחְתּוֹנָה שֶׁל עֲלִיָּה. וְעַל גַּבָּיו גֹּבַהּ אַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת:


עד שיהא תחתיו מעזיבה – [plaster of] clay three handbreadths, so that the lower ceiling of the attic not burn, and above it a space of four cubits.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:2:3)


**וּבַכִּירָה.** שֶׁשּׁוֹפְתִין קְדֵרָה עַל חֲלָלָהּ וְאֵין עוֹשִׂין בְּתוֹכָהּ הֶסֵּק גָּדוֹל כְּמוֹ שֶׁעוֹשִׂין לַתַּנּוּר:


ובכירה – they place the pot on its cavity/space and they don’t make it in a large fire as they do for an oven.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:2:4)


**וְאִם הִזִּיק.** אַחַר שֶׁהָיוּ שָׁם כָּל הַשִּׁעוּרִים הַלָּלוּ:


ואם הזיק – after there were there all of these measurements,


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:2:5)


**מְשַׁלֵּם מַה שֶּׁהִזִּיק.** וְאַף עַל פִּי כֵן מְעַכְּבִין עָלָיו בְּכָל הַשִּׁעוּרִים הַלָּלוּ, שֶׁמָּא יַדְלִיק בָּתֵּיהֶם וְאֵין לוֹ מַה לְשַׁלֵּם. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


he pays for the damage that he caused, and nevertheless, they prevent him with all of these measurements lest he kindle their homes, and he doesn’t have what with to pay. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Shimon.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:3:1)


**בְּיַיִן הִתִּירוּ.** שֶׁהֶעָשָׁן הַיּוֹצֵא מִן הַחֲנוּת שֶׁל נַחְתּוֹמִין וְשֶׁל צַבָּעִין אֵינוֹ קָשֶׁה לַיַּיִן, שֶׁהַחֹם מַשְׁבִּיחַ לַיַּיִן שֶׁבְּאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל. וּבְמָקוֹם שֶׁיָּדוּעַ שֶׁהַחֹם מַזִּיק לַיַּיִן, אַף תַּחַת אוֹצָר שֶׁל יַיִן לֹא יִפְתַּח חֲנוּת שֶׁל צַבָּעִים וְשֶׁל נַחְתּוֹמִין:


ביין התירו – The smoke that goes up from the baker’s oven and from the dyer’s shop is not difficult for the wine, for the heat actually improves the wine that is in the Land of Israel. And in a place where it is known that the heat damages the wine, even under the wine storehouse, one should not not open a dyer’s shop or of a baker.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:3:2)


**חֲנוּת שֶׁבֶּחָצֵר.** אֶחָד מִבְּנֵי חָצֵר שֶׁבָּא לִפְתֹּחַ חֲנוּת בֶּחָצֵר, בְּנֵי הֶחָצֵר מְעַכְּבִין עָלָיו:


חנות שבחצר – one of the members of the courtyard that comes to open a store in the courtyard and the members of the courtyard prevent him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:3:3)


**וְלֹא מִקּוֹל הַתִּינוֹקוֹת.** שֶׁל בֵּית רַבָּן. וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּקוֹל הַבָּא מֵחֲמַת אֲחֵרִים הוּא, לֹא מָצֵי מָחִי, מִשּׁוּם יַגְדִּיל תּוֹרָה וְיַאְדִּיר. וְאִם אֵינוֹ מְלַמֵּד הַתִּינוֹקוֹת תּוֹרָה, אֶלָּא אֻמָּנוּת אוֹ חֶשְׁבּוֹן אוֹ תִּשְׁבֹּרֶת, מָצֵי מָחִי וְלוֹמַר לוֹ אֵינִי יָכוֹל לִישַׁן מִפְּנֵי הַתִּינוֹקוֹת שֶׁנִּכְנָסִים וְיוֹצְאִים:


לא מקול התינוקות – school children. And even though that the sound comes on account of others, they cannot protest, because of making the Torah great and glorious. But, if he is not teaching children Torah, but rather trades or accounting or תשבורת he can protest and say to him that he is not able to sleep because of the young children coming in and leaving.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:4:1)


**מִי שֶׁהָיָה כָתְלוֹ סָמוּךְ לְכֹתֶל חֲבֵרוֹ.** כְּמִין גַּא"ם. וּבָא לַעֲשׂוֹת כֹּתֶל שֵׁנִי כְּנֶגֶד כֹּתֶל חֲבֵרוֹ עַד שֶׁיַּעֲשֶׂה הַשְּׁלֹשָׁה כְּתָלִים כְּמִין בֵּי"ת, הֲרֵי חֲבֵרוֹ מְעַכֵּב עָלָיו עַד שֶׁיַּרְחִיק מִכְּנֶגְדּוֹ אַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת, כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּהְיֶה הַמָּקוֹם בֵּין שְׁנֵי הַכְּתָלִים רָחָב וְיוּכְלוּ לָדוּשׁ בּוֹ רַבִּים, שֶׁדְּרִיסַת הָרֶגֶל בַּקַּרְקַע הַסָּמוּךְ לַכֹּתֶל מְחַזֶּקֶת יְסוֹדוֹת הַכְּתָלִים וּמַעֲמִידָה אוֹתָם. וְדַוְקָא בְּכֹתֶל גִּנָּה אוֹ בְּכֹתֶל חָצֵר שֶׁבְּעִיר חֲדָשָׁה דְּלֹא דָּשׁוּ בָּהּ רַבִּים וְלֹא נִתְחַזֵּק הַקַּרְקַע כָּל צָרְכּוֹ עַל יְדֵי דְּרִיסַת הָרֶגֶל. אֲבָל בְּכֹתֶל חָצֵר שֶׁבְּעִיר יְשָׁנָה סוֹמֵךְ וְאֵין צָרִיךְ לְהַרְחִיק. וְכֵן אִם לֹא הָיָה בְּכֹתֶל חֲבֵרוֹ אַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת אוֹ יוֹתֵר אֵינוֹ צָרִיךְ לְהַרְחִיק, שֶׁכֹּתֶל שֶׁהוּא פָּחוֹת מֵאַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת אֵינוֹ צָרִיךְ חִזּוּק:


מי שהיה כותלו סמוך לכותל חבירו – in the form of a גא"ם/Gamma – an angle, and he comes to make a second wall opposite the wall of his fellow until he makes the three walls like the form of the [Hebrew letter] "בי"ת"/Bet, his fellow will prevent him, until he distances it from opposite him four cubits, in order that the place between the two walls will be wide and many will be able to tread there. For the walking of feet on the ground nearest the wall strengthens the foundations of the wall and preserves them. And especially the wall of a garden or he wall of a courtyard that is in a new city where many have not [yet] tread, and the ground was not strengthened as much as needed through treading of the feet [of people]. But, in the wall of a courtyard that is in an old city is supported and there is no need to distance it. And similarly, if the wall of his fellow was not four cubits or more, he doesn’t have to distance it, for a wall that is less than four cubits does not need strengthening.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:4:2)


**וּבַחַלּוֹנוֹת מִלְמַעְלָן וּמִלְּמַטָּן וּמִכְּנֶגְדָּן אַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת.** הָיְתָה לוֹ חַלּוֹן לְמַעְלָה בְּכָתְלוֹ וּבָנָה חֲבֵרוֹ כֹּתֶל כְּנֶגֶד הַחַלּוֹן מִלְּמַטָּה, אִם נִשְׁאַר מֵרֹאשׁ הַכֹּתֶל שֶׁבָּנָה עַד הַחַלּוֹן פָּחוֹת מֵאַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת בַּגֹּבַהּ, הֲרֵי זֶה כּוֹפֵהוּ לְמַעֵט הַכֹּתֶל, כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יַעֲמֹד עַל רֹאשׁ הַכֹּתֶל וְיַשְׁקִיף בְּעַד הַחַלּוֹן:


ובחלונות מלמעלן מלמטן ומכנגדן ד' אמות – he had a wall above his wall and his fellow built a wall opposite the window from below. If there remained from the top of the wall that he built up until the window less than four cubits in height, he forces him to lower the wall in order tha the not stand on the top of the wall and look out through the window.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:4:3)


**מִלְּמַטָּן.** הָיְתָה הַחַלּוֹן לְמַטָּה בַּכֹּתֶל, כּוֹפֶה אֶת חֲבֵרוֹ לְהַגְבִּיהַּ הַכֹּתֶל שֶׁבָּנָה כְּנֶגְדּוֹ בְּגֹבַהּ אַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת מִן הַחַלּוֹן כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יַבִּיט בּוֹ:


מלמטן – the window was lower than the wall, he forces his fellow to raise the wall that he built opposite him at the height of four cubits from the window in order that he should not see him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:4:4)


**וּמִכְּנֶגֶד.** דְּצָרִיךְ לְהַרְחִיק הַכֹּתֶל מִן הַחַלּוֹן אַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יַאֲפִיל אוֹרוֹ:


ומכנגד – he must distance the wall from the window four cubits in order that he will not obscure his light.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:5:1)


**מַרְחִיקִין אֶת הַסֻּלָּם.** מִי שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ שׁוֹבָךְ שֶׁל יוֹנִים בַּחֲצֵרוֹ סָמוּךְ לְכֹתֶל שֶׁבֵּין שְׁתֵּי חֲצֵרוֹת, וּבָא חֲבֵרוֹ לְהַעֲמִיד סֻלָּם אֵצֶל הַכֹּתֶל, צָרִיךְ לְהַרְחִיק הַסֻּלָּם מִן הַשּׁוֹבָךְ אַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת:


מרחיקין את הסולם – whomever has a dovecote of doves in his courtyard near to the wall that is between the two courtyards and his fellow comes to put up a ladder near the wall, he must distance the latter four cubits from the dovecote, in order that a marten will not jump, and it is small creature, into the dovecote and kill the doves.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:5:2)


**כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא תִּקְפֹּץ הַנְּמִיָּה.** הִיא חַיָּה קְטַנָּה, מִן הַסֻּלָּם לַשּׁוֹבָךְ, וְתַהֲרֹג אֶת הַיּוֹנִים:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:5:3)


**מַזְחִילָה.** הוּא צִנּוֹר גָּדוֹל הַמֻּנָּח עַל אֹרֶךְ הַכֹּתֶל וּמֵימֵי הַגַּג זָבִים לְתוֹכוֹ. וְאִם כָּתְלוֹ מְהַלֵּךְ עַל פְּנֵי חֲצַר חֲבֵרוֹ וּמַזְחִילָה עָלָיו, צָרִיךְ לְהַרְחִיק מִמֶּנָּה אִם בָּא לִבְנוֹת כֹּתֶל בְּצִדָּהּ:


מזחילה – it is a large spout that is placed on the length of the wall, and the waters of the roof flow into it, and if his wall would go along the face of the courtyard of his fellow and with the spout on top of it, he would have to distance it from it if he comes to build a wall on its side four cubits in order that a ladder can stand upright to go up and to repair his spout, to clean it from dust and from rocks that fall in it and prevent the continuous flow of water.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:5:4)


**אַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת כְּדֵי שֶׁיְּהֵא זוֹקֵף שָׁם סֻלָּם.** לַעֲלוֹת וּלְתַקֵּן מַזְחִילָתוֹ לְנַקּוֹתָהּ מֵעָפָר וּמִצְּרוֹרוֹת הַנּוֹפְלִים בָּהּ וּמְעַכְּבִים אֶת קִלּוּחַ הַמַּיִם:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:5:5)


**מַרְחִיקִים אֶת הַשּׁוֹבָךְ מִן הָעִיר.** שֶׁהַיּוֹנִים מַפְסִידִים זֵרְעוֹנֵי הַגַּגּוֹת:


מרחיקים את השובך מן העיר – for the doves cause the loss of the seeds of the gardens.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:5:6)


**אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן יֶשׁ לוֹ חֲמִשִּׁים אַמָּה.** שֶׁלֹּא יַפְסִידוּ הַיּוֹנִים בִּשְׂדֵה חֲבֵרוֹ:


אא"כ יש לו חמשים אמה – so that the doves do not cause the loss in the field of his fellow.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:5:7)


**בֵּית אַרְבַּעַת כּוֹרִין.** בֵּית כּוֹר לְכָל רוּחַ, וְהַכּוֹר שְׁלֹשִׁים סְאִין. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


בית ארבעה כורין – an area requiring a Kor of seed in every direction, and the Kor is thirty Se’ah, but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:5:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:5:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:5:8)


**מְלֹא שֶׁגֶר הַיּוֹנִים.** מְרוּצַת פְּרִיחָתָן בְּפַעַם אַחַת:


מלא שגר היונים – race of their flying at one time [for food].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:5:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:5:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:5:9)


**וְאִם לְקָחוֹ.** כְּמוֹת שֶׁהוּא עִם הַקַּרְקַע, אֲפִלּוּ אֵין לוֹ כָּל סְבִיבָיו אֶלָּא בֵּית רֹבַע הַקַּב:


ואם לקחו – [if he bought it] (a field with a dovecote already in it) in its natural condition with the ground, even if he lacks all around him other than an area of a quarter-Kab (104 1/6 square cubits).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:5:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:5:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:5:10)


**הֲרֵי הוּא בְחֶזְקָתוֹ.** שֶׁהֲרֵי הֻחְזַק בּוֹ הָרִאשׁוֹן כָּךְ:


הרי הוא בחזקתו – since he was the first to have legal presumption of possession.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:6:1)


**נִפּוּל.** גּוֹזָל:


ניפול – a pigeon.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:6:2)


**הַנִּמְצָא בְתוֹךְ חֲמִשִּׁים אַמָּה.** שֶׁל שׁוֹבָךְ:


הנמצא בתוך חמשים אמה – of the dovecote.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:6:3)


**בֵּין שְׁנֵי שׁוֹבָכוֹת.** בְּתוֹךְ חֲמִשִּׁים לִשְׁנֵיהֶם:


בין שני שובכות – within fifty of both of them.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:7:1)


**מַרְחִיקִין אֶת הָאִילָן מִן הָעִיר.** לְפִי שֶׁנּוֹי הוּא לָעִיר כְּשֶׁיֵּשׁ מֶרְחָב פָּנוּי לְפָנֶיהָ:


מרחיקין את האילן מן העיר – because it is beauty to the city (see Talmud Bava Batra 24b) when there is a wide expanse before it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:7:2)


**חָרוּב וְשִׁקְמָה.** עַנְפֵיהֶן מְרֻבִּים:


חרוב ושקמה – their branches are numeous.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:7:3)


**אִילַן סְרָק.** גְּנַאי הוּא לָעִיר:


אילן סרק – (see Mishnah Kilayim, Chapter 6, Mishnah 5 for a detailed definition) – it is a disgrace to the city.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:7:4)


**וְנוֹתֵן דָּמִים.** מִי שֶׁהָעִיר שֶׁלּוֹ:


ונותן דמים – to the person who owns the city.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:7:5)


**סָפֵק וְכוּ'‏ קוֹצֵץ וְאֵינוֹ נוֹתֵן דָּמִים.** דְּכֵיוָן דְּדִינָא הָוֵי דְּבֵין זֶה קָדַם וּבֵין זֶה קָדַם קוֹצֵץ, לְאַחַר שֶׁקָּצַץ אָמְרִינַן לֵיהּ לְבַעַל הָאִילָן אַיְתֵי רְאָיָה שֶׁהָאִילָן קָדַם וּשְׁקֹל:


ספק – cuts it down and he doesn’t make payment, because there was a judgment as to whether this one came first (i.e., the city) or that one came first (i.e., the tree), he cuts it down. After he cut it down, we say to the owner of the tree: “Bring proof that the tree came first and take [the money].”



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:8:1)


**גֹּרֶן קָבוּעַ.** גֹּרֶן שֶׁיֵּשׁ בּוֹ כְּרִי גָּדוֹל שֶׁזּוֹרִים אוֹתוֹ בְּרַחַת, קָרוּי גֹּרֶן קָבוּעַ. וְשֶׁאֵין הַכְּרִי גָּדוֹל וְאֵין בּוֹ לִזְרוֹת הַמֹּץ בְּרַחַת אֶלָּא הָרוּחַ מְנַשֶּׁבֶת בַּכְּרִי וְהַמֹּץ נִדָּף מֵאֵלָיו, קָרוּי גֹּרֶן שֶׁאֵינוֹ קָבוּעַ:


גורן קבע – a threshing floor that has a large pile that they winnow it with a winnowing shovel is called a permanent threshing floor. But if the pile is not large, and he doesn’t have to winnow the chaff with a winnowing shovel, but rather, the wind blows it into a pile, and the chaff is driven off on its own, is called a threshing floor that is not permanent.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:8:2)


**חֲמִשִּׁים אַמָּה.** מִפְּנֵי הַמֹּץ שֶׁמַּזִּיק אֶת בְּנֵי הָעִיר. כְּשֶׁהוּא זוֹרֶה:


חמשים אמה – because the chaff that damages the members of the city when it is winnowed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:8:3)


**וּמִנִּירוֹ.** שֶׁל חֲבֵרוֹ. נִיר, הִיא חֲרִישָׁה שֶׁחוֹרְשִׁים בִּימוֹת הַקַּיִץ כְּדֵי שֶׁיָּמוּתוּ שָׁרְשֵׁי הַקּוֹצִים וְהָעֲשָׂבִים:


ומנירו – [newly ploughed field] of his fellow. A ניר/newly broken or ploughed field. This is a ploughing done during summertime in order that the roots of the thorns and grasses will die.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:8:4)


**כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יַזִּיק.** מַה טַּעַם קָאָמַר, מַה טַּעַם מַרְחִיק מִנְּטִיעוֹתָיו שֶׁל חֲבֵרוֹ וּמִנִּירוֹ חֲמִשִּׁים אַמָּה, כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יַזִּיק הַמֹּץ לִנְטִיעוֹתָיו וּלְנִירוֹ, שֶׁהוּא נַעֲשֶׂה זֶבֶל וּמְקַלְקֵל הַנִּיר וּמְיַבֵּשׁ הַנְּטִיעוֹת:


כדי שלא יזיק – What is the reason that this is said? What is the reason that we distance this from the crops of his fellow and from his newly broken/ploughed field fifty cubits? In order that the chaff not damage his plants and his newly broken/ploughed field, which is made of manure and ruins the newly broken field and dries out the plants/trees.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:9:1)


**אֶת הַנְּבֵלוֹת וְאֶת הַקְּבָרוֹת.** מִשּׁוּם רֵיחַ רַע:


את הנבילות ואת הקברות – because of the bad smell.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:9:2)


**הַבֻּרְסְקִי.** מְקוֹם עִבּוּד הָעוֹרוֹת:


הבורסקי – a place where they work the hides.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:9:3)


**אֶלָּא לְמִזְרַח הָעִיר.** שֶׁאֵין רוּחַ מִזְרָחִית קָשָׁה אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן בָּאָה לְפֻרְעָנוּת. אֲבָל כְּשֶׁהִיא בָּאָה כְּדַרְכָּהּ הִיא חַמָּה וּמְנַשֶּׁבֶת בְּנַחַת, לְפִיכָךְ אֵינָהּ מְבִיאָה הָרֵיחַ לָעִיר:


אלא למזרח העיר – for the east wind is not hard, other than if it comes for retribution. But when it comes in is normal manner, it is warm and blows gently, therefore, it does not bring the smell to the city.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:9:4)


**חוּץ מִמַּעֲרָבָהּ.** לְכָל רוּחַ הוּא עוֹשֶׂה וּמַרְחִיק חֲמִשִּׁים אַמָּה, חוּץ מֵרוּחַ מַעֲרָבִית שֶׁאֵינוֹ עוֹשֶׂה כָּל עִקָּר, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁמִּתְפַּלְּלִים לְאוֹתָהּ הָרוּחַ, שֶׁשְּׁכִינָה בַּמַּעֲרָב. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא:


חוץ מן מערבה – he can make it in any direction and distance it fifty cubits from the western wind, which doesn’t do anything because they pray to that direction where God’s presence is in the west. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Akiva.



### Mishnah 10

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:10:1)


**הַמִּשְׁרָה.** מָקוֹם שֶׁשּׁוֹרִים בּוֹ פִּשְׁתָּן. וּמַפְסִיד אֶת הַיְרָקוֹת הַסְּמוּכוֹת לוֹ:


המשרה – a place where the steep the flax and cause loss to the vegetables that are near them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:10:2)


**וְאֶת הַכְּרֵשִׁין.** כַּרְתִּי בִּלְשׁוֹן תַּלְמוּד. וְהֵם מַזִּיקִין לַבְּצָלִים הַסְּמוּכִים לָהֶם:


הכרישין – leeks in the language of the Talmud, and they destroy the onions that are near them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:10:3)


**וְאֶת הַחַרְדָּל מִן הַדְּבוֹרִים.** שֶׁהוּא מַפְסִיד הַדְּבַשׁ וְעוֹשֶׂה אוֹתוֹ חָרִיף וָחַד:


ואת החרדל מן הדבורים – which destroys the honey and makes it pungent and sharp.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:10:4)


**רַבִּי יוֹסֵי מַתִּיר בַּחַרְדָּל.** שֶׁיָּכוֹל לוֹמַר לוֹ עַד שֶׁאַתָּה אוֹמֵר לִי הַרְחֵק חַרְדָּלְךָ מִן דְּבוֹרַי, הַרְחֵק דְּבוֹרֶיךָ מֵחַרְדָּלִי, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁבָּאוֹת וְאוֹכְלוֹת פִּרְחֵי חַרְדָּלִי. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


רבי יוסי מתיר בחרדל – because he can say to him (i.e., his fellow): “Until you tell me: Distance your mustard plant from my bees; distance your bees from my mustard plant because they come and eat the buds of my mustard plants.” And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yosi.



### Mishnah 11

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:11:1)


**וּבְחָרוּב וּבְשִׁקְמָה.** שָׁרְשֵׁיהֶן מְרֻבִּין:


ובחרוב ובשקמה – their roots are numerous.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:11:2)


**בֵּין מִלְמַעְלָה.** שֶׁהָאֶחָד מֵהֶן מִלְּמַעְלָה בְּגֹבַהּ שִׁפּוּעַ הַר וְהַשֵּׁנִי בְּשִׁפּוּלוֹ:


בין מלמעלה – because one of them is above in height of the slope of the mountain.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:11:3)


**בֵּין מִן הַצָּד.** בְּקַרְקַע שָׁוָה:


בין מן הצד – on level ground.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:11:4)


**וְנוֹתֵן דָּמִים.** דְּכֵיוָן דְּבִרְשׁוּת נָטַע, שֶׁאֵינוֹ מַזִּיק עַד זְמַן גָּדוֹל, לֹא חִיְּבוּהוּ חֲכָמִים לָקֹץ בְּלֹא דָּמִים בִּשְׁבִיל הֶזֵּקָא דְּיָחִיד:


ונותן דמים – for since he planted with permission, that he doesn’t damage until a great time [passes], the Sages did not require him to cut it down without payment because of the wind/blast of an individual [tree].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:11:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:11:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:11:5)


**רַבִּי יוֹסֵי אוֹמֵר וְכוּ'.** וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


רבי יוסי אומר – And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yosi.



### Mishnah 12

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:12:1)


**סָמוּךְ לִשְׂדֵה חֲבֵרוֹ.** בֵּין שְׂדֵה לָבָן בֵּין שְׂדֵה אִילָן:


סמוך לשדה חבירו – whether it is a bright field lacking shade or it is a cultivated field in which trees grow.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:12:2)


**אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן הִרְחִיק מִמֶּנּוּ אַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת.** כְּדֵי עֲבוֹדַת הַכֶּרֶם. שֶׁכְּשֶׁיַּחֲרֹשׁ אֶת אִילָנוֹתָיו לֹא יְהֵא צָרִיךְ לְהַכְנִיס מַחֲרֵשָׁתוֹ לְתוֹךְ שֶׁל חֲבֵרוֹ. וְהָנֵי מִלֵּי בְּאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל וְכַיּוֹצֵא בָּהּ שֶׁמַּחֲרֵשָׁתָן אֲרֻכָּה. אֲבָל בְּבָבֶל וּבִשְׁאָר אֲרָצוֹת שֶׁמַּחֲרֵשָׁתָן קְצָרָה, בְּהַרְחָקַת שְׁתֵּי אַמּוֹת סַגִּי. וְדַוְקָא בִּגְפָנִים לִגְפָנִים וְאִילָנוֹת לְאִילָנוֹת הוּא דְּסַגִּי בִּשְׁתֵּי אַמּוֹת, אֲבָל הַבָּא לִטַּע שְׂדֵה אִילָן סָמוּךְ לַגְּפָנִים, אֲפִלּוּ בְּבָבֶל וְכַיּוֹצֵא בָּהּ צָרִיךְ לְהַרְחִיק אַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת:


אלא אם כן החיק ממנו ארבע אמות – in order for the word of the vineyard, for when he ploughs his trees, there should not be a need to bring his plough into that (i.e., the field) of his fellow. And these words refer to the Land of Israel, and places like it where their ploughs are long. But in Babylonia and in other countries where their ploughs are short, a distance of two cubits is sufficient, and especially with vines to [other] vines, and trees to trees, where two cubits are sufficient. But, one who comes to plant in an orchard near the vines, even in Babylonia, and places similar to it, one has to make a distance of four cubits.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:12:3)


**מַעֲמִיק שְׁלֹשָׁה טְפָחִים.** בַּעַל הַשָּׂדֶה שֶׁיָּצְאוּ שָׁרְשֵׁי אִילָנוֹת חֲבֵרוֹ לְתוֹךְ שָׂדֵהוּ, קוֹצְצָן בְּעֹמֶק שְׁלֹשָׁה טְפָחִים וְאֵינוֹ חוֹשֵׁשׁ:


מעמיק שלשה טפחים – the owner of the field whose fellow’s tree roots went out into his field, he cus them at a depth of three handbreadths and does not worry.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:12:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:12:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:12:4)


**וְהָעֵצִים שֶׁלּוֹ.** שֶׁל בַּעַל הַשָּׂדֶה. וְהוּא שֶׁיִּהְיֶה הַמָּקוֹם שֶׁחוֹפֵר רָחוֹק מֵאִילַן חֲבֵרוֹ שֵׁשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה אַמָּה אוֹ יוֹתֵר. אֲבָל פָּחוֹת מִכֵּן, הָעֵצִים לְבַעַל הָאִילָן. דְּעַד שֵׁשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה אַמָּה מִנַּק יָנְקֵי, טְפֵי לֹא יָנְקֵי:


והעצים שלו – of the owner of the field, and this will be the place where he ploughs sixteen cubits or more from the tree of his fellow, but less than this, the wood belongs to the owner of the trees, for up to sixteen cubits, they absorb sap, more than this, they don’t suck the sap.



### Mishnah 13

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:13:1)


**קוֹצֵץ.** הָעֲנָפִים עַד גֹבַהּ מְלֹא מַרְדֵּעַ, שֶׁלֹּא יְעַכְּבוּהוּ מִלְּהוֹלִיךְ מַחֲרֵשָׁתוֹ שָׁם:


קוצץ – [he cuts] the branches until the height of the handle protruding over the plough (i.e., the ox-goad), to not prevent him from bringing his plough there (see Talmud Bava Batra 27b).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:13:2)


**מַרְדֵּעַ.** מַלְמַד הַבָּקָר:


מרדע – which teaches the cattle [sense].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:13:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:13:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:13:3)


**הֶחָרוּב וְהַשִּׁקְמָה.** שֶׁצִּלָּן מְרֻבֶּה וְקָשֶׁה לַשָּׂדֶה:


החרוב והשקמה – that their shade is great and difficult for the field.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:13:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:13:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:13:4)


**כְּנֶגֶד הַמִּשְׁקֹלֶת.** קוֹצֵץ כָּל מַה שֶּׁנּוֹטֶה לְתוֹךְ שָׂדֵהוּ. מִשְׁקֹלֶת, חוּט שֶׁבּוֹנֵי הַחוֹמָה תּוֹלִים בָּהּ מִשְׁקֹלֶת שֶׁל אֲבָר:


כנגד המשקולת – cuts everything that hangs over into his field. משקולת/plummet is a threat that those who build walls suspend from it the weight of lead.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:13:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:13:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:13:5)


**וְאִם בֵּית הַשְּׁלָחִין הִיא.** אֶרֶץ צְמֵאָה לְמַיִם:


ואם בית השלחין היא – a land thirsting for water.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:13:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:13:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:13:6)


**כָּל הָאִילָן כְּנֶגֶד הַמִּשְׁקֹלֶת.** אֲפִלּוּ אֵינוֹ חָרוּב וְשִׁקְמָה, קוֹצֵץ כְּנֶגֶד הַמִּשְׁקֹלֶת. שֶׁהַצֵּל רַע לְבֵית הַשְּׁלָחִין:


כל האילן כנגד המשקולת – even if it is not a carob or sycamore tree, he cuts as measured by the plummet, for the shade is bad for a field requiring irrigation.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:13:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:13:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:13:7)


**אַבָּא שָׁאוּל אוֹמֵר כוּ'.** אַרֵישָׁא קָאֵי, דְּאָמַר תַּנָּא קַמָּא קוֹצֵץ מְלֹא מַרְדֵּעַ וַאֲפִלּוּ הִיא אִילַן סְרָק, חוּץ מֵחָרוּב וְשִׁקְמָה, וְאָמַר לֵיהּ אַבָּא שָׁאוּל כָּל אִילַן סְרָק שֶׁאֵינוֹ עוֹשֶׂה פֵּרוֹת קוֹצֵץ כְּנֶגֶד הַמִּשְׁקֹלֶת. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּאַבָּא שָׁאוּל:


אבא שאול אומר וכו' – he is referring to the first part [of the Mishnah], as the first Tanna/teacher stated, “he cuts them off as far as the ox-goad protrudes [over the plough] and even if it is a tree that bears no fruit, except for a carob [tree] and sycamore [tree] and Abba Shaul said to him: Any non-fruit bearing tree may be cut by measuring with the plummet. But the Halakha is not according to Abba Shaul.



### Mishnah 14

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:14:1)


**אִילָן הַנּוֹטֶה לִרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים.** קוֹצֵץ אֶת הָעֲנָפִים כְּדֵי שֶׁיְּהֵא הַגָּמָל עוֹבֵר וְרוֹכְבוֹ:


אילן הנוטה לרה"ר – cut the lowest branches in order that the camel and its rider can pass by.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:14:2)


**גָּמָל טָעוּן פִּשְׁתָּן.** וְאֵין צָרִיךְ לָקֹץ כְּדֵי גָּמָל וְרוֹכְבוֹ, דְּרוֹכֵב גָּחֵין וְחָלֵיף תּוּתֵיהּ:


גמל טעון פשתן – and there is no need to cut in order that the camel and its rider, for the rider bends and passes by underneath him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 2:14:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:14:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/2:14:3)


**מִפְּנֵי הַטֻּמְאָה.** שֶׁמָּא יַאֲהִילוּ הָעֲנָפִים עַל כַּזַּיִת מִן הַמֵּת וְכַיּוֹצֵא בָּהּ וְיִטַּמָּא הָאָדָם הָעוֹבֵר שָׁם. וַהֲלָכָה כַּתַּנָּא קַמָּא בִּלְבַד:


מפני הטומאה – lest the branches form a roof/tent on an olive’s bulk from a dead person, and similar matters, and the person who passes there will become ritually defiled. But the Halakha is according to the first Tanna/teacher alone.



## Chapter 3


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:1:1)


**חֶזְקַת הַבָּתִּים.** מִי שֶׁאָבַד שְׁטָרוֹ וְהֵבִיא עֵדִים שֶׁהֶחֱזִיק שָׁלֹשׁ שָׁנִים בְּכָל הָנָךְ דִּכְתִיבֵי בְּמַתְנִיתִין, נֶאֱמָן לוֹמַר שֶׁהֵם לְקוּחִים בְּיָדוֹ, וְלֹא אָמְרִינַן לֵיהּ אַיְתֵי שְׁטָר מְכִירָה שֶׁמְּכָרוּהָ לְךָ, דְּעַד תְּלַת שָׁנִים מִזְדְּהַר אֱינִישׁ בִּשְׁטָרֵיהּ, טְפֵי מִתְּלַת שָׁנִים לֹא מִזְדְּהַר, וְאָמְרִינַן לִכְשֶׁנֶּגְדּוֹ, אִם אִיתָא דְּלֹא זְבַנְתָּהּ הָיָה לְךָ לִמְחוֹת וְלוֹמַר בִּפְנֵי שְׁנַיִם דְּעוּ שֶׁפְּלוֹנִי אָכֵיל לָהּ לְאַרְעָאִי בְּגַזְלָנוּתָא, וְהָיָה הַדָּבָר מַגִּיעַ לְאָזְנָיו וְהָיָה נִזְהָר בִּשְׁטָרוֹ, דַּחֲבֵרָךְ חַבְרָא אִית לֵיהּ, וְחַבְרָא דַּחֲבֵרָךְ חַבְרָא אִית לֵיהּ, וּמִדְּלֹא מָחִית אַתְּ הוּא דְּאַפְסַדְתְּ:


חזקת הבתים – whomever has lost his document [of sale] and brought witnesses that he has taken possession for three years with all of those things that are written in our Mishnah, he is believed to state that they were purchased property in his hand and we don’t say to him, “bring the bill of sale that it it was sold to you for up to three years, people are careful with documents; more than three years, they (i.e., people) are not careful. And we say to the individual that is against him if you have [proof] that you didn’t sell it, you should have protested and state before two [witnesses]: “know that so-and-so consumed my land through theft” and the matter would reach his ears and he would be careful with his document, for your friend has a friend, and the friend of your friend has a friend, and if you don’t


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:1:2)


**שׁוֹבָכוֹת.** שֶׁמְּגַדְּלִים בָּהֶם יוֹנִים:


שובכות – where they raise/grow doves there.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:1:3)


**בֵּית הַבַּדִּים.** שֶׁעוֹצְרִים בָּהֶם זֵיתִים לַעֲשׂוֹת שֶׁמֶן:


בית הבדים – that they press olives in them to produce oil.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:1:4)


**בֵּית הַשְּׁלָחִין.** מִתּוֹךְ שֶׁמַּעְיָן בְּתוֹכוֹ שֶׁמַּשְׁקִין אוֹתוֹ מִמֶּנּוּ תָּמִיד, עוֹשֶׂה פֵּרוֹת תָּדִיר. וְכָל דָּבָר שֶׁעוֹשֶׂה פֵּרוֹת תָּדִיר חֶזְקָתוֹ שָׁלֹשׁ שָׁנִים מִיּוֹם לְיוֹם:


בית השלחין – for since there is a spring in it that waters the field from it always and produces fruits frequently and anything that produces fruits frequently, its presumption of claim based upon undisturbed possession [during a legally fixed period] is three years from day to day.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:1:5)


**וְהָעֲבָדִים.** וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּקַיְמָא לָן הַגּוֹדְרוֹת אֵין לָהֶם חֲזָקָה, כְּלוֹמַר הַצֹּאן, מִלְּשׁוֹן גִּדְרוֹת צֹאן, אֵין לָהֶם חֲזָקָה, וְהוּא הַדִּין לְכָל בַּעֲלֵי חַיִּים, הַיְנוּ חֲזָקָה לְאַלְתַּר הוּא דְּאֵין לָהֶם, שֶׁאִם הָיוּ הַצֹּאן וְהָעֶבֶד יְדוּעִים שֶׁהֵם שֶׁל אָדָם אֶחָד וְנִכְנְסוּ בְּבֵיתוֹ שֶׁל אָדָם אַחֵר וְטָעַן זֶה שֶׁנִּכְנְסוּ בְּבֵיתוֹ לְקוּחִים הֵם בְּיָדִי וַהֲרֵי אֲנִי מֻחְזָק בָּהֶם, אֵין זוֹ חֲזָקָה, שֶׁכֵּן דַּרְכָּם לָלֶכֶת מִבַּיִת לְבַיִת. אֲבָל אִם הֶחֱזִיק הָעֶבֶד שָׁלֹשׁ שָׁנִים הַוְיָא חֲזָקָה וְאֵין צָרִיךְ לִשְׁטַר מְכִירָה:


והעבדים – and even though that we hold that animals living in folds (i.e., moving livestock, which they leave in the daytime) is no evidence of ownership (as they may have come over by accident – see Talmud Bava Batra 36a), meaning to say that sheep from the language "גדרות צאן"/fenced in sheep, have no presumption of ownership (as they may have come over by accident), and the same law applies to all living creatures; that is, a presumption immediately that they lack this if it was known that the sheep and/or the slaves belonged to someone else, and entered the house of the other person. But this one claims that they entered into his house that they were purchased in his hand: “Behold I am known to hold them in possession. This is not a presumption of ownership because it is their manner to go from house to house. But if he held/took possession of a slave for three years, that is claim based upon an undisturbed possession and there is no need [to produce] a bill of sale.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:1:6)


**שְׂדֵה הַבַּעַל.** הַמִּסְתַּפֶּקֶת מִמֵּי גְּשָׁמִים, אֵינָהּ עוֹשָׂה פֵּרוֹת אֶלָּא פַּעַם אַחַת בְּשָׁנָה:


שדה הבעל – it is supplied from rain water and doesn’t produce fruits other than once a year.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:1:7)


**חֶזְקָתָהּ שָׁלֹשׁ שָׁנִים.** וְאֵינָהּ צְרִיכָה מִיּוֹם לְיוֹם:


חזקתה שלש שנים – and it doesn’t have to be from day to day.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:1:8)


**רַבִּי יִשְׁמָעֵאל אוֹמֵר שְׁלֹשָׁה חֳדָשִׁים וְכוּ'.** שֶׁיֵּשׁ תְּבוּאָה גְּדֵלָה לִשְׁלֹשָׁה חֳדָשִׁים, כְּגוֹן שְׂעֹרִים וְשִׁבֹּלֶת שׁוּעָל וַעֲדָשִׁים, וְנִמְצָא אוֹכֵל שָׁלֹשׁ תְּבוּאוֹת בִּשְׁמֹנָה עָשָׂר חֹדֶשׁ:


רבי ישמעאל אומר שלשה חדשים – there is grain that grows in three months such as barley and oats and lentils and it is found that one eats three grains in eight months.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:1:9)


**רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא אוֹמֵר.** יֵשׁ דָּבָר שֶׁגָּדֵל לִשְׁלֹשִׁים יוֹם, כְּגוֹן שַׁחַת וִירָקוֹת. לְפִיכָךְ אִם אָכְלָה אַרְבָּעָה עָשָׂר חֹדֶשׁ הַוְיָא חֲזָקָה. וְרַבִּי יִשְׁמָעֵאל סָבַר, דְּבַאֲכִילַת שַׁחַת וִירָקוֹת לֹא הַוְיָא חֲזָקָה, אֶלָּא בַּאֲכִילַת תְּבוּאָה וּפֵרוֹת גְּמוּרִים:


רבי עקיבא אומר – there is something that grows in thirty days such as corn at the earliest stage/low growth and vegetables. Therefore, if one ate it for fourteen months that is considered a presumption of ownership. But Rabbi Yishmael holds that the eating of corn at the earliest stage and vegetables are not considered presumption of ownership.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:1:10)


**בַּמֶּה דְבָרִים אֲמוּרִים.** שֶׁצָּרִיךְ שְׁמֹנָה עָשָׂר חֹדֶשׁ לִשְׂדֵה הַבַּעַל:


במה דברים אמורים – that one needs eighteen months for a field sufficiently watered by rain and requiring no artificial irrigation.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:1:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:1:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:1:11)


**בִּשְׂדֵה הַלָּבָן.** שֶׁכָּל פֵּרוֹתֶיהָ נִלְקָטִין בְּפֶרֶק אֶחָד, לְפִיכָךְ צָרִיךְ שָׁלֹשׁ שָׁנִים:


בשדה לבן – [a bright, vegetable or grain field that lacks shade] where all of its fruits are collected at one time, therefore, it requires three [complete] years.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:1:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:1:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:1:12)


**אֲבָל בִּשְׂדֵה הָאִילָן.** שֶׁפֵּרוֹתֶיהָ נִלְקָטִים לִפְרָקִים, עֲנָבִים בְּפֶרֶק אֶחָד, וְזֵיתִים בְּפֶרֶק אֶחָד, וּתְאֵנִים בְּפֶרֶק אֶחָד:


אבל בשדה האילן – that whose fruits are collected at periods – grapes at one period and olives at another period and figs at another period.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:1:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:1:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:1:13)


**כָּנַס אֶת תְּבוּאָתוֹ.** יַיִן שֶׁל גְּפָנִים:


כנס את תבואתו – wine of grapes.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:1:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:1:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:1:14)


**מָסַק אֶת זֵיתָיו וְכָנַס אֶת קֵיצוֹ.** לִקֵּט תְּאֵנִים וְיִבְּשָׁן וְהִכְנִיסָן לְבֵיתוֹ, הֲרֵי זוֹ חֲזָקָה כְּאִלּוּ הֵן שָׁלֹשׁ שָׁנִים. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה לֹא כְּרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא וְלֹא כְּרַבִּי יִשְׁמָעֵאל:


כנס את זיתיו וכנס את קיצו – he harvested figs and dried them, and brought them into his house, that is a presumption of ownership, as if it is three years, but the Halakha is not according to either Rabbi Akiva nor Rabbi Yishmael.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:2:1)


**שָׁלֹשׁ אֲרָצוֹת לַחֲזָקָה.** שָׁלֹשׁ אֲרָצוֹת בְּאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל חֲלוּקוֹת זוֹ מִזּוֹ לְעִנְיַן חֲזָקָה, שֶׁאִם הֶחֱזִיק בְּאֶחָד מֵאֵלּוּ הָאֲרָצוֹת וְהָיָה בַּעַל הַקַּרְקַע בְּאַחֶרֶת, אֵין חֶזְקָתוֹ חֲזָקָה, לְפִי שֶׁאֵין שְׁיָרוֹת מְצוּיוֹת מִזּוֹ לָזוֹ. וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵינָן שְׁעַת חֵרוּם וּמִלְחָמָה, כִּשְׁעַת חֵרוּם דָּמִי, וְאִם הָיָה בַּעַל הַקַּרְקַע מוֹחֶה לֹא הָיָה מִי שֶׁיּוֹדִיעַ לְזֶה הַמַּחֲזִיק, לְפִיכָךְ הָיָה רָאוּי לְזֶה הַמַּחֲזִיק לִזָּהֵר בִּשְׁטָרוֹ, וְכֵיוָן שֶׁלֹּא נִזְהַר הִפְסִיד:


שלש ארצות לחזקה – three [distinctive] lands in the Land of Israel are divided each from the other concerning the matter of presumption of claim of ownership. For if he had taken possession in one of these [distinctive] lands, and the owner of the land is in the other, his presumption of ownership is not a presumption, because the caravans are not ae not found from one to the other, and even though it is not an emergency or [time of] war, it is similar to an emergency. But if the owner of the land would renounce, there would not be someone to inform the other who has taken possession. Therefore, it was appropriate to the one taking possession to be careful with his document, and since he was not careful, he would lose.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:2:2)


**עַד שֶׁיְּהֵא עִמּוֹ בַמְּדִינָה.** שֶׁיִּהְיוּ שְׁנֵיהֶם בִּיהוּדָה אוֹ שְׁנֵיהֶם בְּעֵבֶר הַיַּרְדֵּן, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁזֶּה בְּעִיר אַחַת וְזֶה בְּעִיר אַחֶרֶת, דְּכֵיוָן דִּשְׁיָרוֹת מְצוּיוֹת הָיָה לוֹ לִמְחוֹת, וְכֵיוָן דְּלֹא מִחָה הִפְסִיד:


עד שיהא עמו במדינה – that both of them would be in Judea, or both of them beyond the Jordan River and even though this one was in one city and that one was in another city, because there were caravans found, he could protest, and since he did not protest, he lost out.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:2:3)


**אָמַר רַבִּי יְהוּדָה וְכוּ'.** קָסָבַר רַבִּי יְהוּדָה טַעְמָא דַּחֲזָקָה לַאו מִשּׁוּם דְּעַד תְּלַת שְׁנִין מִזְדְּהַר אֱינָשׁ בִּשְׁטָרֵיהּ, טְפֵי לֹא מִזְדְּהַר, אֶלָּא טַעְמָא דַּחֲזָקָה לְדִידֵיהּ, מִשּׁוּם דְּלֹא שָׁבִיק אֱינִישׁ דְּאָכְלֵי אַרְעָא דִּידֵיהּ אֲפִלּוּ שָׁעָה אַחַת וְשָׁתֵיק, וְהַאי דְּהִרְחִיקוּ תְּלַת שְׁנִין כְּדֵי שֶׁאִם הָיָה בְּאַסְפַּמְיָא וְכוּ', אֲבָל אִם הָיָה עִמּוֹ בָּעִיר, לְאַלְתַּר הָוֵי חֲזָקָה. וּבְשָׁלֹשׁ אֲרָצוֹת הַלָּלוּ שֶׁאֵין שְׁיָרוֹת מְצוּיוֹת, בִּתְלַת שְׁנִין מִיהָא הָוֵי חֲזָקָה. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


אמר ר' יהודה וכו' – for Rabbi Yehuda holds that the reason for presumption of ownership is not because until three years because people are careful with their documents, [but] more [than this], they are not careful, but the reason for his presumption of ownership is because people will not leave [someone, a usurper] consuming his own land even for one hour and remain silent. But this one that distanced himself for three years in order that if he was in Spain, etc. But if he was with him in the city, immediately, there would be presumption of ownership and in these three [distinct] lands [of the Land of Israel] where caravans are not found, during the three years, however, he would have presumption of ownership. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:3:1)


**כָּל חֲזָקָה שֶׁאֵין עִמָּהּ טַעֲנָה.** שֶׁיִּטְעֹן לָמָּה מַחֲזִיק בְּשֶׁל חֲבֵרוֹ, אֵינָהּ חֲזָקָה:


כל חזקה שאין עמה טענה – that he will make the claim why does the person who possesses that which belongs to his fellow is not valid possession.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:3:2)


**וְהַבָּא מֵחֲמַת יְרֻשָּׁה.** שֶׁהֶחֱזִיק בָּהּ שָׁלֹשׁ שָׁנִים מִכֹּחַ יְרֻשַּׁת אָבִיו, שֶׁהָיְתָה שֶׁל אָבִיו בְּיוֹם מוֹתוֹ:


והבא מחמת ירושה – that he held possession of it for three years through the strength of the inheritance of his father, for it was his father’s on the day of his death.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:3:3)


**אֵין צָרִיךְ טַעֲנָה.** לְבָרֵר אֵיךְ בָּאָה לְיַד אָבִיו. וּמִיהוּ רְאָיָה בָּעֵי שֶׁרָאוּ אָבִיו דָּר בּוֹ יוֹם אֶחָד:


אין צריך טענה – to prove how it came to his father’s hand. However, proof is required when the saw his father live it in one day.
