###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:3:4)


**הָאֻמָּנִים.** שֶׁמְּתַקְּנִים כֵּלִים.


האומנים – when they repair utensils –


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:3:5)


**אֵין לָהֶם חֲזָקָה.** אִם הֵם מֻחְזָקִים בְּכֵלִים שֶׁל אֲחֵרִים אֵין יְכוֹלִים לִטְעֹן לְקוּחִים הֵם בְּיָדִי, וַאֲפִלּוּ הֵם כֵּלִים שֶׁאֵין עֲשׂוּיִין לְהַשְׁאִיל וּלְהַשְׂכִּיר. וְהָנֵי מִלֵּי כְּשֶׁהַכְּלִי מָצוּי בְּפָנֵינוּ בְּיַד הָאֻמָּן. אֲבָל אִם לֹא נִמְצָא הַכְּלִי בְּפָנֵינוּ בְּיַד הָאֻמָּן אֶלָּא שֶׁבָּא אֶחָד מִן הַשּׁוּק וְטָעַן לָאֻמָּן כְּלִי יֵשׁ לִי אֶצְלְךָ שֶׁנָּתַתִּי לְךָ לְתַקֵּן, הַחֲזִירֵהוּ לִי, וְטָעַן הָאֻמָּן אֱמֶת שֶׁהוּא אֶצְלִי אֲבָל אַתָּה מָכַרְתָּ לִי, נֶאֱמָן הָאֻמָּן בִּשְׁבוּעָה, בְּמִגּוֹ דְּאִי בָּעֵי אָמַר לֹא הָיָה דְּבָרִים מֵעוֹלָם אוֹ הֶחֱזַרְתִּי לְךָ. וְכֵן טָעַן הָאֻמָּן כָּךְ פָּסַקְתָּ לִי בִּשְׂכַר תִּקּוּנוֹ, וְהַלָּה אוֹמֵר לֹא פָּסַקְתִּי אֶלָּא פָּחוֹת. אִם הַכְּלִי מָצוּי בְּפָנֵינוּ בְּיַד הָאֻמָּן בַּעַל הַכְּלִי נֶאֱמָן. וְאִם אֵין הַכְּלִי מָצוּי בְּפָנֵינוּ בְּיַד הָאֻמָּן הֲרֵי הָאֻמָּן נֶאֱמָן בִּשְׁבוּעָה וַאֲפִלּוּ מָסַר לוֹ בְּעֵדִים, בְּמִגּוֹ דְּאִי בָּעֵי אָמַר הֶחֱזַרְתִּיו לְךָ.


They don’t have a claim of possession – If they were in possession of utensils of others, they are not able to claim that there were purchased in his hand and even if they are utensils that are not normally lent or rented out. And these words [refer] to when the utensil is found before us in the hand of the artisan. But if the utensil is not found before us in the hand of the artisan, but rather, that someone comes from the marketplace and claimed to the artisan: “I have a utensil with you that I gave you to repair. Return it to me.” And the artisan claimed: “It is true that it (i.e., the utensil) is with me, but you sold it to me,” the artisan is believed through an oath with מיגו/a legal rule according to which the deponent’s statement is accepted as true on the ground that, if he had intended to tell a lie, he might have invented one more advantageous to his case (see Talmud Bava Batra 31a). For if he wanted, he could have said “that nothing had ever taken place [between us]” or “I returned it to you.” And similarly, the artisan made the claim that is what you fixed a price with a charge for the repair.” But the other can say, “I did not make that arrangement other than for less.” If the utensil is found before us in the hand of the artisan, the owner of the utensil is believed. But if the utensil is not found before us in the hand of the artisan, the artisan is believed with an oath, and even if he transferred it to him with witnesses with the מיגו (see above) that if he had wanted, he could have said, “I had returned it to you.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:3:6)


**וְהַשֻּׁתָּפִים.** שֶׁיֵּשׁ לָהֶן קַרְקַע בְּשֻׁתָּפוּת וְאָכַל אֶחָד מֵהֶן שָׁלֹשׁ שָׁנִים כָּל הַפֵּרוֹת, אֵינָהּ חֲזָקָה. וְהָנֵי מִלֵּי, דְּאֵין בַּקַּרְקַע דִּין חֲלֻקָּה. אֲבָל אִם יֵשׁ בַּקַּרְקַע דִּין חֲלֻקָּה וַאֲכָלָהּ אֶחָד מֵהֶן שָׁלֹשׁ שָׁנִים, הָוֵי חֲזָקָה:


והשותפים – they have property held jointly and one of them consumed all the produce for three years, it is not considered a presumption of possession. And these words are when the land does not have the law of division. But if the property has the law of division, and one of them consumed for three years, that is considered possession.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:3:7)


**וְהָאֲרִיסִים.** שֶׁיּוֹרְדִים לַקַּרְקַע לְמֶחֱצָה לִשְׁלִישׁ וְלִרְבִיעַ, וְאָכַל כָּל הַפֵּרוֹת שָׁלֹשׁ שָׁנִים, אֵינָהּ חֲזָקָה. וְדַוְקָא בַּאֲרִיסֵי בָּתֵּי אָבוֹת, שֶׁהוּא כְּמוֹ אַפּוֹטְרוֹפּוֹס עַל הַבֵּן, אֲבָל הָאָרִיס שֶׁהוֹרִידוֹ בַּעַל הַקַּרְקַע עַצְמוֹ וְאָכַל כָּל הַפֵּרוֹת שָׁלֹשׁ שָׁנִים, יֵשׁ לוֹ חֲזָקָה:


והאריסים – he goes down into the land for one half, for one-third or for one-fourth, and consumed all the produce for three years, it is not considered presumption of possession. And especially with regard to hereditary land-tenants for he is like an אפוטרופוס/a guardian for the son but the tenant farmer who was brought down by the owner of the land himself and he consumed all the produce for three years, he does have presumption of possession.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:3:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:3:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:3:8)


**וְאֵין לָאִישׁ חֲזָקָה בְּנִכְסֵי אִשְׁתּוֹ.** וַאֲפִלּוּ כָּתַב לָהּ בְּעוֹדָהּ אֲרוּסָה דִּין וּדְבָרִים אֵין לִי בִּנְכָסַיִךְ וְלֹא בְּפֵרוֹתֵיהֶן, דְּהַשְׁתָּא אֵינוֹ אוֹכֵל פֵּרוֹת מִן הַדִּין, וְאַחַר כָּךְ הֵבִיא רְאָיָה שֶׁאָכַל פֵּרוֹת שָׁלֹשׁ שָׁנִים, לֹא הַוְיָא חֲזָקָה, דְּאוֹרְחָא דְּאִתְּתָא לְהָנִיחַ לְבַעְלָהּ שֶׁיֹּאכַל פֵּרוֹת נְכָסֶיהָ בֵּין כַּדִּין בֵּין שֶׁלֹּא כַּדִּין:


ואין לאיש חזקה בנכסי אשתו – and even if he wrote to her while she was still his betrothed an unequivocal judgment: “I have nothing with regard to your property, nor the fruits of it, for now, he does not consume its produce from the law, and afterwards brought a proof that he ate produce for three years, that is not considered presumption of possession, for it was the manner of the wife to allow her husband that he can consume the fruits of her property, whether by law or not by law.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:3:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:3:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:3:9)


**וְאֵין לָאִשָּׁה חֲזָקָה בְּנִכְסֵי בַעְלָהּ.** וַאֲפִלּוּ יִחֵד לָהּ קַרְקַע לִמְזוֹנוֹתֶיהָ וְאָכְלָה פֵּרוֹת מִקַּרְקַע אַחֶרֶת שֶׁל בַּעְלָהּ שָׁלֹשׁ שָׁנִים, אֲפִלּוּ הָכִי לֹא הַוְיָא חֲזָקָה, שֶׁדֶּרֶךְ הָאִישׁ לְהָנִיחַ לְאִשְׁתּוֹ שֶׁתֹּאכַל בִּנְכָסָיו אֲפִלּוּ בְּדָבָר שֶׁאֵין לָהּ בּוֹ זְכוּת:


ואין לאשה חזקה בנכסי בעלה – and even if he designated for her land for her food and she consumed produce from another land belonging to her husband for thee years, even so, this is not a presumption of possession, for it is manner of a man to allow his wife that she may consume from his property, even with something that she does not have authority.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:3:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:3:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:3:10)


**וְלֹא לָאָב בְּנִכְסֵי הַבֵּן וְלֹא לַבֵּן וְכוּ'.** לְפִי שֶׁהֵם כְּאַפּוֹטְרוֹפְּסִים זֶה עַל זֶה:


ולא לאב בנכסי הבן [ולא לבן וכו'] – Because they are like guardians one for the other.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:3:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:3:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:3:11)


**בַּמֶּה דְבָרִים אֲמוּרִים.** דְּאֵינָהּ חֲזָקָה:


במה דברים אמורים – that they are not a presumption of possession.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:3:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:3:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:3:12)


**בְּמַחֲזִיק.** בַּחֲזָקָה שֶׁיֵּשׁ עָלֶיהָ עִרְעוּר, שֶׁחֲבֵרוֹ טוֹעֵן גְּזוּלָה הִיא בְּיָדְךָ:


במחזיק – when he is possessing something where there is a protest/evidence of illegitimacy or disqualification (see Talmud Bava Batra 31b), for his fellow makes the claim that what is in your hand was stolen.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:3:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:3:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:3:13)


**אֲבָל הַנּוֹתֵן מַתָּנָה.** בְּפָנֵינוּ, וְאָמַר לִמְקַבֵּל הַמַּתָּנָה לֵךְ חֲזֵק וּקְנֵה, כָּל הָנָךְ דְּאָמְרִינַן לְעֵיל בְּמַתְנִיתִין דְּאֵין לָהֶם חֲזָקָה, מִשֶּׁהֶחֱזִיקוּ כִּשְׁאָר מְקַבְּלֵי מַתָּנָה, קָנוּ, וְאֵין הַנּוֹתֵן יָכוֹל לַחֲזֹר בּוֹ. וְהָאִשָּׁה שֶׁנָּתְנָה אוֹ שֶׁמָּכְרָה לְבַעְלָהּ נִכְסֵי מְלוֹג שֶׁלָּהּ, מִשֶּׁהֶחֱזִיק בָּהֶן הַבַּעַל קָנָה, וְאֵינָהּ יְכוֹלָה לוֹמַר נַחַת רוּחַ עָשִׂיתִי לְבַעֲלִי. דְּדַוְקָא בְּנִכְסֵי צֹאן בַּרְזֶל אוֹ בְּקַרְקַע שֶׁיִּחֵד לָהּ בַּעְלָהּ בִּכְתֻבָּתָהּ אָמְרִינַן שֶׁאֵין מִכְרָהּ מֶכֶר וְאֵין מַתְּנָתָהּ מַתָּנָה מִפְּנֵי שֶׁיְּכוֹלָה לוֹמַר נַחַת רוּחַ עָשִׂיתִי לְבַעֲלִי, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁיֵּשׁ לְבַעְלָהּ שַׁיָּכוּת בָּהֶן. אֲבָל בְּנִכְסֵי מְלוּג שֶׁאֵין לְבַעְלָהּ בְּעִקָּרָן שׁוּם שַׁיָּכוּת, אֵינָהּ יְכוֹלָה לוֹמַר נַחַת רוּחַ עָשִׂיתִי לְבַעֲלִי. וְכֵן הָאִישׁ שֶׁמָּכַר לְאִשְׁתּוֹ מִנְּכָסָיו, אִם הַמָּעוֹת שֶׁקָּנְתָה בָּהֶן הָאִשָּׁה אוֹתָן נְכָסִים לֹא הָיוּ טְמוּנִים אֶצְלָהּ, הַמֶּכֶר קַיָּם, וְיִהְיוּ אוֹתָן הַנְּכָסִים לָאִשָּׁה וְהַבַּעַל אוֹכֵל פֵּרוֹת. וְאִם הָיוּ אוֹתָן מָעוֹת טְמוּנִים אֶצְלָהּ, הַמֶּכֶר בָּטֵל, דְּאָמַר, לִגְּלוּיֵי זוּזֵי שֶׁהָיוּ טְמוּנִים אֶצְלָהּ אָמַרְתִּי שֶׁאֲנִי מוֹכֵר לָהּ:


אבל הנותן מתנה – in our presence and stated to the recipient: “this gift is for you, take possession and acquire it.” All of these [things] that are stated above in our Mishnah lack presumption of possession, when they took possession like others who received a gift and acquired it, that the giver cannot retract. But the woman who gave or had sold to her husband her usufruct (i.e., that which belongs to the wife’s estate that the husband can use without responsibility for loss or deterioration), and when he took possession of it, the husband acquired it, and she cannot say: “I gave pleasure/gratification to my husband,” and specifically for mort-main (i.e., the wife’s estate is held by her husband, which, the case of her death or divorce, he must restore in specie, being responsible for all his landed property for loss or deterioration), or property that her husband designated for her in the Jewish marriage contract, we say that her sale is not a sale, and her gift [to someone] is not a gift, because she can claim: ‘I did it give my husband pleasure,” because her husband has an attachment to them. But her usufruct, which her husband, in principle, has no attachment to them, she cannot say: “I gave pleasure/gratification to my husband. But similarly, the man who sold to his wife from his property, if the monies that the wife purchased them are not that property, they are not hidden/preserved with her. The sale goes is established, and those properties belong to the woman and the husband can eat the produce, and if those monies were hidden with her, the sale is void, for he can say, “[it was done] to reveal monies that were hidden/preserved with her. I stated that I am selling to her.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:3:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:3:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:3:14)


**וְהָאַחִין שֶׁחָלְקוּ.** וְהֶחֱזִיק כָּל אֶחָד מֵהֶן בְּחֶלְקוֹ, אֵין יְכוֹלִים לַחֲזֹר:


והאחין שחלקו – and each one held possession of his portion and they cannot retract.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:3:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:3:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:3:15)


**וְהַמַּחֲזִיק בְּנִכְסֵי הַגֵּר.** שֶׁמֵּת וְאֵין לוֹ יוֹרְשִׁים, וְכָל הַקּוֹדֵם לְהַחֲזִיק בִּנְכָסָיו זָכָה:


והמחזיק בנכסי הגר – [the convert] who died and he no inheritors. And whomever comes first to take possession of his properties, takes possession.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:3:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:3:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:3:16)


**נָעַל.** שֶׁעָשָׂה דֶּלֶת:


נעל – that he made a door.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:3:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:3:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:3:17)


**וְגָדַר.** אוֹ שֶׁעָשָׂה חוֹמָה:


וגדר –[or] he made a wall.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:3:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:3:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:3:18)


**וּפָרַץ.** אוֹ שֶׁפָּרַץ בָּהּ פִּרְצָה:


ופרץ – or made a breach in it.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:4:1)


**מְשַׁלְּשִׁין בֵּינֵיהֶם.** כָּל כַּת יִתֵּן הַשְּׁלִישׁ. שֶׁהֲרֵי הֵן שָׁלֹשׁ כִּתּוֹת לְשָׁלֹשׁ שָׁנִים:


משלשין ביניהם – each band pays one-third, for they are three bands for the three years (i.e., two plotting/scheming witnesses for each year).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:4:2)


**שְׁלֹשָׁה אַחִים.** לְכָל שָׁנָה אָח אֶחָד וְאַחֵר עִם הָאָח, שֶׁאוֹתוֹ אַחֵר מֵעִיד עִם כֻּלָּן:


שלשה אחים – for each year, one brother, and another person with the brother, and that other person testifies with all of them and there three [distinct] testimonies, for what this one testifies, the other did not testify, and therefore, their testimony is valid.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:4:3)


**הֲרֵי כָּאן שָׁלֹשׁ עֵדֻיּוֹת.** דְּמַאי דְּמַסְהֵיד הַאי לֹא מַסְהֵיד הַאי, וּלְכָךְ עֵדוּתָן כְּשֵׁרָה:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:4:4)


**וְהֵן עֵדוּת אַחַת.** לְעִנְיַן הֲזָמָה, שֶׁאִם הוּזְמוּ מְשַׁלְּשִׁין בֵּינֵיהֶן, וְאֵין נַעֲשִׂים זוֹמְמִין עַד שֶׁיּוּזְמוּ כֻּלָּן:


והן עדות אחת – regarding the conviction of false witnesses, if they were found to be refuted, they divide up the cost between them, and they are not made false witnesses until all of them are proved to be false witnesses.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:5:1)


**שֶׁיֵּשׁ לָהֶם חֲזָקָה.** אִם הֶחֱזִיק בְּקַרְקַע חֲבֵרוֹ לְעִנְיָן זֶה שָׁלֹשׁ שָׁנִים הַוְיָא חֲזָקָה:


שיש להם חזקה – if he had taken possession of his fellow land for this purpose for three years, it is considered possession.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:5:2)


**הָיָה מַעֲמִיד בְּהֶמְתּוֹ בֶּחָצֵר.** הַאי תַּנָּא בַּחֲצַר הַשֻּׁתָּפִים קָא מַיְרֵי, דְּלֹא קָפְדֵי אַהֲדָדֵי אַהַעֲמָדַת בְּהֵמָה וְכַיּוֹצֵא בָּהּ, וּמִשּׁוּם הָכִי לֹא הַוְיָא חֲזָקָה אֲפִלּוּ כִּי הֶחֱזִיק לְהַאי מִלְּתָא שָׁלֹשׁ שָׁנִים:


היה מעמיד במהתו בחצר – this Tanna/teacher is speaking of a jointly owned courtyard, where they are not strict with each other concerning keeping cattle [in the courtyard] and other similar things. And because of this, it is not considered a presumption of possession even though he had possessed that thing for three years.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:5:3)


**אֲבָל אִם עָשָׂה מְחִצָּה וְכוּ'.** דִּבְכִי הַאי גַּוְנָא וַדַּאי קָפְדֵי, אִי שָׁתִיק לָהּ שָׁלֹשׁ שָׁנִים וְלֹא מִחָה, וַדַּאי הַוְיָא חֲזָקָה. וְרַבֵּינוּ מֹשֶׁה בַּר מַיְמוֹנִי פֵּרֵשׁ, דִּבְשֻׁתָּף דְּקָפֵיד אַעֲשִׂיַּת מְחִצָּה, אִם עָשָׂה שֻׁתָּפוֹ מְחִצָּה וְלֹא מִחָה לְאַלְתַּר הַוְיָא חֲזָקָה. אֲבָל אֱינָשׁ דְּעָלְמָא אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁעָשָׂה מְחִצָּה וְלֹא מִחָה לֹא הַוְיָא חֲזָקָה עַד לְאַחַר שָׁלֹשׁ שָׁנִים:


אבל אם עשה מחיצה וכו' – for in such a manner, he is definitely strict. If he had been silent about this for three years and didn’t protest, it is considered possession. But Rabbi Moshe ben Maimonuni commented that a joint owner who is strict about making a partition and no one protested immediately is considered possession. But even though he made a partition, as a mere individual and no one protested, it is not considered possession until after three years.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:6:1)


**הַמַּרְזֵב.** צִנּוֹר קָטָן שֶׁנּוֹתְנִים אוֹתוֹ בְּסוֹף צִנּוֹר הַגָּדוֹל הַהוֹלֵךְ עַל פְּנֵי כָּל הַגַּג:


מרזב – a small spout, that they place it at the end of the large spout that goes all around the perimeter of the roof.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:6:2)


**אֵין לוֹ חֲזָקָה וְיֵשׁ לִמְקוֹמוֹ חֲזָקָה.** אִם הָיָה שָׁם מַרְזֵב וּבָא בַּעַל הֶחָצֵר לְעָקְרוֹ לְגַמְרֵי שֶׁלֹּא יִשָּׁפְכוּ מֵי הַגַּג לְתוֹךְ חֲצֵרוֹ, אֵינוֹ יָכוֹל, שֶׁכְּבָר יֶשׁ לוֹ חֲזָקָה לָזֶה שֶׁיִּהְיוּ מֵימֵי גַּגּוֹ שׁוֹפְכִים דֶּרֶךְ אוֹתוֹ מַרְזֵב. אֲבָל אִם בָּא לְהָפְכוֹ, שֶׁהָיָה מֻנָּח לְרוּחַ דְּרוֹמִי וּבָא לָתֵת אוֹתוֹ לְרוּחַ צְפוֹנִי, אֵין בַּעַל הַמַּרְזֵב יָכוֹל לְעַכֵּב עָלָיו, דְּלַאו מִידֵי אַפְסְדֵיהּ, וְאֵין לוֹ חֲזָקָה שֶׁיִּהְיֶה תָּמִיד עוֹמֵד בְּרוּחַ זוֹ:


אין לו חזקה ויש למקומו חזקה – if there was a there a spout and the owner of the courtyard came to uproot it completely, so that the water of he roof would not spill into his courtyard, he is not able to do so, for he already has possession for this that the root waters spill via that spout, but if he comes to reverse it, that it was placed in the southern direction and he comes to place it in the northern direction, the owner of the movable tube attached to the roof gutters cannot delay it, for it is not something that causes him loss, and he lacks possession, for it will always stand in that direction.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:6:3)


**מַרְזֵב.** מָקוֹם שֶׁהַטִּפָּה שֶׁל גְּשָׁמִים זָבָה מִמֶּנּוּ. מָר, טִפָּה. כְּמוֹ הֵן גּוֹיִם כְּמַר מִדְּלִי (ישעיה מ):


מרזב – the place where the drop of rain water flows from it. [The word] מר /bitter is a טפה/droop, like (Isaiah 40:15): “The nations are but a drop in a bucket.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:6:4)


**מַזְחִילָה.** צִנּוֹר גָּדוֹל הַמַּחֲזִיק כָּל אֹרֶךְ הַגַּג. וְהָוֵי מִלְּתָא דִּקְבִיעוּתָא, לְכָךְ יֶשׁ לוֹ חֲזָקָה:


מזחילה – a large spout that supports all the length of the roof. This is a something fixed, therefore it has presumed possession.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:6:5)


**סֻלָּם הַמִּצְרִי.** קָטָן וְאֵינוֹ קָבוּעַ, וְלֹא קָפֵיד עֲלֵיהּ אִם נוֹתֵן אוֹתוֹ לְתוֹךְ חֲצַר חֲבֵרוֹ כְּדֵי לַעֲלוֹת בּוֹ לְגַגּוֹ וּלְשׁוֹבָכוֹ, לְכָךְ אֵין לוֹ חֲזָקָה:


סולם [המצרי] – small and is not fixed, and no one is strict about it if one puts it into the courtyard of his fellow in order to ascend on it to his roof or to the dovecote. Therefore, it does not have presumption of possession.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:6:6)


**חַלּוֹן הַמִּצְרִית.** חַלּוֹן קָטָן שֶׁאֵין רֹאשׁוֹ שֶׁל אָדָם נִכְנָס בּוֹ:


חלון המצרי – a small window where the head of person cannot enter it,


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:6:7)


**אֵין לוֹ חֲזָקָה.** וְאִי בָּעֵי בַּעַל הֶחָצֵר לִבְנוֹת כְּנֶגֶד הַחַלּוֹן וּלְסָתְמוֹ, לֹא מָצֵי בַּעַל הַחַלּוֹן לוֹמַר כְּבָר הֶחֱזַקְתִּי בּוֹ וְאֵינְךָ יָכוֹל לִסְתֹּם, דְּאָמַר לֵיהּ הִיאַךְ, לֹא נִתְפַּיַּסְתִּי אֶלָּא לְפִי שֶׁלֹּא הָיָה לִי בּוֹ הֶזֵּק. וַאֲפִלּוּ הָכִי אִי הֲוָה בָּעֵי מֵעִקָּרָא כְּשֶׁפְּתָחוֹ הָוֵי מָצֵי מְעַכֵּב עָלָיו שֶׁלֹּא יִפְתְּחֶנּוּ, וַאֲפִלּוּ הָיָה גָּבוֹהַּ יוֹתֵר מֵאַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת, דְּמָצֵי אָמַר לֵיהּ שֶׁמָּא תָּשִׂים סַפְסָל סָמוּךְ לַחַלּוֹן וְתַבִּיט בִּי, וְאַחַר שֶׁהִנִּיחוֹ נַמִּי וּפָתַח, אֵין לוֹ חֲזָקָה:


he has no presumption of possession, but if the owner of the courtyard wanted to build opposite the widow and to close it up, the owner of the window cannot say: “I have already taken possession of it and you cannot close it up.” And he said to him this: “You were not appeased other than that I didn’t have any damages in it, and even so, if he had wanted from the outset, when he opened it up, he could have delayed it that he would not open it. And even if it was higher than four cubits, for he could have said to him, lest you place a small bench near the window so that you can look at me, and after he placed it [also] he opened it, he has no presumption of possession.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:6:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:6:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:6:8)


**וְלַצּוֹרִית יֶשׁ לוֹ חֲזָקָה.** חַלּוֹן שֶׁרֹאשׁוֹ שֶׁל אָדָם נִכְנָס בּוֹ, וַאֲפִלּוּ הוּא לְמַעְלָה מֵאַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת. אוֹ חַלּוֹן הֶעָשׂוּי לְאוֹרָה, וַאֲפִלּוּ הוּא קָטָן מְאֹד. אוֹ חַלּוֹן שֶׁהוּא לְמַטָּה מֵאַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת. כָּל אֶחָד מִשְּׁלֹשָׁה חַלּוֹנוֹת הַלָּלוּ אִם הִנִּיחוֹ וּפָתַח, יֶשׁ לוֹ חֲזָקָה וְשׁוּב אֵין יָכוֹל לִבְנוֹת כְּנֶגְדּוֹ וּלְסָתְמוֹ, דְּיֵשׁ חֲזָקָה לִנְזָקִין. חוּץ מֵעָשָׁן וְאָבָק וּבֵית הַכִּסֵּא כְּלוֹמַר רֵיחַ רַע, שֶׁאֵלּוּ הַשְּׁלֹשָׁה אֵין לָהֶם חֲזָקָה אֲפִלּוּ הֶחֱזִיק בָּהֶם כַּמָּה שָׁנִים. אֲבָל הֶזֵּק רְאִיָּה יֶשׁ לָהּ חֲזָקָה. וְלֹא בָּעֵי חֲזָקָה לִנְזָקִין שָׁלֹשׁ שָׁנִים, אֶלָּא מִכִּי קָאֵי נִזְקָא כְּדֵי שֶׁיּוּכַל לוֹמַר לַנִּזָּק כְּבָר יָדַעְתָּ בַּנֵּזֶק וְלֹא מָחִיתָ, הַוְיָא חֲזָקָה:


ולצורית יש לו חזקה – a window where the head of a person can enter into it, and even where it is higher than four cubits, or a window that is made of for its light, and even if it is very small, or a window which is lower than four cubits, each one of these three windows, if he left it and opened it, he has possession, but furthermore, he is not able to build opposite it and close it up, for there is possession for damages except for fumigating and dust and the bathroom, meaning to say, bad smell, for these three [things] do not have presumption of possession, even if he held them several years. But damage is a proof that he has possession, and he doesn’t need possession for damages for three years but when damage occurs, in order that he can say to the one who suffered damage: “you already knew of this damage and didn’t protest, this is possession.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:6:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:6:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:6:9)


**מַלְבֵּן.** בִּנְיָן מִבַּחוּץ כְּעֵין מַשְׁקוֹף מִלְּמַעְלָה וְאַסְקוּפָּה מִלְּמַטָּה:


מלבן – a building from outside like a kind of lintel from above or a lower door-sill from below.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:6:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:6:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:6:10)


**זִיז.** עֵץ אוֹ אֶבֶן הַיּוֹצֵא מִכָּתְלוֹ לַאֲוִיר חֲצַר חֲבֵרוֹ, אִם יֶשׁ בּוֹ טֶפַח אוֹ יוֹתֵר, יֶשׁ לוֹ חֲזָקָה וְאֵין יָכוֹל בַּעַל הֶחָצֵר לִבְנוֹת כְּנֶגְדּוֹ לִסְתֹּם הַזִּיז, מֵאַחַר שֶׁהֶחֱזִיק בּוֹ:


זיז – wood or stone that projects out from its wall to the airspace of his fellow’s courtyard. If it has a handbreadth or more, he has possession and the owner of the courtyard opposite to him cannot build to close the projection [serving as a shed over the entrance], after he has taken possession of it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:6:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:6:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:6:11)


**וְיָכוֹל לִמְחוֹת בּוֹ.** כְּשֶׁבָּא בַּעַל הַכֹּתֶל לְהוֹצִיא הַזִּיז שֶׁיֵּשׁ בּוֹ טֶפַח אוֹ יוֹתֵר, יָכוֹל בַּעַל הֶחָצֵר לִמְחוֹת בּוֹ שֶׁלֹּא יוֹצִיאֶנּוּ:


ויכול למחות בו – when the owner of the wall comes to remove the projection which has a handbreadth or more, the owner of the courtyard can protest it that he should not remove it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:6:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:6:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:6:12)


**פָּחוֹת מִטֶּפַח.** לֹא הָוֵי מִידֵי דִּקְבִיעוּתָא, לְכָךְ לֹא הָוֵי חֲזָקָה וְיָכוֹל לִבְנוֹת כְּנֶגְדּוֹ וּלְסָתְמוֹ, וּכְשֶׁבָּא בַּעַל הַכֹּתֶל לְהוֹצִיאוֹ לְכַתְּחִלָּה, אֵין בַּעַל הֶחָצֵר יָכוֹל לְעַכֵּב עָלָיו:


פחות מטפח – this is not a matter of being fixed. Therefore, he does not have possession, and he can build opposite him and close it up, and when the owner of the wall comes to remove it ab initio, the owner of the courtyard cannot prevent it.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:7:1)


**לַחֲצַר הַשֻּׁתָּפִים.** לְחָצֵר שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ שֻׁתָּפוּת בָּהּ, וְכָל שֶׁכֵּן לַחֲצַר חֲבֵרוֹ. מִשּׁוּם הֶזֵּק רְאִיָּה:


לחצר השותפים – into a courtyard which has joint ownership of it, and all the more so, to the courtyard of his fellow because of the damage of sightlines.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:7:2)


**לָקַח בַּיִת בְּחָצֵר אַחֶרֶת.** הַסְּמוּכָה לְחָצֵר זוֹ שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ בָּהּ שֻׁתָּפוּת. לֹא יִפְתַּח לוֹ פֶּתַח לְחָצֵר זוֹ. שֶׁמַּרְבֶּה עֲלֵיהֶם דְּרִיסַת הָרֶגֶל מִדַּיָּרֵי אוֹתוֹ בַּיִת:


לקח בית מחצר אחרת – that is next to his this courtyard which has joint ownership, he should not open for himself a door to that courtyard, for there is a great deal of foot traffic from those who dwell in that house.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:7:3)


**בּוֹנֶה חֶדֶר לִפְנִים מִבֵּיתוֹ.** שֶׁאֵינוֹ מוֹסִיף כְּלוּם אֶלָּא חוֹלֵק בֵּיתוֹ לִשְׁנַיִם. דִּבְלָאו הָכִי יָכוֹל לְמַלְּאוֹת בֵּיתוֹ דִּיּוּרִין אִם יִרְצֶה:


בונה חדר לפנים מביתו – which does not add anything, but divides his home into t, for without this, he could fill his house with inhabitants/tenants if he wanted.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:7:4)


**פֶּתַח כְּנֶגֶד פֶּתַח.** דְּאָמַר קְרָא (במדבר כד) וַיִּשָּׂא בִּלְעָם אֶת עֵינָיו וַיַּרְא אֶת יִשְׂרָאֵל שׁוֹכֵן לִשְׁבָטָיו. מָה רָאָה, רָאָה שֶׁלֹּא הָיוּ פִּתְחֵיהֶן מְכֻוָּנִים זֶה כְּנֶגֶד זֶה:


פתח כנגד פתח – as it states in the Biblical verse (Numbers 24:2): “As Balaam looked up and saw Israel encamped tribe by tribe.” What did he saw? He saw that their openings [of the tents] were not directed one opposite the other (allowing people to see inside each other’s tent).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:7:5)


**קָטָן לֹא יַעֲשֶׂנּוּ גָדוֹל.** דְּאָמַר לֵיהּ בְּפִתְחָא זוּטָא מָצִינָא לְאִצְטַנּוּעֵי מִנָּךְ, בְּפִתְחָא רַבָּא לֹא מָצִינָא לְאִצְטַנּוּעֵי מִנָּךְ:


קטן לא יעשנו גדול – for he said to him: “with a small opening, I can be private (i.e., protect myself) from your gaze; with a large opening, I cannot be private (i.e., protect myself) from your gaze (see Talmud Bava Batra 24aa and 60a).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:7:6)


**אֶחָד לֹא יַעֲשֶׂנּוּ שְׁנָיִם.** דְּאָמַר לֵיהּ בְּחַד פִּתְחָא מָצִינָא לְאִצְטַנּוּעֵי, בִּתְרֵי פִּתְחֵי לֹא מָצִינָא לְאִצְטַנּוּעֵי:


אחד לא יעשנו שנים – for he said to him; with one opening I can be private (i.e., protect myself); with two, I cannot be private (i.e., protect myself).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:7:7)


**אֲבָל פּוֹתֵחַ הוּא לִרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים.** דְּאָמַר לֵיהּ, סוֹף סוֹף קָא בָּעֵית לְאִצְטַנּוּעֵי מִבְּנֵי רְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים:


אבל פותח הוא לרשות הרבים – for he said to him, that finally, you are able to be private from the members of the public domain.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:8:1)


**אֵין עוֹשִׂין חָלָל תַּחַת רְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים.** אֲפִלּוּ קָבִיל עֲלֵיהּ כָּל הֶזֵּקָא דְּאָתֵי מֵחֲמָתֵיהּ. שֶׁאֵין בְּנֵי אָדָם רוֹצִים [לִזּוֹק וְלֵירֵד לַדִּין] לָדוּן עַל עֵסֶק מָמוֹנָם:


אין עושין חלל תחת רשות הרבים – even if he accepted upon himself every blast/wind that comes from the consequences of this, for people do not want to cause damage and to go to court to judge on the matters of their money.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:8:2)


**רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר מַתִּיר.** וּבִלְבַד שֶׁיְּכַסֶּנּוּ בְּחֹזֶק כְּדֵי שֶׁתְּהֵא עֲגָלָה מְהַלֶּכֶת עָלָיו טְעוּנָה אֲבָנִים. וְלֹא חָיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא לְיָמִים יִתְקַלְקֵל הַכִּסּוּי. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר:


רבי אליעזר מתיר – as long as they cover it with force in order that a wagon carrying stones will travel upon it, and we should not worry lest sometimes the covering will be ruined. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Eliezer.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:8:3)


**זִיזִין.** רָהִיטִים קְטַנִּים:


זיזין – small floor beams.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:8:4)


**גְּזֻזְטְרָאוֹת.** קוֹרוֹת גְּדוֹלוֹת. שֶׁמָּא יִכָּשְׁלוּ בָּהֶן בְּנֵי רְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים:


גזוזטראות – large beams lest the members of the public domain stumble against them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:8:5)


**כּוֹנֵס לְתוֹךְ שֶׁלּוֹ.** שֶׁמַּכְנִיס בַּקַּרְקַע שֶׁלּוֹ כְּשִׁעוּר הוֹצָאַת הַזִּיזִין וּמוֹצִיא:


כונס לתוך שלו – brings into his land like the measure of the removal of the small floor beams and take them out.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 3:8:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:8:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/3:8:6)


**הֲרֵי זוֹ בְחֶזְקָתָהּ.** דְּטוֹעֲנִין לַלּוֹקֵחַ וְאָמְרִינַן שֶׁמָּא זֶה שֶׁמְּכָרָהּ לוֹ כָּנַס בְּתוֹךְ שֶׁלּוֹ:


הרי זו בחזקתה – we load them for the purchaser and we state that the person who sold it to him and brought them into his own [home].



## Chapter 4


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 4:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:1:1)


**הַמּוֹכֵר אֶת הַבַּיִת.** הַיָּצִיעַ. כְּמִין חֶדֶר שֶׁעוֹשִׂין סְבִיבוֹת כָּתְלֵי הַבַּיִת מִבַּחוּץ, כְּמוֹ שֶׁהָיָה בַּמִּקְדָּשׁ, דִּכְתִיב (מלכים א ו) וְהַיָּצִיעַ הַתַּחְתּוֹנָה. וְיֵשׁ שֶׁעוֹשִׂין אוֹתָהּ בָּעֳבִי הַכֹּתֶל. וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁפָּתוּחַ לַבַּיִת וְתַשְׁמִישׁוֹ בְּתוֹכוֹ, אֲפִלּוּ הָכִי אֵינוֹ מָכוּר. וְהוּא דַּהֲוֵי אַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת. אֲבָל אִי לֹא הָוֵי אַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת, לֹא חָשִׁיב, וְנִמְכָּר עִם הַבַּיִת:


המוכר את הבית. היציע – like a kind of room that they make around the walls of the house from the outside, like what was in the Temple, as it is written (I Kings 6:6): “The lowest story was [five cubits wide],” and there are those who make it in the thickness of the wall. But even though that it is open to the house and its usage is within, even so, it is not sold. And it is four cubits, but if it is not four cubits, it is not considered [separate] and it is sold with the house.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 4:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:1:2)


**וְלֹא אֶת הַחֶדֶר שֶׁלִּפְנִים.** מִן הַבַּיִת. וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהוּא פָּתוּחַ לַבַּיִת וּדְרִיסָתוֹ עָלָיו:


ולא את החדר שלפנים – from the house, and even though it is open to the house and it is tread upon.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 4:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:1:3)


**בִּזְמַן שֶׁיֶּשׁ לוֹ מַעֲקֶה גָבוֹהַּ עֲשָׂרָה.** חָשׁוּב בִּפְנֵי עַצְמוֹ וְלֹא בָּטֵל לְגַבֵּי בַּיִת:


בזמן שיש לו מעקה גבוה עשרה – it is considered by itself and not nullified in regard to the house.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 4:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:1:4)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר וְכוּ'.** וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


רבי יהודה אומר וכו' – The Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 4:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:2:1)


**בּוֹר.** חֲפִירָה בַּקַּרְקַע:


בור – a pit in the ground.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 4:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:2:2)


**דּוּת.** בִּנְיָן שֶׁל אֲבָנִים עַל גַּבֵּי קַרְקַע עָשׂוּי כְּעֵין בּוֹר:


דות – a building of stones on top of the ground made like a pit.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 4:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:2:3)


**אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁכָּתַב לוֹ עֻמְקָא וְרוּמָא.** לֹא קָנָה בּוֹר וָדוּת, הוֹאִיל וְתַשְׁמִישָׁן חָלוּק מִן הַבַּיִת, שֶׁאֵין עֲשׂוּיִין אֶלָּא לִשְׁאֹב מַיִם. עַד שֶׁיִּכְתֹּב לוֹ מֵאַרְעִית תְּהוֹמָא עַד רוּם רְקִיעָא:


אף על פי שכתב לו עומקא ורומא – He did not purchase a cistern/pit or a subterranean masoned storeroom since their usages are separate from the house, and they are not made other than to draw out water, until he writes: “from the lower portion/bottom of the depths until the heights of the sky.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 4:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:2:4)


**וְצָרִיךְ.** הַמּוֹכֵר:


וצריך – the seller


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 4:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:2:5)


**לִקַּח לוֹ דֶרֶךְ.** מִן הַלּוֹקֵחַ, לֵילֵךְ לְבוֹר וָדוּת. דְּמוֹכֵר בְּעַיִן יָפָה הוּא מוֹכֵר וְלֹא שִׁיֵּר לְעַצְמוֹ כְּלוּם:


to purchase for him a path/right-of-way, from the purchaser to walk to the pit and the building of stones of top of the ground made like a pit, because the seller sells with good will, and doesn’t leave anything to himself.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 4:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:2:6)


**וַחֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים אֵינוֹ צָרִיךְ.** דְּסַבְרֵי מוֹכֵר בְּעַיִן רָעָה הוּא מוֹכֵר, וּכְשֶׁמָּכַר הַבַּיִת שִׁיֵּר לְעַצְמוֹ דֶּרֶךְ שֶׁיֵּלֵךְ לַבּוֹר וְלַדּוּת:


וחכמים אומרים אינו צריך – for they hold that he sells ill-will/selfishness/envy and when he sold the house, he retained for himself a path that he can walk to the pit/cistern and the subterranean masoned storeroom.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 4:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:2:7)


**בִּזְמַן שֶׁאָמַר חוּץ מֵאֵלּוּ.** חוּץ מִבּוֹר וָדוּת, דִּתְנַאי שֶׁלֹּא לְצֹרֶךְ הוּא וּלְאַטְפוּיֵי דֶּרֶךְ קָאָתֵי:


בזמן שאמר חוץ מאלו – except for a pit and a subterranean masoned storeroom, for it is a condition that that it is not for any need for he comes to add a pathway.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 4:2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:2:8)


**מְכָרָן לְאַחֵר.** מָכַר הַבּוֹר וָדוּת לְאַחֵר, וְשִׁיֵּר הַבַּיִת לְעַצְמוֹ:


מכרן לאחר – he sold the pit and the subterranean masoned stoneroom to another, and left the house to himself.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 4:2:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:2:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:2:9)


**רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא אוֹמֵר אֵין הַלּוֹקֵחַ צָרִיךְ לִקַּח מִמֶּנּוּ דָרֶךְ.** דְּמוֹכֵר בְּעַיִן יָפָה מוֹכֵר, וּכְשֶׁמָּכַר לוֹ הַבּוֹר וְהַדּוּת, דֶּרֶךְ נַמִּי מָכַר לוֹ. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא:


רבי עקיבא אומר אין הלוקח צריך ליקח ממנו דרך – Because the seller sells with good will, and when he sold him the pit and the subterranean masoned storeroom, he also sold him a right-of-way/path. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Akiva.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 4:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:3:1)


**הַמּוֹכֵר אֶת הַבַּיִת.** סְתָם:


המוכר את הבית – undefined.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 4:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:3:2)


**מָכַר אֶת הַדֶּלֶת.** שֶׁכָּל תַּשְׁמִישֵׁי הַבַּיִת הַקְּבוּעִים בּוֹ בִּכְלָל הַבַּיִת הֵם:


מכר את הדלת – because all of the fixed implements of the house are included in the house.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 4:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:3:3)


**אֲבָל לֹא אֶת הַמַּפְתֵּחַ.** דְּמִידִי דְּמִטַּלְטֵל הוּא:


אבל לא את המפתח – because it is something movable.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 4:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:3:4)


**הַמַּכְתֶּשֶׁת.** הַקְּבוּעָה בַּקַּרְקַע:


המכתשת – that is fixed in the ground.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 4:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:3:5)


**הָאִצְטְרֻבָּל.** עִגּוּל שֶׁל עֵץ שֶׁעָלָיו מַעֲמִידִים הָרֵחַיִם. וְקָבוּעַ הוּא:


האיצטרובל – a circle of wood that they place the millstone on it and it is fixed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 4:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:3:6)


**הַקֶּלֶת.** אֲפַרְכֶּסֶת שֶׁעוֹשִׂים סָבִיב לָרֵחַיִם לְקַבֵּל הַקֶּמַח הַנִּטְחָן שֶׁלֹּא יִפֹּל לָאָרֶץ. וּמִטַּלְטֵל הוּא:


הקלת – the hopper/grain-receiver (on top of the millstone) that they make around the millstone to receive the ground flour that it will not fall to the ground and it is movable.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 4:3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:3:7)


**וְלֹא אֶת הַתַּנּוּר וְלֹא אֶת הַכִּירָיִם.** דְּמִטַּלְטְלִים הֵם. וְאִית סְפָרִים דְּגָרְסֵי, מָכַר תַּנּוּר מָכַר כִּירַיִם. וּמַיְרֵי בִּקְבוּעִים וּמְחֻבָּרִים בַּקַּרְקַע:


ולא את התנור ולא את הכירים – for they are movables. And there are books which have the textual reading:"מכר תנור, מכר כירים"/if he sold the oven, he sold the double-stove also and it is speaking of something permanent and attached to the ground.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 4:3:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:3:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:3:8)


**הֲרֵי כֻלָּם מְכוּרִים.** כָּל הָנֵי תַּשְׁמִישֵׁי דְּבֵיתָא. אֲבָל שְׁאָר מָאנֵי תַּשְׁמִישְׁתֵּיהּ אֵינָם מְכוּרִים, אֲפִלּוּ אָמַר וְכָל מַה שֶּׁבְּתוֹכָהּ, לֹא רִבָּה אֶלָּא תַּשְׁמִישִׁים הַמְיֻחָדִים לַבַּיִת כְּגוֹן מַפְתֵּחַ וָקֶלֶת וְכַיּוֹצֵא בָהֶן:


הרי כולם מכורים – all of these are implements of the house, but the rest of kinds of utensils are not sold, even if he (i.e., the seller) stated: “and everything that is inside it” – he did not include other than implements unique to the house like a key and a hopper/grain-receiver and things like that.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 4:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:4:1)


**מָכַר אֶת הַבָּתִּים.** הַפְּתוּחִים לֶחָצֵר:


מכר את הבתים – that are open to the courtyard.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 4:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:4:2)


**וּבוֹרוֹת שִׁיחִים וּמְעָרוֹת.** שֶׁבְּתוֹךְ הַבָּתִּים. וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּהַמּוֹכֵר בַּיִת לֹא מָכַר בּוֹר שִׁיחַ וּמְעָרָה, לְגַבֵּי חָצֵר מִיהָא בְּטֵלִים הֵן:


ובורות שיחין ומערות – that are within the houses and even though the person selling a house did not sell the cistern, the trench and the cave/vault, but regarding the courtyard, however, they cease to exist.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 4:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:4:3)


**בֵּין כָּךְ וּבֵין כָּךְ.** אֲפִלּוּ אָמַר כָּל מַה שֶּׁבְּתוֹכָהּ, לֹא מָכַר אֶת הַמֶּרְחָץ וְכוּ'. דְּאֵינָן בִּכְלָל חָצֵר:


בין כך ובין כך – even if he said, “everything that is within it,” he did not sell the bathhouse, etc. because they are not part of the courtyard.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 4:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:4:4)


**רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר אוֹמֵר וְכוּ'.** וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר:


רבי אליעזר אומר וכו' – But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Eliezer.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 4:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:5:1)


**מָכַר אֶת הַיָּם.** הָאֶבֶן שֶׁנּוֹתְנִים בְּתוֹכָהּ הַזֵּיתִים בִּשְׁעַת טְחִינָתָן:


מכר את הים – the stone that they place the olives into at the time of their grinding.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 4:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:5:2)


**הַמַּמָּל.** הָרֶכֶב הָעֶלְיוֹנָה שֶׁבָּהּ מְפָרְכִים הַזֵּיתִים:


ממל – The upper millstone where they crush the olives.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 4:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:5:3)


**הַבְּתוּלוֹת.** כְּלוּנְסוֹת שֶׁל אֶרֶז שֶׁמַּעֲמִידִין בָּהֶם קוֹרוֹת בֵּית הַבַּד:


הבתולות – cedar beams/poles supporting the traverse press beam (see Talmud Bava Batra 67b)of the olive press.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 4:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:5:4)


**עֲכִירִין.** נְסָרִים כְּבֵדִים שֶׁנּוֹתְנִים עַל הָאַמְתָּחוֹת שֶׁשָּׂמִים בָּהֶם הַזֵּיתִים הַכְּתוּשִׁים כְּדֵי לְכָבְשָׁן:


עבירין – heavy planed boards that they place on the אמתחות – which they place on the pounded olives in order to pickle them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 4:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:5:5)


**הַגַּלְגַּל.** שֶׁמְּגַלְגֵּל הָאֶבֶן וּמַכְבִּיד עַל הַזֵּיתִים הַכְּתוּשִׁים לְהוֹצִיא שַׁמְנָן:


הגלגל – when the stone revolves and puts heavy weight on the pounded olives to remove their oils.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 4:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:6:1)


**הַנְּסָרִים.** לוּחוֹת שֶׁמְּשִׂימִין עֲלֵיהֶם הַבְּגָדִים:


הנסרים – boards that we place clothing on them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 4:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:6:2)


**הַסַּפְסָלִים.** שֶׁיּוֹשְׁבִים עֲלֵיהֶם. וְיֵשׁ גּוֹרְסִים סְפָלִים, שֶׁנּוֹתְנִים בָּהֶם מַיִם לִרְחֹץ:


הספסלים – that we sit upon them. But there are those who have the reading "ספלים"/bowls or basins – that one puts water in them to wash.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 4:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:6:3)


**וִילָאוֹת.** מָסָךְ כְּנֶגֶד הַפֶּתַח. פֵּרוּשׁ אַחֵר, אֲלוּנְטִית שֶׁמִּסְתַּפְּגִין בָּהּ. וְיֵשׁ אוֹמְרִים סִנָּר שֶׁמְּכַסִּין בּוֹ הָעֶרְוָה כְּשֶׁיּוֹשְׁבִין בַּמֶּרְחָץ:


וילאות – curtain opposite the opening. Another interpretation: wrapping cloth that one uses to dry off. And there are those who say that it is a petticoat/breach-cloth in which we cover with the genitals when sitting in the bathhouse.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 4:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:6:4)


**מְגֻרוֹת שֶׁל מַיִם.** בְּרֵכוֹת הַשּׁוֹפְכוֹת מַיִם לַמֶּרְחָץ:


מגורות של מים – pools that pour water into the bathhouse.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 4:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:6:5)


**אוֹצָרוֹת שֶׁל עֵצִים.** שֶׁמְּחַמְּמִין בּוֹ הַמֶּרְחָץ:


אוצרות של עצים – that warm up the bathhouse.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 4:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:7:1)


**מָכַר בָּתִּים.** וְכָל שֶׁכֵּן חֲצֵרוֹת שֶׁהֵן עִקַּר הָעִיר:


מכר בתים – and all the more so, courtyards which are the essence of the city.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 4:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:7:2)


**בֵּית הַשְּׁלָחִין.** גִּנּוֹת וּפַרְדֵּסִין הַשַּׁיָּכִין לָעִיר:


בית השלחין – gardens and orchards that belong to the city.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 4:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:7:3)


**אֲבָל לֹא הַמִּטַּלְטְלִין.** מָאנֵי תַּשְׁמִישְׁתָּא, כְּגוֹן מַפְתֵּחַ וָקֶלֶת וְכַיּוֹצֵא בָּהֶן. וְכָל שֶׁכֵּן שֶׁלֹּא מָכַר חִטֵּי וּשְׂעָרֵי:


אבל לא המטלטלין – utensils that are used such as a key, a woman’s work basket and similar kinds of things, and all the more so, that he did not sell wheat and barley.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 4:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:7:4)


**הֲרֵי כֻלָּן מְכוּרִים.** אֲפִלּוּ עֲבָדִים וּבְהֵמוֹת דְּמִטַּלְטְלֵי דְּנַיְדֵי נִינְהוּ. וְכָל שֶׁכֵּן חִטֵּי וּשְׂעָרֵי דַּהֲווּ מִטַּלְטְלֵי דְּלֹא נַיְדֵי:


הרי כולן מכורים – even slaves and animals that are movables that are unsteady. And, all the more so, wheat and barley that are movables that are not unsteady.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 4:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:7:5)


**הַסַּנְטֵר.** הָעֶבֶד הַמְמֻנֶּה לִשְׁמֹר הָעִיר. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבָּן שִׁמְעוֹן בֶּן גַּמְלִיאֵל:


הסנטר – the slave appointed to guard the city. But the Halakha is not according to Rabban Shimon ben Gamaliel.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 4:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:8:1)


**הָאֲבָנִים שֶׁהֵם לְצָרְכָּהּ.** לַעֲשׂוֹת גָּדֵר:


האבנים שהם לצורכה – to make a fence.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 4:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:8:2)


**וְאֶת הַקָּנִים.** שֶׁקּוֹשְׁרִים בָּהֶם הַגְּפָנִים:


ואת הקנים – that we tie up the vines with them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 4:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:8:3)


**וְאֶת מְחִצַּת הַקָּנִים.** קָנִים הַרְבֵּה גְּדֵלִים בְּקֶלַח אֶחָד, כְּשֶׁהִיא פְּחוּתָה מִבֵּית רֹבַע אָז הִיא בְּטֵלָה לְגַבֵּי הַשָּׂדֶה:


ואת מחיצת הקנים – many canes grow on one stem/stalk when it is less than a piece of ground of the capacity of a one-fourth of a Kab of seed, therefore it is neutralized concerning the field.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 4:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:8:4)


**שׁוֹמֵרָה.** סֻכַּת שׁוֹמְרִים הַמְחֻבֶּרֶת לַקַּרְקַע בְּטִיט:


שומרה – a guard booth that is attached to the ground with plaster.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 4:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:8:5)


**וְאֶת הֶחָרוּב.** בְּבַחֲרוּתוֹ שֶׁעֲדַיִן לֹא הֻרְכָּב. דִּכְשֶׁמַּזְקִין מִתְגַּבֵּר וּמַרְכִּיבִין אוֹתוֹ וְיֵשׁ לוֹ שֵׁם בִּפְנֵי עַצְמוֹ וְלֹא בָּטֵיל לְגַבֵּי שָׂדֶה:


ואת החרוב – in its youth when they are not yet engrafted and when they cause damage when they grow strong and it is engrafted, and it has name of its own, and is not neutralized concerning the field.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 4:8:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:8:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:8:6)


**וְאֶת בְּתוּלַת הַשִּׁקְמָה.** בְּבַחֲרוּתָהּ קֹדֶם שֶׁחָתְכוּ עֲנָפֶיהָ. דִּכְשֶׁמַּזְקִין קוֹצְצִים הָעֲנָפִים וּגְדֵלִין הַרְבֵּה בִּמְקוֹמָן וְקָרוּי סַדַּן שִׁקְמָה:


ואת בתולת השקמה – in its youth before they cut its branches or when it causes damage, we cut the ranches that have grown a great deal in their place, and it is called the trunk of the sycamore tree.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 4:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:9:1)


**לֹא מָכַר אֶת מְחִצַּת הַקָּנִים וְכוּ'.** שֶׁכָּל אֵלֶּה חֲשׁוּבִים כְּשָׂדֶה בִּפְנֵי עַצְמָן:


לא מכר את מחיצת הקנים [וכו'] – for all of these are considered as a field [on its own].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 4:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:9:2)


**לֹא מָכַר אֶת הַבּוֹר.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁמָּכַר לוֹ הַשָּׂדֶה אֵין אֵלּוּ מְכוּרִין:


לא מכר את בור – even though he sold him the field, these are not sold.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 4:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:9:3)


**וְצָרִיךְ הַמּוֹכֵר לִקַּח לוֹ דֶרֶךְ מִן הַלּוֹקֵחַ.** לָבֹא לַבּוֹר וְלַגַּת. דְּמוֹכֵר בְּעַיִן יָפָה מוֹכֵר כִּדְפָרִישְׁנָא לְעֵיל:


צריך המוכר ליקח לו דרך מן הלוקח – to go to the cistern and to the wine-press, for he sells with good-will as I have explained above (see Tractate Bava Batra, Chapter 4, Mishnah 2).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 4:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:9:4)


**נָתַן אֶת כֻּלָּן.** וַאֲפִלּוּ לְמַאן דְּאָמַר מוֹכֵר בְּעַיִן רָעָה מוֹכֵר וְצָרִיךְ הַלּוֹקֵחַ לִקַּח לוֹ דֶּרֶךְ הֵיכָא דְּמָכַר לוֹ אֶחָד מִכָּל אֵלּוּ וְשִׁיֵּר הַשָּׂדֶה לְעַצְמוֹ, דַּוְקָא בְּמוֹכֵר אָמְרִינַן הָכִי, מִשּׁוּם דַּהֲוָה לֵיהּ לַלּוֹקֵחַ לְאַתְנוּיֵי וּלְפֵרוּשֵׁי, וְכֵיוָן דְּלֹא אַתְנֵי אִיהוּ הוּא דְּאַפְסִיד, אֲבָל גַּבֵּי נוֹתֵן שֶׁמְּקַבֵּל הַמַּתָּנָה מִתְבַּיֵּשׁ לוֹמַר לַנּוֹתֵן פָּרֵשׁ לִי מַה שֶּׁאַתָּה נוֹתֵן לִי, לֹא אָמְרִינַן מִדְּלֹא פֵּרֵשׁ אִיהוּ הוּא דְּאַפְסִיד, אֶלָּא אָמְרִינַן בְּעַיִן יָפָה נָתַן וְאֵין צָרִיךְ מְקַבֵּל הַמַּתָּנָה לִקַּח לוֹ דֶּרֶךְ. וְכֵן נַמִּי כָּל הָנֵי דְּאָמְרִינַן לְעֵיל לֹא מָכַר לְפִי שֶׁאֵינָן בִּכְלָל שָׂדֶה, וַאֲפִלּוּ הָנֵי דְּאֵינָן בִּכְלָל כָּל מַה שֶּׁבְּתוֹכָהּ כְּגוֹן חָרוּב וְסַדַּן הַשִּׁקְמָה וּבוֹר וָגַת, אִם נָתַן הַשָּׂדֶה מַתָּנָה, נָתַן אֶת כֻּלָּן, מִשּׁוּם דִּבְעַיִן יָפָה יוֹתֵר מִדַּאי הוּא נוֹתֵן:


נתן את כולם – and even to one who stated that he sells with ill-will, he sells it, and the purchaser must buy a right of way [when] he sold him one of these kind, and leaves the field to himself. Especially when he sells as we state here, because the purchaser can make conditions and explanations, for since he didn’t make a condition, he doesn’t lose out. But regarding a person who gives, when he receives the gift, he is embarrassed to state to the giver – “explain to me what you are giving me.” We don’t say that when he doesn’t explain, he is the one who loses, but we say is that he gave it to him with good will, and the recipient of the gift does not have to buy a right of way, and similarly, all of these things that we have stated above, he didn’t sell because they are not included in the field and even those things that are not included – everything that is within it, such as a carob or a fully grown sycamore tree and the cistern and the wine-press. If he gave [the field as a gift], he gave him everything, because with good well is too much of what he gives.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 4:9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:9:5)


**זָכוּ בַשָּׂדֶה זָכוּ בְכֻלָּן.** אֲפִלּוּ בּוֹר וָגַת וְשׁוֹבָךְ שֶׁבַּשָּׂדֶה. מִפְּנֵי שֶׁכָּל אֶחָד זוֹכֶה בְּשֶׁלּוֹ וּמִסְתַּלֵּק מֵחֲבֵרוֹ מִכֹּל וָכֹל:


זכו בשדה זכו בכולן – even a cistern and a wine-press and a dovecote that are in the field, because each one merits with its own and it is removed from his fellow completely.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 4:9:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:9:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:9:6)


**הֶחֱזִיק בְּכֻלָּם.** וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּחָרוּב וְשִׁקְמָה לֹא בְּטִילֵי לְגַבֵּי שָׂדֶה, הֲווּ כִּשְׁתֵּי שָׂדוֹת וְאֵין מֵצַר בֵּינֵיהֶן, דְּאִם הֶחֱזִיק בְּאַחַת מֵהֶן קָנָה חֲבֶרְתָּהּ:


החזיק בכולם – And even though that a carob [tree] and a sycamore [tree] are not neglected regarding the field, they are like two fields, and there is pathway between them and if he gained possession of one of them, he bought its neighbor.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 4:9:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:9:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:9:7)


**הִקְדִּישׁ אֶת כֻּלָּם.** דְּמַקְדִּישׁ בְּעַיִן יָפָה מַקְדִּישׁ:


הקדיש כולם – when he dedicates it [to the Temple], he dedicates it with good will.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 4:9:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:9:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/4:9:8)


**לֹא הִקְדִּישׁ אֶלָּא אֶת הֶחָרוּב.** מִכָּל הָנָךְ דְּאָמְרִינַן בְּמַתְנִיתִין גַּבֵּי מֶכֶר לֹא מָכַר, גַּבֵּי הֶקְדֵּשׁ נַמִּי לֹא הִקְדִּישׁ, דְּדַעַת מַקְדִּישׁ כְּדַעַת מוֹכֵר, לְבַד מֵחָרוּב הַמֻּרְכָּב וְסַדַּן הַשִּׁקְמָה, דְּאַף עַל גַּב דִּבְמֶכֶר לֹא מָכַר, בְּהֶקְדֵּשׁ קַדְּשֵׁי. וְטַעְמָא, הוֹאִיל וּמִשְּׂדֵה הֶקְדֵּשׁ קָא יָנְקֵי:


לא הקדיש אלא חרוב – from all of these that are mentioned in our Mishnah, regarding a sale, he did not sell, regarding devoting to the Temple, he did not devote, for the knowledge of the one who dedicates is like the knowledge of the sell, except for the grafted carob tree and the fully grown sycamore tree, and even though that the sale was not a sale, with a dedication it is sanctified, and the reason is that since from a dedicated field, it absorbs.



## Chapter 5


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:1:1)


**הַמּוֹכֵר אֶת הַסְּפִינָה.** סְתָם:


המוכר את הספינה – undefined.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:1:2)


**מָכַר אֶת הַתֹּרֶן.** עֵץ גָּבוֹהַּ שֶׁתּוֹלִין עָלָיו אֶת הַנֵּס. וְהַנֵּס הוּא כְּמִין וִילוֹן שֶׁתּוֹלִין בַּתֹּרֶן לְנֶגֶד הָרוּחַ:


מכר את התורן – (see Talmud Bava Batra 73a) a tall [piece of] wood upon which they suspend the banner/flag. And the banner is a kind of curtain that they suspend on the mast towards the wind.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:1:3)


**עוֹגִין.** בַּרְזֶל שֶׁקּוֹשְׁרִין אוֹתוֹ בַּחֶבֶל וְזוֹרְקִין אוֹתוֹ בְּעֹמֶק הַמַּיִם לְעַכֵּב וּלְעַגֵּן הַסְּפִינָה:


עוגין – iron that they tie with rope and throw it into the depths of the water to detain and to anchor the ship.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:1:4)


**מַנְהִיגִים.** אֵלּוּ הַמְּשׁוֹטוֹת שֶׁבָּהֶם מוֹלִיכִין הַסְּפִינָה לַמָּקוֹם שֶׁרוֹצִים:


מנהיגים – These are oars that through them they lead the ship to the place that they desire.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:1:5)


**מַרְצוּפִין.** אַמְתָּחוֹת שֶׁנּוֹתְנִים בָּהֶם הַסְּחוֹרָה:


מרצופין – large sacks that they place goods into.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:1:6)


**אַנְתִּיקִי.** הַפְּרַקְמַטְיָא שֶׁבְּתוֹךְ הַסְּפִינָה. וְכָל הָנֵי דְּחָשֵׁיב הָכָא דִּבְמֶכֶר לֹא מָכַר, אִם נָתַן וְהִקְדִּישׁ אֶת הַסְּפִינָה לֹא הֲווּ הָנָךְ בִּכְלָל הַמַּתָּנָה וְהַהֶקְדֵּשׁ. וְלֹא דָּמֵי לְבוֹר וְדוּת וְגַת דְּפִרְקִין דִּלְעֵיל דְּכֻלְּהוּ מְקַרְקְעֵי נִינְהוּ וּבָטְלֵי לְגַבֵּי שָׂדֶה לְעִנְיַן הֶקְדֵּשׁ וּמַתָּנָה, אֲבָל הָנָךְ לֹא בָּטְלֵי כְּלָל:


אנתיקי – [the funds and stores connected with the business – see Talmud Bava Batra 77b] the practical things that are within the ship, and all of these are considered here that are in the sale, he did not sell if he gave or dedicated the ship [to the Temple]. These are not included in the gift and the dedication to the Temple, and are not similar to a cistern or a subterranean masoned storeroom or a winepress [mentioned] in the chapter above (Chapter 4, Mishnah 2), for all of them are in land and are made void regarding a field regarding sanctification [to the Temple] and gifting. But these are not made void at all.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:1:7)


**פְּרָדוֹת.** בְּהֵמוֹת הַמּוֹשְׁכוֹת אֶת הַקָּרוֹן. וְהוּא שֶׁאֵין אֲדוּקִים בּוֹ בִּשְׁעַת הַמְּכִירָה. וְאִית דִּמְפָרְשֵׁי פְּרָדוֹת, עֵצִים הַנִּפְרָדִים מִן הָעֲגָלָה שֶׁמּוֹשְׁכִים אֶת הָעֲגָלָה בָּהֶם. וְהוּא שֶׁלֹּא יִהְיוּ קְשׁוּרִים בָּהּ בִּשְׁעַת הַמְּכִירָה:


פרדות – [mules], animals that pull the wagon, but they are not attached to it (i.e., the wagon) at the time of the sale. But there are those who explain פרדות as wood that is separate from the wagon that on pulls the wagon with them, but they are not attached to the it at the time of the sale.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:1:8)


**צֶמֶד.** הוּא הָעֹל שֶׁצּוֹמֵד וּמְחַבֵּר אֶת הַשְּׁוָרִים יַחַד:


צמד – it is the yoke that they attach and tie [the] bulls together with.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:1:9)


**אֵין הַדָּמִים רְאָיָה.** וְהָא דְּאָמַר דִּשְׁתוּת קָנָה וּמַחֲזִיר אוֹנָאָה יוֹתֵר מִשְּׁתוּת בָּטֵל מִקָּח, הָנֵי מִלֵּי בִּכְדֵי שֶׁהַדַּעַת טוֹעָה, דְּסָבוּר הַלּוֹקֵחַ שֶׁשָּׁוֶה כָּךְ, וְהָוֵי מִקַּח טָעוּת, אֲבָל בִּכְדֵי שֶׁאֵין הַדַּעַת טוֹעָה, כְּגוֹן דְּקָנָה צֶמֶד שֶׁשָּׁוֶה זוּז בְּמָאתַיִם, אָמְרִינַן בְּמַתָּנָה נְתָנָם לוֹ וְאֵין כָּאן בִּטּוּל מִקָּח. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


אין הדמים ראיה – for that which he said that one-sixth [overcharge] he purchased [the object], and he (i.e., the seller) returns the overcharge; more than one-sixth, the purchase is void; these words are incidental that the mind errs as the purchaser thinks that it is worth such. And this is an errant purchase, but in order that the mind doesn’t error, such as the example that he purchased a yoke that was worth a Zuz for two hundred, we say that it was given to him as a gift, and there is no voiding of the transaction here. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:2:1)


**לֹא מָכַר אֶת כֵּלָיו.** בְּכֵלִים הָעֲשׂוּיִים לִרְכִיבָה כְּגוֹן אֻכָּף וּמִרְדַּעַת, כֻּלֵּי עָלְמָא לֹא פְּלִיגֵי דְּקָנָה אֲפִלּוּ אֵינָם עָלָיו בִּשְׁעַת מְכִירָה. כִּי פְּלִיגֵי בְּכֵלִים שֶׁל מַשְּׂאוֹי, כְּגוֹן שַׂק וְדִיסְקְיָא, תַּנָּא קַמָּא סָבַר לֹא מָכַר הַכֵּלִים שֶׁל מַשְּׂאוֹי שֶׁעָלָיו, וְנַחוּם הַמָּדִי סָבַר מָכַר הַכֵּלִים שֶׁל מַשְּׂאוֹי שֶׁעָלָיו. וַהֲלָכָה כַּתַּנָּא קַמָּא, וְלֹא קָנָה הַכֵּלִים שֶׁל מַשְּׂאוֹי וַאֲפִלּוּ הָיוּ עָלָיו בִּשְׁעַת מְכִירָה:


לא מכר אל כליו – the utensils that are made for riding such as the saddle and pack-saddle, no one would argue that he purchased it, even if they were not upon it (i.e., the animal) at the time of the sale. What they do dispute about are the utensils of burden, such as the sack and the bag with two pouches (see Talmud Bava Batra 78a). The first Tanna/teacher [of the Mishnah] holds that he did not sell the utensils of burden that are upon it (i.e., the animal). But Nahum HaMadi holds that he sold the utensils of burden that are upon it. But the Halakha is according to the first Tanna/teacher, and he did not acquire the utensils of burden, and even if they were upon it (i.e., the animal) at the time of the sale.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:2:2)


**חֲמוֹרְךָ זֶה.** מַשְׁמַע כְּמוֹת שֶׁהוּא עִם כֵּלָיו:


חמורך זה – implying as it is with its utensils.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:2:3)


**חֲמוֹרְךָ הוּא.** הָוֵי כְּשׁוֹאֵל לוֹ חֲמוֹרְךָ הוּא, מְכֹר אוֹתוֹ לִי. וְהָוֵי כְּמוֹכֵר חֲמוֹר סְתָם, וְאֵין כֵּלָיו מְכוּרִים וַאֲפִלּוּ הֵן עָלָיו בִּשְׁעַת מְכִירָה. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


חמורך [הוא] – it is like he is asking him: “Is this your donkey? Sell it to me.” It is like he is selling a donkey, undefined, and the utensils are not sold, even if they are upon it at the time of the sale. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:3:1)


**הַמּוֹכֵר אֶת הַחֲמוֹר מָכַר אֶת הַסְּיָח.** בְּנוֹ שֶׁל חֲמוֹר. וּבַגְּמָרָא מוֹקֵי לָהּ כְּגוֹן שֶׁאָמַר לוֹ חֲמוֹר מֵנִיקָה אֲנִי מוֹכֵר לְךָ. דַּחֲלֵב הָאָתוֹן לְמַאי מַהֲנֵי, אֶלָּא וַדַּאי הִיא וּבְנָהּ קָאָמַר לֵיהּ. וְסֵיפָא דְּאָמַר פָּרָה מֵנִיקָה אֲנִי מוֹכֵר לְךָ לֹא קָנָה אֶת בְּנָהּ, דְּפָרָה לַחֲלָבָהּ עוֹמֶדֶת, וּפָרָה וַחֲלָבָהּ קָאָמַר לֵיהּ:


המוכר את החמור מכר את הסייח – the offspring of the donkey and in the Gemara (Talmud Bava Batra 78b) it establishes that he (i.e., the seller) said to him (i.e., the purchaser): This nursing donkey I am selling [to you],” for the milk of the she-ass, of what benefit Is it? But absolutely, she and her offspring are spoken of to him. And the concluding part [of our Mishnah] where he stated: “I am selling to you a nursing cow.” He did not acquire her offspring, for a cow exists for its milk, and he mentioned to him: “a cow for its milk.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:3:2)


**מָכַר אַשְׁפָּה.** מָקוֹם גָּבוֹהַּ שְׁלֹשָׁה טְפָחִים אוֹ יוֹתֵר, אוֹ עָמֹק שְׁלֹשָׁה טְפָחִים אוֹ יוֹתֵר, שֶׁרָגִיל לְהָנִיחַ שָׁם זֶבֶל בְּהֵמוֹתָיו:


מכר אשפה – a tall place of three handbreadths or more, or three [handbreadths] deep or more, for it is normal to place there the manure of his cattle.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:3:3)


**מָכַר בּוֹר מָכַר מֵימָיו.** מַתְנִיתִין יְחִידָאָה הִיא, וּפְלִיגֵי רַבָּנָן עֲלָהּ וְאָמְרֵי מָכַר בּוֹר לֹא מָכַר מֵימָיו. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


מכר בור מכר מימיו – Our Mishnah is the opinion of a single authority, but the Rabbis dispute it and state that if one sold a cistern, one did not sell its water, and the Halakha is according to the Sages.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:3:4)


**הַלּוֹקֵחַ פֵּרוֹת שׁוֹבָךְ.** מַה שֶּׁיָּלְדוּ הַיּוֹנִים כָּל הַשָּׁנָה:


הלוקח פירות שובך – what the doves gave birth to all year.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:3:5)


**מַפְרִיחַ.** הַלּוֹקֵחַ:


מפריח – the purchaser [must let the first brood fly away].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:3:6)


**בְּרֵכָה רִאשׁוֹנָה.** וְיַנִּיחֶנָּה לַמּוֹכֵר. וְכָל שְׁנֵי וְלָדוֹת קְרוּיִין בְּרֵכָה. וּמַנִּיחָן עִם אִמָּם כְּדֵי לִהְיוֹת לְצַוְתָּא לְאִמָּם שֶׁלֹּא יִבְרְחוּ:


בריכה ראשונה – and leave it for the seller and every two offspring are called a בריכה/brood and he leaves them with its mother in order to be in the company of their mother so that they don’t fly away.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:3:7)


**פֵּרוֹת כַּוֶּרֶת.** דְּבוֹרִים הַנּוֹלָדִים מִן הַכַּוֶּרֶת זֹאת הַשָּׁנָה. נוֹטֵל הַלּוֹקֵחַ שְׁלֹשָׁה נְחִילִים, שְׁלֹשָׁה חֲבוּרוֹת מִוְּלָדוֹת הַדְּבוֹרִים הָרִאשׁוֹנִים הַיּוֹצְאִים מִן הַכַּוֶּרֶת, וּמַכְנִיסָן לְתוֹךְ הַכַּוֶּרֶת שֶׁלּוֹ. שֶׁהָרִאשׁוֹנִים הֵם חֲשׁוּבִים, וְאַחֲרוֹן אַחֲרוֹן גָּרוּעַ. וּמִשָּׁם וָאֵילַךְ מְסָרֵס, כְּלוֹמַר נוֹטֵל אַחַת וּמַנִּיחַ אַחַת לַמּוֹכֵר, כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּגְדְּלוּ וְיִתְחַבְּרוּ עִם הָאָבוֹת כְּדֵי שֶׁתִּתְיַשֵּׁב הַכַּוֶּרֶת. וְכֵן לְעוֹלָם:


פירות כוורת – bees that are born from this beehive this year. The purchaser takes three swarms, three groups from the bees born that first go out from the beehive and brings them into his own beehive. The first ones are important/valuable, whereas the last of the last is the worst. From there, he (i.e., the owner) makes the bees impotent, meaning to say, he takes one, and leaves one for the seller in order that they can grow and become attached with the parents so that the beehive will be refreshed and similarly forever.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:3:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:3:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:3:8)


**חַלּוֹת דְּבַשׁ.** הַדְּבַשׁ עִם הַשַּׁעֲוָה יַחַד מְעֹרָבִים עָשׂוּי חַלּוֹת חַלּוֹת:


חלות דבש – the honey with the wax together are combined to make honeycombs.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:3:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:3:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:3:9)


**מַנִּיחַ שְׁתֵּי חַלּוֹת.** שֶׁמִּתְפַּרְנְסִים הַדְּבוֹרִים מֵהֶם כָּל יְמוֹת הַגְּשָׁמִים:


מניח שתי חלות – that the bees are supported from them all the rainy days.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:3:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:3:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:3:10)


**גְּרוֹפִיּוֹת.** עֲנָפִים. וְחוֹזֵר וְצוֹמֵחַ:


גרופיות – boughs, and they grow once again.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:4:1)


**הַקּוֹנֶה שְׁנֵי אִילָנוֹת.** סְתָמָא, לֹא קָנָה קַרְקַע כְּלָל סְבִיבוֹתָיו:


הקונה שני אילנות – undefined, he did not purchase the land surrounding it at all.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:4:2)


**הִגְדִּילוּ.** הִרְחִיבוּ הָעֲנָפִים:


הגדילו – the boughs became wider.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:4:3)


**לֹא יְשַׁפֶּה.** לֹא יִכְרֹת אוֹתָם בַּעַל הַקַּרְקַע אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהַצֵּל שֶׁלָּהֶם מַזִּיק לַקַּרְקַע שֶׁלּוֹ, דְּכֵיוָן דְּאֵין לוֹ קַרְקַע לְבַעַל הָאִילָנוֹת הֲרֵי בַּעַל הַקַּרְקַע שִׁעְבֵּד לוֹ קַרְקָעוֹ לְכָל צָרְכָּן כָּל זְמַן שֶׁהֵם קַיָּמִים:


לא ישפה – the owner of the land should not cut them, even though their shade is destroying his land, for since the owner of the trees has no land, the owner of the land mortgaged to him (i.e., the owner of the trees) for all their needs all the while that they are existing.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:4:4)


**גֶּזַע.** כָּל שֶׁהוּא לְמַעְלָה מִן הָאָרֶץ שֶׁרוֹאֶה פְּנֵי חַמָּה:


גזע – whatever is above the ground and sees the face of the sun.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:4:5)


**שָׁרָשִׁין.** כָּל שֶׁהֵן לְמַטָּה מִן הָאָרֶץ:


שרשין – whatever is below the ground [level].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:4:6)


**שֶׁלּוֹ.** שֶׁל בַּעַל הָאִילָן. וְלֹא שֶׁיַּנִּיחֶנּוּ שָׁם וְיִגְדַּל, דְּחָיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא תַּגְבִּיהַּ הַקַּרְקַע עַד שֶׁיִּתְכַּסֶּה הָאִילָן הַיּוֹצֵא מִן הַגֶּזַע מִקְצָתוֹ בַּקַּרְקַע וְיֵרָאוּ כִּשְׁלֹשָׁה אִילָנוֹת, וְיֹאמַר לוֹ הַלּוֹקֵחַ שְׁלֹשָׁה אִילָנוֹת מָכַרְתָּ לִי וְיֵשׁ לִי קַרְקַע, אֶלָּא יָקֹץ אוֹתוֹ וְיִשְׂרְפֶנּוּ:


שלו – of the owner of the tree, and he should leave it there and let it grow, for we suspect lest the ground rise until it covers the tree coming out of the stump, part of which is in the ground, and they will see something like three trees and the purchase will say: “You sold me three trees and I have land” but he should cut it and burn it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:4:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:4:7)


**וְאִם מֵתוּ.** שֶׁיָּבַשׁ הָאִילָן:


ואם מתו – the tree dried up.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:4:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:4:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:4:8)


**אֵין לוֹ קַרְקַע.** שֶׁיּוּכַל לִטַּע אַחֵר בִּמְקוֹמוֹ:


אין לו קרקע – where he is able to plant another [tree] in its place.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:4:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:4:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:4:9)


**קָנָה קַרְקַע.** דַּחֲשִׁיבֵי שְׂדֵה הָאִילָן, וְקָנָה בַּקַּרְקַע שֶׁיֵּשׁ בֵּין אִילָן לְאִילָן, וְתַחְתֵּיהֶן וְחוּצָה לָהֶן כִּמְלֹא אוֹרֶה וְסַלּוֹ, כְּדֵי שֶׁיָּכוֹל לַעֲמֹד מְלַקֵּט הַפֵּרוֹת עִם הַסַּל שֶׁלּוֹ. וְהָנֵי מִלֵּי, כְּשֶׁיֵּשׁ בֵּין אִילָן לְאִילָן לֹא פָּחוֹת מֵאַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת וְלֹא יוֹתֵר עַל שֵׁשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה אַמָּה, אָז קָנָה בַּקַּרְקַע שֶׁבֵּין אִילָן לְאִילָן וְתַחְתֵּיהֶן וְחוּצָה לָהֶן כִּדְאָמְרָן. אֲבָל אִם יֵשׁ בֵּין אִילָן לְאִילָן פָּחוֹת מֵאַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת אוֹ יוֹתֵר מִשֵּׁשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה אַמָּה, לֹא קָנָה קַרְקַע:


קנה קרקע – for they are considered to be an orchard and he bought the land that is between one tree and another tree. But under them and outside of them is the fulness of fig collector and his basket, in order that he can stand, he can harvest his fruits with his basket. And these words [apply] when there is between one tree and another tree no less than four cubits, but no more than sixteen cubits. Then, he purchased the land between each tree and what is under them. And outside of it, as we have stated, but if there is less than four cubits between each tree, or more than sixteen cubits, he did not purchase the land.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:5:1)
