**לֹא מָכַר אֶת הָרַגְלָיִם.** בַּתּוֹסֶפְתָּא תַּנְיָא, בַּמֶּה דְּבָרִים אֲמוּרִים, בִּמְקוֹם שֶׁלֹּא נָהֲגוּ, אֲבָל בִּמְקוֹם שֶׁנָּהֲגוּ הַכֹּל כְּמִנְהַג הַמְּדִינָה:


לא מכר את הרגלים – In the Tosefta (Tosefta Bava Metzia, Chapter 4, Halakha 8; Lieberman edition and Tosefta Kifshuta, Tractate Bava Batra, page 376, note 29), it is taught: What are we speaking about? In a place where they did not have the practice, but in a place where they did have the practice, everything is according to the customs of the country.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:5:2)


**מָכַר אֶת הַקָּנֶה.** אֶת הָרֵאָה. וְנִקְרֵאת עַל שֵׁם הַקָּנֶה שֶׁלָּהּ:


מכר את הקנה – the lungs, and it is called by this name because of its windpipe.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:6:1)


**אַרְבַּע מִדּוֹת.** דִּינִים חֲלוּקִים זֶה מִזֶּה:


ד' מידות – laws divided one from the other.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:6:2)


**מָכַר לוֹ חִטִּים יָפוֹת.** הִתְנָה עִמּוֹ שֶׁיִּתֵּן לוֹ חִטִּין יָפוֹת וְנִמְצְאוּ רָעוֹת, הָוֵי כְּאוֹנָאָה, וּלְכָךְ יָכוֹל לַחֲזֹר בּוֹ, מִי שֶׁנִּתְאַנָּה בִּלְבַד, דְּהוּא לוֹקֵחַ, אֲבָל מוֹכֵר אֵינוֹ יָכוֹל לַחֲזֹר בּוֹ וַאֲפִלּוּ הוּקְרוּ הַחִטִּין הַרְבֵּה:


מכר לו חטים יפות – he made a stipulation with him that he should give him nice wheat and they were found to be bad. It is like overreaching/אונאה ; therefore, he (i.e., the buyer) can retract – whomever feels imposed upon/one’s self overreached alone, which is the purchaser, but the seller is not able to retract, and even if the wheat increased greatly in value.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:6:3)


**אֵין אֶחָד מֵהֶן יָכוֹל לַחֲזֹר בּוֹ.** וַאֲפִלּוּ הוּקְרוּ אוֹ הוּזְלוּ. וְלֹא מָצֵי לוֹקֵחַ לְמֵימַר אֲנִי לְיָפוֹת נִתְכַּוַּנְתִּי וְהַאי דַּאֲמֵינָא רָעוֹת מִשּׁוּם דְּרַע רַע יֹאמַר הַקּוֹנֶה. וְכֵן אִפְּכָא לֹא מָצֵי מוֹכֵר לְמֵימַר אֲנִי לְרָעוֹת נִתְכַּוַּנְתִּי וְהַאי דַּאֲמֵינָא יָפוֹת דְּדֶרֶךְ הַמּוֹכֵר לוֹמַר לָרַע טוֹב:


אין אחד מהן יכול לחזור בו – even if they increased in value or became cheaper, for the purchaser is not able to say: “I intended for the beautiful ones, and this one who might say they are bad, because the purchaser tells him: “what is bad is bad.” And similarly, the opposite, that the seller is not able to state that I intended for the bad ones. And this one that I might say are good, it is the manner of the seller to state the bad ones become good ones.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:6:4)


**שְׁחַמְתִּית.** אֲדֻמָּה. וְכָל חוּם (בראשית ל) מְתַרְגְּמִינַן שָׁחוּם:


שחמתית – reddish (Genesis 30:35): “and all the dark-colored [sheep], which we translate in Aramaic as שחום/dark, black (or brown).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:6:5)


**וְנִמְצֵאת לְבָנָה.** וְאִיכָּא דְּנִיחָא לֵיהּ בְּהַאי וְאִיכָּא דְּנִיחָא לֵיהּ בְּהַאי. וְכָל כְּהַאי גַּוְנָא הָוֵי מִקַּח טָעוּת לִשְׁנֵיהֶם וּשְׁנֵיהֶם יְכוֹלִים לַחֲזֹר. דְּאִלּוּ בְּיָפוֹת וְנִמְצְאוּ רָעוֹת כֻּלֵּי עָלְמָא נִיחָא לְהוּ בְּיָפוֹת:


ונמצאת לבנה – there are those for whom it is pleasant with this, and there are those for whom it is pleasant with that. But in this manner, it is an errant transaction for both of them, and both of them can retract, for if it was supposed to “nice ones,” and it turned out to be “bad ones,’ everyone enjoys “good ones.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:6:6)


**יַיִן וְנִמְצָא חֹמֶץ.** אִיכָּא דְּנִיחָא לֵיהּ בְּחַמְרָא וְאִיכָּא דְּנִיחָא לֵיהּ בְּחַלָּא:


יין ונמצא חומץ – there are those who are pleased with wine, and there are those who are pleased with vinegar.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:7:1)


**מָשַׁךְ.** מֵרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים בְּסִמְטָא אוֹ בְּחָצֵר שֶׁל שְׁנֵיהֶם, קָנָה. אֲבָל מְשִׁיכָה בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים לֹא מַהֲנְיָא:


משך – from the public domain in a recess adjoining an open place to which merchants retire to transact business/market-stand under a colonnade or in a courtyard of both of them, he has acquired it. But taking possession by drawing towards one’s self the object to be acquired in the public domain has no effect.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:7:2)


**אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא מָדַד.** וּכְגוֹן שֶׁפָּסַק לוֹ מִתְּחִלָּה הַדָּמִים בְּכָךְ וְכָךְ יִתֵּן לוֹ הַמִּדָּה. אֲבָל לֹא פָּסַק לוֹ הַדָּמִים, אֲפִלּוּ מָדַד וּמָשַׁךְ לֹא קָנָה, דְּלֹא סַמְכָא דַּעְתַּיְהוּ, שֶׁהַמּוֹכֵר יָכוֹל לְהַעֲלוֹת הַדָּמִים כְּמוֹ שֶׁרוֹצֶה, וְהַקּוֹנֶה אוֹמֵר אֵינִי קוֹנֶה אֶלָּא בְּזוֹל:


אע"פ שלא מדד – and such as he case where he fixed a price from the outset for such and then he gives him the measurement, but if he did not fix the price [even though] he measured and drew it towards himself, he did not acquire it, because he did not rely upon the fact that the seller is able to raise the price as he wants and the buyer says: “I won’t purchase it other than at a cheap price.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:7:3)


**מָדַד.** הַמּוֹכֵר לַלּוֹקֵחַ בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים, אֲפִלּוּ בְּכֵלָיו שֶׁל לוֹקֵחַ, לֹא קָנָה, שֶׁאֵין כֵּלָיו שֶׁל אָדָם קוֹנִים לוֹ בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים. וְאִם לוֹקֵחַ עַצְמוֹ מָדַד, אֲפִלּוּ בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים קָנָה בְּהַגְבָּהָה:


מדד – the seller to the purchaser in the public domain, even in the utensils of the purchaser, he did not acquire it, for the utensils of a person do not purchase things for him in the public domain, but if the purchaser himself measured it, even in the public domain, he acquired it through lifting it up.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:7:4)


**אִם הָיָה פִקֵּחַ.** הַלּוֹקֵחַ:


אם היה פקח – the purchaser.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:7:5)


**שׂוֹכֵר אֶת מְקוֹמוֹ.** אִם הוּא בִּרְשׁוּת בְּעָלִים. וּמְקוֹמוֹ קוֹנֶה לוֹ. וּבְמַשָּׂאוֹת גְּדוֹלוֹת אַיְרֵי, דְּאֵין דַּרְכָּן לְהַגְבִּיהַּ, לְכָךְ קָנִי בִמְשִׁיכָה:


שוכר את מקומו – if he is in the domain of the owners, and hs place acquires for him. And we are speaking about large burdens where it was not the manner to lift it up, therefore, he acquires it through pulling it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:7:6)


**עַד שֶׁיְּטַלְטְלֶנּוּ.** הַיְנוּ הַגְבָּהָה. וְאוֹרְחָא דְּמִלְּתָא נָקַט, דְּדֶרֶךְ מַגְבִּיהַּ לְטַלְטֵל מִמָּקוֹם לְמָקוֹם:


עד שיטלטלנו – that is through lifting, and it (i.e, the Mishnah) took the way of things – that the way of lifting to carry from one place to another place.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:7:7)


**וְאִם הָיָה מְחֻבָּר לַקַּרְקַע.** בַּגְּמָרָא מוֹקֵי לַהּ כְּגוֹן שֶׁאָמַר לוֹ מוֹכֵר לַלּוֹקֵחַ לֵךְ וְיַפֵּה לִי קַרְקַע כָּל שֶׁהוּא וּקְנֵי כָּל מַה שֶּׁעָלֶיהָ, דְּכֵיוָן שֶׁשְּׂכָרוֹ לַעֲשׂוֹת מְלָאכָה בַּקַּרְקַע שֶׁלּוֹ וְעָשָׂה בּוֹ מְלָאכָה כָּל שֶׁהוּא, קָנָה שְׂכִירוּתוֹ, וְקָנָה נַמִּי אוֹתוֹ דָּבָר שֶׁרוֹצֶה לְהַקְנוֹת לוֹ עִם הַשְּׂכִירוּת, לְפִיכָךְ אִם תָּלַשׁ כָּל שֶׁהוּא קָנָה:


אם היה מחובר לקרקע – In the Gemara (Tractate Bava Batra 87a and see Rashi’s explanation and the reference to Tractate Bava Batra 54a) it is established it as in the case where the seller says to the purchaser: “go and take possession of a small piece of the land and you will acquire everything that is upon it,” for since he had been hired to do work on his (i.e., the owner’s) land, and worked on it a little bit, his being hired acquired it for him, and he acquired also that thing which he had wanted to acquire for himself with his being hired. Therefore, if he tore out a little bit, he acquired/bought it.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:8:1)


**עַד שֶׁלֹּא נִתְמַלְּאָה הַמִּדָּה לַמּוֹכֵר.** בְּמִדָּה שֶׁאֵינָהּ שֶׁל שְׁנֵיהֶם מַיְרֵי, וְהַמַּשְׁאִיל הִשְׁאִילָהּ לִשְׁנֵיהֶם, וַהֲרֵי הִיא שְׁאוּלָה לַמּוֹכֵר עַד שֶׁיְּמַלְּאֶנָּה, וּמִשָּׁם וָאֵילַךְ הִיא שְׁאוּלָה לַלּוֹקֵחַ. לְפִיכָךְ עַד שֶׁלֹּא נִתְמַלְּאָה הַמִּדָּה, שֶׁהַכְּלִי שָׁאוּל לַמּוֹכֵר, לֹא קָנָה לוֹקֵחַ, וּמִשֶּׁנִּתְמַלְּאָה הַמִּדָּה, שֶׁהַכְּלִי שָׁאוּל לַלּוֹקֵחַ, קָנָה לוֹקֵחַ, שֶׁקָּנוּ לוֹ כֵּלָיו. וּבְסִמְטָא מַיְרֵי אוֹ בִּרְשׁוּת הַלּוֹקֵחַ:


עד של נתמלאה המדה – it (i.e., the Mishnah) is speaking about the measure [of a middleman] that is not either of theirs, and the lender lent to both of them, it is borrowed by the seller; therefore, before the measure was filled up, the utensil is lent to the seller, and the burying did not acquire it. Once the measure was filled up, the utensil is lent to the buyer; the buyer has acquired it, for he has acquired it. And we are speaking of a market-stand underneath a colonnade or in the domain of the purchaser.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:8:2)


**הָיָה סַרְסוּר בֵּינֵיהֶם וְנִשְׁבְּרָה הֶחָבִית.** וְהָיְתָה הֶחָבִית שֶׁלּוֹ:


היה סרסור ביניהם ונשברה החבית – the utensil was his (i.e., the agent’s/middleman’s).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:8:3)


**נִשְׁבְּרָה לַסַּרְסוּר.** וְלֹא אָמְרִינַן שְׁלוּחוֹ שֶׁל לוֹקֵחַ הוּא וְנִשְׁבְּרָה לַלּוֹקֵחַ:


נשברה לסרסור – and we don’t say that he is the agent of the purchaser and it was broken by the purchaser.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:8:4)


**וְחַיָּב לְהַטִּיף לוֹ שָׁלֹשׁ טִפִּין.** הַמּוֹכֵר לַלּוֹקֵחַ, מִן הַנִּדְבָּק בְּדָפְנֵי הַמִּדָּה לְאַחַר שֶׁעֵרָה הַיַּיִן וְהַשֶּׁמֶן:


וחייב להטיף לו ג' טפין – the seller to the purchaser from the what cleaved to the walls of the measure after he had emptied out the wine or the oil.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:8:5)


**הִרְכִּינָהּ וּמִצָּה.** הִטָּה אֶת הַמִּדָּה עַל צִדָּהּ לְאַחַר שֶׁעֵרָה הַשֶּׁמֶן וְהַיַּיִן וְהִטִּיף שָׁלֹשׁ טִפִּין, וְנִתְאַסֵּף מִצַּת הַמִּדָּה לְשׁוּלֵי הַכְּלִי אֶל מָקוֹם אֶחָד:


הרכינה ומיצת – he turned it the measure over on its side after he emptied the oil or the wine and let it drip three drops and what was drained of the measure was gathered at the rim of the utensil to one place, and they belong to the seller, for the purchaser had despaired of it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:8:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:8:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:8:6)


**הֲרֵי הוּא שֶׁל מוֹכֵר.** שֶׁהֲרֵי נִתְיָאֵשׁ הַלּוֹקֵחַ מִמֶּנּוּ:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:8:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:8:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:8:7)


**וְהַחֶנְוָנִי אֵינוֹ חַיָּב לְהַטִּיף שָׁלֹשׁ טִפִּין.** לְפִי שֶׁהוּא טָרוּד, שֶׁמּוֹכֵר כָּל שָׁעָה:


והחנוני אינו חייב להטיף ג' טפין – because he is preoccupied that he is selling every hour.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:8:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:8:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:8:8)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר.** לֹא אָמְרוּ שֶׁחֶנְוָנִי פָּטוּר מִלְּהַטִּיף שָׁלֹשׁ טִפִּין, אֶלָּא עֶרֶב שַׁבָּת עִם חֲשֵׁכָה. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


ר' יהודה אומר – they did not say that the storekeeper is exempt from letting three drops drip out, but rather, it was the Eve of the Sabbath near dusk. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:9:1)


**הַשּׁוֹלֵחַ בְּנוֹ.** קָטָן אֵצֶל הַחֶנְוָנִי וּבְיָדוֹ פֻּנְדְּיוֹן שֶׁהֵם שְׁנֵי אִסָּרִין, לְהָבִיא לוֹ בְּאִסָּר שֶׁמֶן וְאִסָּר אֶחָד יִתֵּן לוֹ, וְכֵן עָשָׂה, וְשָׁבַר הַתִּינוֹק אֶת הַצְּלוֹחִית וְנִשְׁפַּךְ הַשֶּׁמֶן וְאִבֵּד אֶת הָאִסָּר שֶׁנָּתַן לוֹ הַחֶנְוָנִי: **חֶנְוָנִי חַיָּב.** בַּשֶּׁמֶן וּבַצְּלוֹחִית וּבָאִסָּר. דְּלֹא שָׁלַח הָאָב אֶת בְּנוֹ הַקָּטָן אֶל הַחֶנְוָנִי אֶלָּא לְאוֹדוּעֵי לֵיהּ שֶׁהוּא צָרִיךְ לַשֶּׁמֶן, לֹא שֶׁיִּשְׁלַח הַשֶּׁמֶן עַל יָדוֹ. וּבַגְּמָרָא פָּרֵיךְ, אַמַּאי חַיָּב הַחֶנְוָנִי עַל הַצְּלוֹחִית, אֲבֵדָה מִדַּעַת הִיא. שֶׁהֲרֵי הוּא עַצְמוֹ שְׁלָחָהּ בְּיַד בְּנוֹ. וּמְשָׁנֵי, כְּגוֹן שֶׁנְּטָלָהּ חֶנְוָנִי לָמֹד בָּהּ שֶׁמֶן לַאֲחֵרִים, הִלְכָּךְ נַעֲשֶׂה גַּזְלָן עֲלֵיהּ וְקָמָה לֵיהּ בִּרְשׁוּתֵיהּ עַד דִּמְהַדֵּר לָהּ לְמָרָהּ, וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּאַהַדְרָהּ לַתִּינוֹק לֹא מִפְטַר מִנַּהּ:


השולח בנו – minor [child] with the storekeeper and in his hand was a Duponium/a Roman coin equal to two Asses which are two Issarim to bring to him an Issar of oil. And he will give him another Issar. And this is what he did. But the young child broke the flask and the oil spilled and he lost the Issar that the storekeeper gave him. The storekeeper is liable for oil, and for the flask and for the Issar, for the father did not send his minor-age child to the storekeeper other than to inform him that he needs oil, not that he should send the oil through his (i.e., the child’s) hand. And in the Gemara (Tractate Bava Batra 87b-88a), it raises the question: why is the storekeeper liable for the flask? It is a loss with consent. For he himself (i.e., the father) sent it in the hand of his son, and [the Gemara] answers for example that the storekeeper took it to measure oil for others. Therefore, he became a thief concerning it. And it exists in his domain until he returns it to the mater. And even though he returned it to the young child, he is not exempt from it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:9:2)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה פּוֹטֵר שֶׁעַל מְנָת כֵּן שְׁלָחוֹ.** כִּי הֵיכִי דְּלִשְׁדַר לֵיהּ חֶנְוָנִי בְּיַד בְּנוֹ:


רבי יהודה פוטר שעל מנת כן שלחו – for just as the storekeeper sent it in the hand of his son


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:9:3)


**שֶׁהוּא פָטוּר.** מִדְּמֵי צְלוֹחִית. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


שהוא פטור – from the cost of the flask, but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 10

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:10:1)


**הַסִּיטוֹן.** סוֹחֵר שֶׁקּוֹנֶה הַרְבֵּה בְּיַחַד וְחוֹזֵר וּמוֹכֵר לַחֶנְוָנִים מְעַט מְעַט:


הסיטון – a business man that purchases a lot together, and then goes back and sells to storekeepers bit by bit (i.e., wholesale provision dealer).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:10:2)


**מְקַנֵּחַ מִדּוֹתָיו.** בִּשְׁבִיל הַיַּיִן וְהַשֶּׁמֶן שֶׁנִּקְרָשׁ בְּתוֹכָם וְהַמִּדָּה מִתְמַעֶטֶת:


מקנח מדיותיו – for the wine and/or the oil hat became congealed in them and the measurement is lessened.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:10:3)


**וּבַעַל הַבַּיִת.** שֶׁאֵינוֹ מוֹכֵר תָּדִיר כַּסִּיטוֹן, מְקַנֵּחַ פַּעַם אַחַת בְּשָׁנָה:


ובעל הבית – that doesn’t sell frequently like a wholesale provision dealer, cleanses them once a year.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:10:4)


**פַּעֲמַיִם בַּשַּׁבָּת.** שֶׁהַחֶנְוָנִי אֵינוֹ חַיָּב לְהַטִּיף שָׁלֹשׁ טִפִּין כְּמוֹ שֶׁחַיָּב הַסִּיטוֹן וּבַעַל הַבַּיִת, וּמִשְׁתַּיֵּר בַּכְּלִי וְנִדְבָּק בּוֹ:


פעמים בשבת – the storekeeper is not liable to drip three drops as the wholesale provision dealer and the owner as it remains in the utensil and cleaves to it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:10:5)


**וּמְמַחֶה.** מְקַנֵּחַ אַבְנֵי מִשְׁקְלוֹתָיו שֶׁשּׁוֹקֵל בָּהֶן דָּבָר לַח:


וממחה – he cleanses the stones of his scales that he weighs on them a moist/liquid thing.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:10:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:10:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:10:6)


**עַל כָּל מִשְׁקָל וּמִשְׁקָל.** כָּל פַּעַם שֶׁשּׁוֹקֵל מְקַנֵּחַ:


על כל משקל ומשקל – every time that he weighs something, he cleanses it/wipes it down.



### Mishnah 11

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:11:1)


**וְחַיָּב לְהַכְרִיעַ טֶפַח.** מִמִּשְׁקַל לִיטְרָא וָאֵילַךְ. אֲבָל פָּחוֹת מִלִּיטְרָא אֵין צָרִיךְ הֶכְרֵעַ טֶפַח:


וחייב להכריע טפח – from the weight a liter and beyond, but less than a liter, he doesn’t need the customary additional weight in retail of a handbreadth (see Tractate Bava Batra 89a).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:11:2)


**הָיָה שׁוֹקֵל עַיִן בְּעַיִן.** כְּגוֹן בְּמָקוֹם שֶׁלֹּא נָהֲגוּ לְהַכְרִיעַ וּמַעֲמִיד קְנֵה הַמֹּאזְנַיִם בְּשָׁוֶה:


היה שוקל עין בעין – as for example, in a place where they did not have the practice of the customary additional weight in retail of a handbreadth and he restores the reed of the balance equally.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:11:3)


**נוֹתֵן לוֹ אֶת גֵּרוּמָיו.** הַכְרָעוֹתָיו. לְכָל עֲשָׂרָה לִיטְרָא מוֹסִיף לוֹ עֲשִׂירִית לִיטְרָא, שֶׁהוּא אֶחָד לְמֵאָה כְּשֶׁיִּמְכֹּר בַּלַּח. וּבַיָּבֵשׁ מוֹסִיף [חֲצִי] עֲשִׂירִית לִיטְרָא לְכָל עֶשְׂרִים לִיטְרָא:


נותן לו את הגירומין – his customary additional weight in retailing for every ten liters, he adds one-tenth of a liter which is 1/100 when he sells liquid/moist, and with dry [products], he adds [one-half] of one tenth of a liter to every twenty liters.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:11:4)


**לָמֹד בַּדַּקָּה.** בְּמִדָּה קְטַנָּה:


למוד בדקה – with a small measure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:11:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:11:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:11:5)


**לֹא יָמֹד בַּגַּסָּה.** בְּמִדָּה גְּדוֹלָה. דַּהֲוֵי פְּסֵדָא דְּלוֹקֵחַ, שֶׁאֵין נוֹתֵן לוֹ אֶלָּא הֶכְרֵעַ אֶחָד וְהָיָה צָרִיךְ לִתֵּן לוֹ הַכְרָעוֹת רַבּוֹת:


לא ימוד בגסה – with a large measure, for there is loss to the purchaser as he doesn’t give him anything other than the one customary additional weight in retailing. But he should need to give him many customary additional weights in retailing.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:11:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:11:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:11:6)


**לִמְחֹק.** לְהָסִיר מַה שֶּׁעוֹלֶה עַל שְׂפַת הַמִּדָּה:


למחוק – to remove what goes upon the rim of the measure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:11:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:11:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:11:7)


**לֹא יִגְדֹּשׁ.** וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁמּוֹסִיף לוֹ בַּדָּמִים:


לא יגדוש – and even though he adds cost to him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 5:11:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:11:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/5:11:8)


**לֹא יִמְחֹק.** וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁפּוֹחֵת לוֹ מִן הַדָּמִים:


ימחוק – and even though he lessens for him from the payment.



## Chapter 6


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 6:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:1:1)


**הַמּוֹכֵר פֵּרוֹת.** סְתָם, וְלֹא פֵּרֵשׁ לַאֲכִילָה אוֹ לִזְרִיעָה:


המוכר פירות – undefined, and it (i.e., the Mishnah) does not explain whether for eating or for sowing.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 6:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:1:2)


**אֲפִלּוּ זֶרַע פִּשְׁתָּן.** דְּרֻבָּא קוֹנִים אוֹתוֹ לִזְרִיעָה, מָצֵי לְמֵימַר אֲנִי לַאֲכִילָה מְכַרְתִּיו. דְּאֵין הוֹלְכִין בְּמָמוֹן אַחַר הָרֹב:


אפילו זרע פשתן – for most purchase it for sowing. One can say: “I sold it for eating, and we don’t follow after the majority for money.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 6:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:1:3)


**רַבָּן שִׁמְעוֹן בֶּן גַּמְלִיאֵל אוֹמֵר וְכוּ'.** בַּגְּמָרָא מוֹקֵי כֻּלָּהּ מַתְנִיתִין אַלִּיבָּא דְּרַבָּן שִׁמְעוֹן בֶּן גַּמְלִיאֵל. וַחֲסוּרֵי מִחַסְּרָא וְהָכִי קָתָנֵי, אֲפִלּוּ זֶרַע פִּשְׁתָּן אֵינוֹ חַיָּב בְּאַחֲרָיוּתָן, הָא זֵרְעוֹנֵי גִּנָּה שֶׁאֵינָן נֶאֱכָלִין חַיָּב בְּאַחֲרָיוּתָן, דִּבְרֵי רַבָּן שִׁמְעוֹן בֶּן גַּמְלִיאֵל. שֶׁרַבָּן שִׁמְעוֹן בֶּן גַּמְלִיאֵל אוֹמֵר, זֵרְעוֹנֵי גִּנָּה שֶׁאֵינָן נֶאֱכָלִין חַיָּב בְּאַחֲרָיוּתָן, דַּהֲוֵי מִקַּח טָעוּת, דְּוַדַּאי לִזְרִיעָה זַבְּנִינְהוּ:


רבן שמעון בן גמליאל אומר וכו' – The Gemara (Talmud Bava Batra 93b) establishes all of our Mishnah according to Rabban Shimon ben Gamaliel but the Mishnah is deficient and should be read as follows: even if it were flax seed, he is not responsible. But surely, garden seeds which are not eaten, one is responsible for them, the words of Rabbi Shimon ben Gamaliel. For Rabban Shimon ben Gamaliel states that garden seeds which are not eaten, one is responsible for them, for it is an errant transaction for certainly he sold them for planting.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 6:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:2:1)


**הַמְקַבֵּל פֵּרוֹת.** שֶׁקָּנָה תְּבוּאָה מֵחֲבֵרוֹ. הַלּוֹקֵחַ מְקַבֵּל עָלָיו רֹבַע טִנֹּפֶת לְכָל סְאָה, דְּדֶרֶךְ תְּבוּאָה בְּכָךְ. וְלֹא יוֹתֵר:


המקבל פירות – that he bought grain from his fellow. The purchaser accepts for himself for every Seah a quarter-kab of refuse, for that is the manner of grain in this and not more.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 6:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:2:2)


**עֶשֶׂר מְתֻלָּעוֹת.** אֲכוּלוֹת מִתּוֹלָעִים. לְכָל מֵאָה, דְּהַיְנוּ אֶחָד מֵעֶשֶׂר:


עשר מתליעות – [ten] eaten by worms for every one hundred, which is one out of ten.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 6:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:2:3)


**קוֹסְסוֹת.** יַיִן רַע. אִם הַמַּרְתֵּף שֶׁל חָבִיּוֹת גְּדוֹלוֹת מְקַבֵּל עָלָיו עֶשֶׂר חָבִיּוֹת גְּדוֹלוֹת. וְאִם הַמַּרְתֵּף שֶׁל כַּדּוֹת, מְקַבֵּל עָלָיו עֶשֶׂר כַּדּוֹת. וְדַוְקָא כְּשֶׁאָמַר מַרְתֵּף זֶה אֲנִי מוֹכֵר לְךָ, וּלְמִקְפָּה, כְּלוֹמַר לָשִׂים בְּתוֹךְ הַתַּבְשִׁיל. אֲבָל אִם אָמַר מַרְתֵּף שֶׁל יַיִן אֲנִי מוֹכֵר לְךָ לְמִקְפָּה, צָרִיךְ לִתֵּן לוֹ יַיִן שֶׁכֻּלּוֹ יָפֶה. שֶׁיַּיִן שֶׁל מִקְפָּה צָרִיךְ שֶׁיִּהְיֶה יָפֶה וּמִתְקַיֵּם, שֶׁאֵין מִסְתַּפְּקִים מִמֶּנּוּ אֶלָּא מְעַט מְעַט. וְאִם אָמַר סְתָם יַיִן אֲנִי מוֹכֵר לְךָ וְלֹא זָכַר לְמִקְפָּה, נוֹתֵן לוֹ יַיִן בֵּינוֹנִי הַנִּמְכָּר בַּחֲנוּת. וְאִם אָמַר מַרְתֵּף זֶה אֲנִי מוֹכֵר לְךָ וְלֹא הִזְכִּיר יַיִן, אֲפִלּוּ כֻּלּוֹ חֹמֶץ, הִגִּיעוֹ:


קוסמות – bad wine. If the cellar of large wine jugs accepts upon itself ten large jugs, and if the storeroom of pitchers accepts upon itself ten pitchers, and specifically when he stated: “I am selling you this cellar.” And the stiff mass of grist, oil and onions (i.e., a porridge), meaning to say, to place within the cooked dish but if he said: “[I am selling] you a cellar of wine for the stiff mass of grist, oil and onions,” he must give him wine which is all fine for wine of a stiff mass of grist, oil and onions must be fine and enduring and that we have doubts about it other than a little bit. But, if he said, “I am selling you undefined wine,” and no mention of the stiff mass of grist, oil and onions, he gives him intermediate-quality wine that is sold in the store. But if he said: “This cellar I am selling to you,” but he did not mention even if all of it had soured/fermented had arrived.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 6:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:2:4)


**קַנְקַנִּים בַּשָּׁרוֹן.** בְּאֶרֶץ הַמִּישׁוֹר:


קנקנים בשרון – in the land of the plains.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 6:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:2:5)


**מְקַבֵּל עָלָיו עֶשֶׂר פִּיטַסְיָאוֹת.** שֶׁאֵינָן מְבֻשָּׁלוֹת כָּל צָרְכָּן. וְהֵן שׁוֹאֲבוֹת הַיַּיִן וּמַזִּיעוֹת אוֹתוֹ:


מקבל עליו עשר פטסיאות – that are not boiled all the way through and they draw wine and drip it.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 6:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:3:1)


**אֵינוֹ חַיָּב.** הַמּוֹכֵר בְּאַחֲרָיוּתוֹ. דְּבִשְׁעַת מְכִירָה יַיִן הֲוָה. וְדַוְקָא שֶׁנַּעֲשֶׂה חֹמֶץ בְּכֵלָיו דְּלוֹקֵחַ, דְּאִי בְּכֵלָיו שֶׁל מוֹכֵר, מָצֵי אָמַר לֵיהּ הָא חָמָרָךְ וְהָא קַנְקַנָּךְ, וְלֹא מָצֵי מוֹכֵר אָמַר לֵיהּ לָמָּה הִשְׁהֵיתָ אוֹתוֹ בַּקַּנְקַנִּים כָּל כָּךְ, אַתְּ הוּא דְּאַפְסַדְתְּ, דְּמָצֵי הִיאַךְ אָמַר לֵיהּ הָא מֵעִקָּרָא הוֹדַעְתִּיךָ שֶׁלֹּא הָיָה בְּדַעְתִּי לְהִסְתַּפֵּק מִן הַיַּיִן אֶלָּא מְעַט מְעַט:


אינו חייב – [the seller in his responsibility] for at the time of the sale, it was wine. And specifically, when it became fermented/sour in the utensils of the purchaser, for if it were in the utensils of the seller [when it became fermented], he (i.e., the purchaser) could say to him (i.e., the seller): “ this is your wine and this your vessel,” and the seller is not able to say to him: “why did you leave it (i.e., the wine) in these vessels so long? You are the one who has taken a loss,” for this one could have said to him: but from the beginning, I informed you that it was not my intention to be supported by this wine, other than a little bit [at a time].”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 6:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:3:2)


**וְאִם יָדוּעַ שֶׁיֵּינוֹ מַחְמִיץ.** שֶׁאֵינוֹ מִתְקַיֵּם. וְאִידָךְ אָמַר לֵיהּ דְּלֹא בָּעֵי לַיַּיִן אֶלָּא לַמִּקְפָּה וְלַשְׁהוֹתוֹ לִקַּח מִמֶּנּוּ מְעַט מְעַט:


ואם ידוע שיינו מחמיץ – that it is not preserved and the other one says to him that he didn’t need wine, but rather for a stiff mass of grist, oil and onions and to let it stand to take from it only bit by bit.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 6:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:3:3)


**הֲרֵי זֶה מִקַּח טָעוּת.** דַּהֲוָה לֵיהּ לְאוֹדוּעֵי שֶׁאֵין יֵינוֹ מִתְקַיֵּם:


הרי זה מקח טעות – he should have informed him that his wine does not endure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 6:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:3:4)


**מְבֻשָּׂם.** דַּרְכּוֹ לְהִתְקַיֵּם עַד עֲצֶרֶת. וּמִשָּׁם וָאֵילַךְ הַחֹם מְקַלְקְלוֹ:


מבוסם – it is its manner to endure until Shavuot. But from then onward, the heat spoils it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 6:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:3:5)


**וְיָשָׁן.** אִם מָכַר לוֹ יַיִן עַל מְנָת שֶׁהוּא יָשָׁן, נוֹתֵן לוֹ מִשֶּׁל אֶשְׁתָּקַד, מִן הַשָּׁנָה שֶׁעָבְרָה הַקּוֹדֶמֶת לָזוֹ:


וישן – if he sold him wine on the condition that it is old, he gives him from the year before – from the year that past that precedes this one.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 6:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:4:1)


**בֵּית חַתְנוּת לִבְנוֹ.** שֶׁדֶּרֶךְ אָדָם לַעֲשׂוֹת לִבְנוֹ בַּיִת כְּשֶׁנּוֹשֵׂא אִשָּׁה:


בית חתנות לבנו – for it is way of a person to make a house for his son when he gets married to a woman.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 6:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:4:2)


**בֵּית אַלְמְנוּת לְבִתּוֹ.** כְּשֶׁמֵּת בַּעְלָהּ וְשָׁבָה אֶל בֵּית אָבִיהָ. אֲבָל בְּחַיֵּי בַּעְלָהּ הִיא אֵצֶל בַּעְלָהּ, שֶׁאֵין דֶּרֶךְ חָתָן לָדוּר עִם חָמִיו:


בית אלמנות לבתו – when her husband dies and she returns to the house of her father, but during the life of her husband, she is with her husband, for it is not the way for the a groom to live with his father-in-law.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 6:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:4:3)


**רֶפֶת בָּקָר הוּא זֶה.** וְצָרִיךְ לַעֲשׂוֹתוֹ גָּדוֹל יוֹתֵר:


רפת בקר הוא זה – he would need to make it much bigger.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 6:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:4:4)


**בַּיִת קָטָן.** הַפָּחוּת שֶׁבַּבָּתִּים הוּא שֵׁשׁ עַל שְׁמֹנֶה. וְהַמּוֹכֵר מָקוֹם לַחֲבֵרוֹ סְתָם לַעֲשׂוֹת לוֹ בַּיִת צָרִיךְ שֶׁיִּהְיֶה שֵׁשׁ עַל שְׁמֹנֶה:


בית קטן – the least of these houses is six [cubits] by eight [cubits]. But a person who sells his friend a place, undefined to make for himself a house, it must be six [cubits] by eight [cubits].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 6:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:4:5)


**גָּדוֹל.** וְאִם פֵּרֵשׁ לַעֲשׂוֹת לוֹ בַּיִת גָּדוֹל:


גדול – and if he specified to make for him a large house.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 6:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:4:6)


**טְרַקְלִין.** עָשׂוּי לְמוֹשַׁב שָׂרִים:


טרקלין – it is made for the seating of princes.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 6:4:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:4:7)


**רוּמוֹ כַּחֲצִי אָרְכּוֹ וְכַחֲצִי רָחְבּוֹ.** אַכֻּלְּהוּ קָאֵי, דְּהַיְנוּ בְּבַיִת קָטָן רוּמוֹ שִׁבְעָה, וּבְבַיִת גָּדוֹל תִּשְׁעָה, וּטְרַקְלִין עֶשֶׂר:


רומו כחצי ארכו וכחצי רחבו – it refers to all of them, meaning for a small house, its height is seven [cubits] and for a large house, nine [cubits] and for a reception room, ten [cubits].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 6:4:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:4:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:4:8)


**רְאָיָה לַדָּבָר הֵיכָל.** שֶׁהָיָה אָרְכּוֹ אַרְבָּעִים וְרָחְבּוֹ עֶשְׂרִים וְרוּמוֹ שְׁלֹשִׁים, שֶׁהֵם חֲצִי אָרְכּוֹ וְרָחְבּוֹ:


ראיה לדבר היכל – that its length was forty [cubits] and its width twenty [cubits] and its height thirty [cubits], which are one-half its length and width (see First Kings, Chapter 6, verses 2 and 17 for a description of the First Temple and Tractate Middot, Chapter 4, Mishnayot 6-7 for a description of the Second Temple).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 6:4:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:4:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:4:9)


**הַכֹּל כְּבִנְיַן הֵיכָל.** אִיכָּא דְּאָמְרֵי הָא דִּתְנַן לְעֵיל רוּמוֹ כַּחֲצִי אָרְכּוֹ וְכַחֲצִי רָחְבּוֹ, רְאָיָה לַדָּבָר הֵיכָל, רַבָּן שִׁמְעוֹן בֶּן גַּמְלִיאֵל קָתָנֵי לַהּ, שֶׁרַבָּן שִׁמְעוֹן בֶּן גַּמְלִיאֵל אוֹמֵר הַכֹּל כְּבִנְיַן הֵיכָל. וְאִיכָּא דְּאָמְרֵי רַבָּן שִׁמְעוֹן בֶּן גַּמְלִיאֵל פָּלֵיג אַתַּנָּא קַמָּא וְאַתְּמוֹהִי קָא מִתַּמֵּהּ, וְכִי הַכֹּל בּוֹנִים כְּבִנְיַן הֵיכָל, אֵין הַדָּבָר תָּלוּי אֶלָּא בְּמִנְהַג הַמְּדִינָה:


הכל כבנין היכל – There is one who say that what is taught in the Mishnah above that its height should be [the sum of] half of its length and half of its width (see also Tractate Eruvin, Chapter 6, Mishnah 6). The proof of the matter is the Sanctuary/Temple. Rabban Shimon ben Gamaliel taught it as Rabbi Shimon ben Gamaliel states: הכל כבנין היכל/Everything should be in accord with the building of the Sanctuary/Temple. But there is one who says that Rabban Shimon ben Gamaliel disputes with the first Tanna/teacher [of the Mishnah] and expresses astonishment in asking: “Should everything be in accordance with the Sanctuary/Temple?” The matter is not dependent upon anything but the custom of the country.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 6:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:5:1)


**בְּשָׁעָה שֶׁבְּנֵי אָדָם נִכְנָסִים.** בַּיּוֹם. וְלֹא שֶׁיַּטְרִיחֵהוּ לַעֲמֹד בַּלַּיְלָה:


בשעה שבני אדם נכנסים – during the daytime and not to trouble him to get up at night.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 6:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:5:2)


**זֶה עוֹשֶׂה לוֹ פוֹתַחַת.** שֶׁלֹּא יִגְנֹב בַּעַל הַבַּיִת מִמֵּי בּוֹרוֹ. וּבַעַל הַבַּיִת עוֹשֶׂה לוֹ פּוֹתַחַת אַחֶרֶת בְּאוֹתוֹ פֶּתַח הַפָּתוּחַ לַבּוֹר, שֶׁלֹּא יוּכַל לָבֹא לִשְׁאֹב בְּלֹא דַּעַת בַּעַל הַבַּיִת, וְלֹא יָבֹא לְעוֹלָם בְּבֵיתוֹ שֶׁל בַּעַל הַבַּיִת אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן יֶשְׁנוֹ לְבַעַל הַבַּיִת בְּבֵיתוֹ. וּמִשּׁוּם חֲשַׁד אִשְׁתּוֹ תִּקְּנוּ כֵּן:


זה עושה לו פותחת – so that the owner of the house will not steal from his cistern, and the owner of the house makes another lock on the same door that opens to the well, so that he cannot come to draw water without the knowledge of the owner. And, he will not ever come into the house of the owner of the house other than if the owner of the house is at home, and they established this because of the suspicions of his wife.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 6:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:6:1)


**מִי שֶׁיֶּשׁ לוֹ גִנָּה.** וְנִתְרַצָּה חִיצוֹן לָתֵת לַפְּנִימִי דֶּרֶךְ בְּאֶמְצַע שָׂדֵהוּ:


מי שיש לו גינה – [and the owner] of the outer [garden] was reconciled to give to [the owner of] the inner garden a path in the middle of his field.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 6:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:6:2)


**לֹא יִכָּנֵס מִתּוֹכָהּ.** דְּכֵיוָן דְּמַזִּיקוֹ הֶזֵּק גָּדוֹל שֶׁעוֹבֵר בְּאֶמְצַע שָׂדֵהוּ, אֲנַן סַהֲדֵי דְּלֹא נִתְרַצָּה לוֹ אֶלָּא לְצֹרֶךְ גִּנָּה בִּלְבַד, וְלֹא שֶׁיִּכָּנֵס לְצֹרֶךְ תַּשְׁמִישׁ אַחֵר:


לא יכנס בתוכה – for since he causes great damage when he passes through the middle of his field, we testify that he was not reconciled with him other than for the needs of his garden alone, and that he should not enter for any other usage.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 6:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:6:3)


**וְהַחִיצוֹן זוֹרֵעַ.** דְּכֵיוָן דִּבְאֶמְצַע שָׂדֵהוּ הוּא, אֲנַן סַהֲדֵי דְּלֹא מָחַל לוֹ הַדֶּרֶךְ לְגַמְרֵי וְשֶׁלֹּא יוּכַל הוּא לְזָרְעָהּ:


והחיצון זורע – for since that he is in the middle of his (i.e., the other’s) field, we testify that he did not pardon him completely and that he should not be able to sow seeds.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 6:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:6:4)


**וְזֶה וָזֶה אֵין רַשָּׁאִין לְזָרְעָהּ.** דְּכֵיוָן דְּמִן הַצַּד הוּא, לְהִלּוּךְ גְּרֵידָא יִחֲדוּהוּ לוֹ:


זה וזה אין רשאין לזרעה – because It is from the side; for walking through only he designated it.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 6:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:7:1)


**מִי שֶׁהָיְתָה דֶרֶךְ הָרַבִּים עוֹבֶרֶת בְּתוֹךְ שָׂדֵהוּ.** שֶׁהֶחֱזִיקוּ רַבִּים לַעֲבֹר שָׁם לְעוֹלָם:


מי שהיתה דרך [הרבי'] עוברת בתוך שדהו – the masses always presumed to be able to pass through there.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 6:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:7:2)


**וְשֶׁלּוֹ לֹא הִגִּיעוֹ.** וְיֵשׁ לָרַבִּים שְׁנֵי הַדְּרָכִים. דְּקַיְמָא לָן מֵצַר שֶׁהֶחֱזִיקוּ בּוֹ רַבִּים אָסוּר לְקַלְקְלוֹ:


ושלו לא הגיעו – but the many have two paths, for we hold that a narrow path marking the boundary that the masses presumed [to use], it is forbidden to ruin.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 6:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:7:3)


**דֶּרֶךְ הַיָּחִיד.** הַמּוֹכֵר לַחֲבֵרוֹ דֶּרֶךְ תּוֹךְ שָׂדֵהוּ צָרִיךְ לִתֵּן לוֹ אַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת:


דרך היחיד – a person who sells to his fellow a path within his field needs to give him four cubits.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 6:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:7:4)


**דֶּרֶךְ הַמֶּלֶךְ אֵין לוֹ שִׁעוּר.** שֶׁהַמֶּלֶךְ פּוֹרֵץ גָּדֵר לְפָנָיו לַעֲשׂוֹת לוֹ דֶּרֶךְ:


דרך המלך אין לו שעור – for the king breaches a fence in front of him to make for himself a path.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 6:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:7:5)


**דֶּרֶךְ הַקֶּבֶר.** שֶׁנּוֹשְׂאִין הַמֵּת לְקָבְרוֹ, אֵין לוֹ שִׁעוּר. תַּקָּנַת חֲכָמִים הִיא, מִשּׁוּם יְקָרָא דְּמֵת. וְלֹא שֶׁיְּכוֹלִים לִפְרֹץ גָּדֵר כְּדֶרֶךְ הַמֶּלֶךְ, אֶלָּא שֶׁיְּכוֹלִים הַמְלַוִּין אֶת הַמֵּת לַעֲבֹר עַל גַּבֵּי זְרָעִים וְאֵין צְרִיכִים לִנְטוֹת לְכָאן וּלְכָאן:


דרך הקבר – when they carry the dead person to his grave, there is no measure. It is an enactment of the Sages because of the dignity of the deceased, but they are not able to breach a fence like the way of the king, but those who accompany the dead can pass ove seeds and the don’t have to turn to the one side or the other.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 6:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:7:6)


**בֵּית אַרְבַּעַת קַבִּין.** הוּא [שְׁלֹשִׁים וְשָׁלֹשׁ אַמּוֹת וּשְׁנֵי טְפָחִים] רֹחַב בְּאֹרֶךְ חֲמִשִּׁים אַמָּה. וּמְקוֹם הַמַּעֲמָד הוּא שֶׁהָיוּ עוֹשִׂים שִׁבְעָה מַעֲמָדוֹת שִׁבְעָה מוֹשָׁבוֹת כְּשֶׁהָיוּ חוֹזְרִין מִלִּקְבֹּר הַמֵּת, כְּנֶגֶד שִׁבְעָה הֲבָלִים שֶׁבִּתְחִלַּת סֵפֶר קֹהֶלֶת:


בית ארבעה קבים – it is [thirty three cubits and two handbreadths] wide at the length of fifty cubits. And the funeral-halting place where they would make seven halts of the funeral escort on returning from burial for lamentation or consolation, corresponding to the seven mentions of הבל/vanity in at the beginning of the [Biblical] book of Ecclesiastes.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 6:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:8:1)


**הַמְקַבֵּל מֵחֲבֵרוֹ.** בְּקִבֹּלֶת שֶׁיַּחְפֹּר לוֹ קֶבֶר:


המקבל מחבירו – with contract-labor job that he should dig him a grave.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 6:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:8:2)


**אַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת.** רֹחַב, וְשֵׁשׁ בָּאֹרֶךְ:


ארבע אמות – in width and six [cubits] in length.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 6:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:8:3)


**שְׁמֹנָה כוּכִין.** שְׁמֹנָה קְבָרִים:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 6:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:8:4)


**שְׁלֹשָׁה מִכָּאן שְׁלֹשָׁה מִכָּאן.** לִשְׁנֵי צְדָדִין שֶׁל אֹרֶךְ הַמְּעָרָה. וּשְׁנַיִם כְּנֶגֶד הַנִּכְנָס לַמְּעָרָה. וְכָל קֶבֶר וָקֶבֶר אַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת אֹרֶךְ וְרֹחַב שִׁשָּׁה טְפָחִים וְרוּם שִׁבְעָה. נִמְצָא בֵּין כָּל קֶבֶר וָקֶבֶר שֶׁמִּן הַצְּדָדִים אַמָּה וּמֶחֱצָה. וּבֵין הַשְּׁנַיִם שֶׁמִּכְּנֶגֶד שְׁתֵּי אַמּוֹת:


שלש מכאן ושלש מכאן – to the two sides of the length of the cave, and two opposite the entrance to the cave, and each grave is four cubits long, and its width is six handbreadths at a height of seven cubits, it is found that between each and every grave from the sides is a cubit-and-a-half, and between the two that are opposite them two cubits.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 6:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/6:8:5)


**הַכֹּל לְפִי הַסָּלַע.** אִם הַמָּקוֹם קָשֶׁה אֵין הַמְקַבֵּל חַיָּב לַחְפֹּר אֶלָּא שֵׁשׁ אַמּוֹת בָּאֹרֶךְ וְאַרְבַּע בָּרֹחַב כְּדִבְרֵי תַּנָּא קַמָּא. וְאִם הַמָּקוֹם רַךְ וְתִחוֹחַ חַיָּב הַמְקַבֵּל לַחְפֹּר שֵׁשׁ עַל שְׁמֹנֶה כְּדִבְרֵי רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן. וַהֲלָכָה כְּתַנָּא קַמָּא:


הכל לפי הסלע – if the place is hard, the recipient is not liable to dig other than six cubits length and four cubits width according to the words of the first Tanna/teacher [of the Mishnah], but if the place is soft and crushed earth, the recipient is obligated to dig [a grave] six [cubits] by eight [cubits] according to the words of Rabbi Shimon, but the Halakha is according to the first Tanna/teacher.



## Chapter 7


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 7:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/7:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/7:1:1)


**הָאוֹמֵר לַחֲבֵרוֹ בֵּית כּוֹר עָפָר.** מִדַּת בֵּית כּוֹר הוּא חֲמִשָּׁה וְשִׁבְעִים אֶלֶף אַמָּה. שֶׁחֲצַר הַמִּשְׁכָּן הָיָה בֵּית סָאתַיִם, וְהָיָה מֵאָה עַל חֲמִשִּׁים, וְכוֹר שְׁלֹשִׁים סְאִין:


האומר לחבירו בית כור עפר – the measurement of a Khor’s worth of seed is seventy-five thousand cubits where the courtyard of the Tabernacle was a field requiring two Se’ah of seed, which was one hundred [cubits] by fifty [cubits] and a Khor is thirty Se’ah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 7:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/7:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/7:1:2)


**עָפָר.** הָרָאוּי לִזְרִיעָה מַשְׁמַע. אֲבָל אִם אָמַר בֵּית כּוֹר סְתָם, אוֹ בֵּית כּוֹר קַרְקַע, אֲפִלּוּ הָיָה כֻּלּוֹ סְלָעִים הִגִּיעוֹ, דְּשֶׁמָּא לִבְנוֹת בַּיִת אוֹ לְמִשְׁטַח בֵּיהּ פֵּרוֹת בָּעֵי לֵיהּ:


עפר – that is appropriate for sowing is implied. But if he stated, in an area requiring a Khor’s of seed undefined, or a field requiring a Khor of seed, even if all of it is rocks, he takes possession of it, for perhaps it was to build a house or to spread out fruit [to dry], he required it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 7:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/7:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/7:1:3)


**נְקָעִים.** בְּקָעִים עֲמֻקִּים אוֹ סְלָעִים גְּבוֹהִים עֲשָׂרָה. וּכְגוֹן שֶׁרְחָבִים אַרְבָּעָה עַל אַרְבָּעָה, דַּהֲוֵי מָקוֹם חָשׁוּב:


נקעים – fields ten handbreadths deep or [rocks] ten handbreadths high, and for example, they are wide four handbreadths by four handbreadths and are an important place.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 7:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/7:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/7:1:4)


**אֵין נִמְדָּדִין עִמָּהּ.** וְצָרִיךְ שֶׁיִּתֵּן לוֹ בֵּית כּוֹר שָׁלֵם מִקַּרְקַע חָלָק. שֶׁאֵין אָדָם רוֹצֶה לִתֵּן מְעוֹתָיו בְּמָקוֹם אֶחָד וְיֵרָאוּ לוֹ כִּשְׁנַיִם וּשְׁלֹשָׁה מְקוֹמוֹת:


אין נמדדין עמה – and he must give him an area requiring a complete Khor of seed from level/smooth ground, for a person does not want to put all of his money into one place, and he will show him two or three places.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 7:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/7:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/7:1:5)


**כְּבֵית כּוֹר.** מַשְׁמַע כְּמוֹ שֶׁהִיא, בֵּין סְלָעִים בֵּין עָפָר:


כבית כור – which implies as it is, whether it is rocks or soil.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 7:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/7:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/7:2:1)


**מִדָּה בַחֶבֶל.** כְּלוֹמַר בְּצִמְצוּם אֲנִי מוֹכֵר לְךָ שָׂדֶה זוֹ שֶׁיֶּשׁ בָּהּ בֵּית כּוֹר עָפָר, כְּמוֹ שֶׁמּוֹדְדִים בְּמִדַּת הַחֶבֶל, לֹא פָּחוֹת וְלֹא יוֹתֵר:


מדה בחבל – meaning to say, with an exact measurement, I sell you this field which has a Khor’s worth of soil, just as they measure with the rope’s measurement, no less and no more.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 7:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/7:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/7:2:2)


**פִּחֵת כָּל שֶׁהוּא.** שֶׁלֹּא הָיָה בְּאוֹתוֹ שָׂדֶה בֵּית כּוֹר שָׁלֵם, הַמִּקָּח קַיָּם, וִינַכֶּה הַמּוֹכֵר כְּפִי הַפְּחָת:


פחת כל שהוא – for that field did not have a complete Khor’s worth of soil, the transaction exists and the seller deducts according to that which is less.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 7:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/7:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/7:2:3)


**הוֹתִיר.** קַרְקַע כָּלְשֶׁהוּ עַל בֵּית כּוֹר, יַחֲזִיר לַמּוֹכֵר הַקַּרְקַע שֶׁהוֹתִיר אוֹ דְּמֵי הַקַּרְקַע, כְּדִמְפָרֵשׁ וְאָזִיל:


הותיר – land [was additional] a little bit, over the Khor’s worth, he (i.e., the purchaser) returns to the seller the land that was added on or the value of the land as will be explained further on.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 7:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/7:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/7:2:4)


**אִם אָמַר לוֹ.** מוֹכֵר לַלּוֹקֵחַ, בֵּית כּוֹר עָפָר אֲנִי מוֹכֵר לְךָ הֵן חָסֵר הֵן יָתֵר, בְּכָךְ וְכָךְ דָּמִים, וְלֹא אָמַר לוֹ מִדָּה בַּחֶבֶל:


אם אמר לו – the area of a Khor’s worth of soil I am selling to you, whether it be less, whether it be more for such and such a cost, and he did not tell him, “according to the measurement of the rope.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 7:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/7:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/7:2:5)


**אֲפִלּוּ פִחֵת אוֹ הוֹתִיר רֹבַע קַב.** לְכָל סְאָה, דְּהַיְנוּ שְׁלֹשִׁים רְבִיעִיּוֹת לְבֵית כּוֹר, הִגִּיעוֹ. וּמִדְּתָנֵי תַּנָּא אֲפִלּוּ פִּחֵת רֹבַע לַסְּאָה, וְלֹא תָּנֵי אֲפִלּוּ פִּחֵת שִׁבְעָה קַבִּין וּמֶחֱצָה לְכוֹר, שָׁמְעִינַן מִנַּהּ דַּאֲפִלּוּ בִּמְכִירָה מֻעֶטֶת, כְּגוֹן בֵּית סְאָה אֲנִי מוֹכֵר לְךָ הֵן חָסֵר הֵן יָתֵר, אִם פִּחֵת רֹבַע אוֹ הוֹסִיף רֹבַע, הִגִּיעוֹ. וְאִם אָמַר בֵּית כּוֹר סְתָם, סְתָמָא נַמִּי כְּהֵן חָסֵר הֵן יָתֵר דָּמִי:


אפילו פחת או הותיר רובע קב – for every Se’ah, which means thirty quarters (i.e., a quarter Kab equals 104 1/6 square cubits) for the area requiring a Khor of seed, he takes possession of it. For since the Tanna/teacher [of the Mishnah] taught even if he lessened it by one-quarter for a Se’ah (i.e., a Se’ah’s area equals 2500 square cubits), and did not teach “even if he lessened it seven and one-half kabs to the Khor, we learn from it, that even in a small sale, such as a Se’ah I am selling you, whether it be less or more, if he lessened it by a quarter of a Khor or added to it a quarter, he takes possession of it. But if he said, an undefined area requiring a Khor of seed, also whether it is less or more, it is similar.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 7:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/7:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/7:2:6)


**יוֹתֵר מִכָּאן.** יוֹתֵר מֵרֹבַע לְכָל סְאָה אִם הוֹתִיר, יַעֲשֶׂה חֶשְׁבּוֹן, יַחְשֹׁב כַּמָּה הוֹתִיר עַל בֵּית כּוֹר וְכַמָּה שָׁוִין לְפִי חֶשְׁבּוֹן שֶׁמָּכַר אֶת בֵּית כּוֹר:


יותר מכאן – more than one-quarter for every Se’ah if he gave too much, he should make an accounting, calculating how much he gave over the area requiring a Khor of seed, and how much they are worth, according to the calculations that he sold him an area requiring a Khor of seed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 7:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/7:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/7:2:7)


**וְלָמָּה אָמְרוּ יַחֲזִיר לוֹ מָעוֹת.** שֶׁמִּן הַדִּין אֵין לְהַחֲזִיר לוֹ אֶלָּא קַרְקַע שֶׁהֲרֵי לֹא קָנָה הַלּוֹקֵחַ אֶלָּא בֵּית כּוֹר בִּלְבַד. וְלֹא תִּקְּנוּ חֲכָמִים שֶׁיִּתֵּן לוֹ מָעוֹת כְּנֶגֶד זֶה הַיֶּתֶר, אֶלָּא לְיַפּוֹת כֹּחוֹ שֶׁל מוֹכֵר, שֶׁלֹּא יַפְסִיד אוֹתוֹ קַרְקַע מֻעָט דְּלֹא חֲזִי לֵיהּ לְמִידִי:


ולמה אמרו יחזיר לו מעות – for according to the law, he doesn’t have to return anything to him other than land, for the purchaser did not buy anything other than an area requiring a Khor of seed., but the Sages did not establish that he should give him money corresponding to this extra part, but rather to give the prerogative to the seller, so that he will not lose that small amount of land, and it will not appear to him to be anything.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 7:2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/7:2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/7:2:8)


**שֶׁאִם שִׁיֵּר.** בְּבֵית כּוֹר שָׂדֶה בֵּית תִּשְׁעָה קַבִּין, דְּהַשְׁתָּא חֲזִי לֵיהּ הַאי קַרְקַע, כִּדְאָמְרִינַן דַּף י"א, דְּבֵית תִּשְׁעָה קַבִּין חָשׁוּב לִהְיוֹת נִקְרָא שָׂדֶה, אָז אֵין מַחֲזִיר לוֹ מָעוֹת, אֶלָּא הַמּוֹכֵר נוֹטֵל אֶת הַקַּרְקַע שֶׁלּוֹ. וְשָׁמְעִינַן מִמַּתְנִיתִין דְּשִׁבְעַת קַבִּין וּמֶחֱצָה לְכוֹר, שֶׁהוּא רֹבַע הַקַּב לְכָל סְאָה, הָוֵי מְחִילָה. מִשִּׁבְעָה קַבִּין וּמֶחֱצָה עַד תִּשְׁעָה קַבִּין וְלֹא עַד בִּכְלָל, יַעֲשֶׂה חֶשְׁבּוֹן. תִּשְׁעָה קַבִּין אוֹ יוֹתֵר, יַחֲזִיר קַרְקַע:


שאם שייר – in the area of a field requiring a Khor of seed, a house of nine Kabs, for now it appears to him that this field that we spokk about which is a house of nine Kabs is considered to be called a field, therefore, he does not return to him the money, but rather, the seller takes his field. And we learn from our Mishnah that seven and one-half kabs for a Khor which is a quarter of a kab for every Se’ah, there is a pardon. From seven and one-half kabs until nine kabs but not exactly in total, he makes an accounting; nine tabs or more, he returns the land.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 7:2:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/7:2:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/7:2:9)


**וְלֹא אֶת הָרֹבַע.** הָכִי קָאָמַר, הֵיכָא דַּאֲמָרַן יַעֲשֶׂה חֶשְׁבּוֹן אוֹ יַחֲזִיר קַרְקַע, לֹא אֶת הַמּוֹתָר בִּלְבַד הוּא מַחֲזִיר, אֶלָּא גַּם כָּל הַשְּׁלֹשִׁים רְבִיעִיּוֹת שֶׁל בֵּית כּוֹר הַיְתֵרִים הוּא מַחֲזִיר, דְּכֵיוָן דְּאִיכָּא קַרְקַע חָשׁוּב בֵּין הַכֹּל, לֹא מָחִיל מִידֵי:


ולא את הרובע – this is what he said: where we said that he should make an accounting, or return the land, not the additional alone does he return, but also the thirty fourths of the extra area of the Khor of seed he also returns. And because he has important land among everything, he does not forgive anything.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 7:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/7:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/7:3:1)


**מִדָּה בַחֶבֶל וְכוּ'.** הָאוֹמֵר לַחֲבֵרוֹ שְׁתֵּי לְשׁוֹנוֹת הַלָּלוּ הַסּוֹתְרִים זֶה אֶת זֶה, מִדָּה בַּחֶבֶל דְּמַשְׁמַע בְּצִמְצוּם, הֵן חָסֵר הֵן יָתֵר דְּמַשְׁמַע דְּלֹא קָפֵיד עַל מְעַט פָּחוֹת אוֹ יוֹתֵר. בִּטֵּל לָשׁוֹן אַחֲרוֹן שֶׁהוּא הֵן חָסֵר הֵן יָתֵר, אֶת לָשׁוֹן רִאשׁוֹן שֶׁהוּא מִדָּה בַּחֶבֶל. וַאֲפִלּוּ פִּחֵת רֹבַע לִסְאָה אוֹ הוֹתִיר רֹבַע לִסְאָה, הִגִּיעוֹ:


מדה בחבל וכו' – A person who says to his fellow these two languages that contradict each other, the measure of the rope, which implies be defining exactly/squeezed in/confined whether less or more [as measured by the rope] that implies that he was not strict about a small amount less or more, the latter language which is a small amount less or more nullifies the first language which is that which is measured by the rope, and even if he lessened it a quarter of a Se’ah or made it larger by a quarter of a Se’ah, he has taken possession of it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 7:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/7:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/7:3:2)


**דִּבְרֵי בֶן נַנָּס.** דִּסְבִירָא לֵיהּ תְּפוֹס לָשׁוֹן אַחֲרוֹן. וּפְלִיגֵי רַבָּנָן עֲלֵיהּ, וְאָמְרֵי הַלֵּךְ אַחַר פָּחוּת שֶׁבַּלְּשׁוֹנוֹת, בֵּין רִאשׁוֹן בֵּין אַחֲרוֹן. דִּמְסָפְקָא לְהוּ אִי תְּפוֹס לָשׁוֹן רִאשׁוֹן אוֹ לָשׁוֹן אַחֲרוֹן, וְנוֹטֵל הַלּוֹקֵחַ כַּפָּחוּת שֶׁבַּשִּׁעוּרִין. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


דברי בן ננס – for he holds that one should take hold of the latter language. And the Rabbis disagree with him, whether the first [language] or the latter [language] for they doubt if he took possession of the first language or the latter language. And the purchaser takes the lesser of the measurements, and the Halakha is according to the Sages.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 7:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/7:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/7:3:3)


**בְּסִימָנָיו וּבִמְצָרָיו.** אָמַר לוֹ בֵּית כּוֹר עָפָר אֲנִי מוֹכֵר לְךָ בְּתוֹךְ הַסִּימָנִים וְהַמְּצָרִים הָאֵלֶּה:


בסימניו ובמצריו – He (i.e., the seller) said to him (i.e., the purchaser): “I am selling to you an area requiring a Khor of seed of soil within these markers and these boundaries.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 7:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/7:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/7:3:4)


**הָכִי גָּרְסִינַן פָּחוֹת מִשְּׁתוּת הִגִּיעוֹ עַד שְׁתוּת יְנַכֶּה.** וְהָכִי פֵּרוּשָׁא, אִם פִּחֵת מִן הַבֵּית כּוֹר שְׁתוּת וְכָל שֶׁכֵּן פָּחוֹת מִשְּׁתוּת, הִגִּיעוֹ. אֲבָל יוֹתֵר עַל שְׁתוּת וּלְמַטָּה עַד שְׁתוּת וְלֹא שְׁתוּת בִּכְלָל, יְנַכֶּה:


[הכי גרסינן] פחות משתות הגיעו [עד] שתות ינכה – This is the interpretation: if he lessened it by one-sixth of the area of a Khor of seed, and all the more so, less than one-sixth, he takes possession of it, but more than one-sixth and below up until one-sixth, but not one-sixth included, he deducts from it.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 7:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/7:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/7:4:1)


**מְשַׁמְּנִין בֵּינֵיהֶן.** רוֹאִין הַמָּקוֹם הַשָּׁמֵן שֶׁבְּשָׂדֶה זוֹ, וְנוֹטֵל אוֹתוֹ הַמּוֹכֵר, דְּיַד לוֹקֵחַ עַל הַתַּחְתּוֹנָה. וּכְנֶגְדּוֹ נוֹתֵן לַלּוֹקֵחַ מִן הַמָּקוֹם הַגָּרוּעַ כְּמוֹ שֶׁשָּׁוֶה הַמָּקוֹם הַשָּׁמֵן שֶׁנָּטַל הַמּוֹכֵר. דְּכִי קָאָמַר לֵיהּ חֲצִי שָׂדֶה אֲנִי מוֹכֵר לְךָ, כְּדֵי דְּמֵי חֲצִי שָׂדֶה קָאָמַר לֵיהּ. וְאִם הָיְתָה בַּת מֵאָה אַמָּה בְּמֵאָה דִּינָרִין, וְיֵשׁ בָּהּ שְׁלֹשִׁים אַמָּה מָקוֹם שָׁמֵן שֶׁשָּׁוֶה חֲמִשִּׁים דִּינָרִין, נוֹטְלוֹ הַמּוֹכֵר, וְנוֹתֵן לַלּוֹקֵחַ שִׁבְעִים אַמָּה מִן הַגָּרוּעַ בַּחֲמִשִּׁים דִּינָרִין. וְנַפְקָא מִנַּהּ, דְּרוֹצֶה אָדָם בְּקַב עִדִּית מִשְּׁנֵי קַבִּין זִבּוּרִית, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁדְּמֵי שְׁנֵיהֶן שָׁוִין:


משמנין ביניהן – they see the place of the fatter/better part of the field, and the seller takes that part, for the hand of the purchaser is at a disadvantage, and corresponding to it, he (i.e., the seller) gives to the boundarTalmud Bava Batra 107b). For when he (i.e., the seller) said to him (i.e., the purchaser): “I am selling you one-half of the field,” according to the worth of the half of the field, he said to him. And if it was one-hundred cubits [in total] for one hundred denars, but within it are thirty cubits which is the fatter/better part worth fifty denars, the seller takes that, and gives to the purchaser the seventy cubits which are the from the worst part for fifty denars. And the practical difference is that a person wants a Kab of the best took hold of the first language or the latter language, and the purchaser takes the less of the


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 7:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/7:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/7:4:2)


**חֶצְיָהּ שֶׁבַּדָּרוֹם אֲנִי מוֹכֵר לְךָ.** אֵין שָׁמִין כָּל הַשָּׂדֶה כְּהַהִיא דְּרֵישָׁא, אֶלָּא שָׁמִין כַּמָּה שָׁוֶה חֲצִי הַשָּׂדֶה שֶׁבַּדָּרוֹם בֵּין טוֹב וּבֵין רַע, וּכְנֶגֶד מַה שֶּׁשָּׁוֶה חֶצְיָהּ שֶׁבַּדָּרוֹם נוֹתֵן לוֹ מוֹכֵר לַלּוֹקֵחַ בְּכָל רוּחַ שֶׁיִּרְצֶה מִן הַגָּרוּעַ שֶׁיֶּשׁ לוֹ. דְּיַד לוֹקֵחַ עַל הַתַּחְתּוֹנָה, וְשֶׁמָּא הָכִי קָאָמַר לֵיהּ, מַה שֶּׁשָּׁוֶה חֲצִי שֶׁבַּדָּרוֹם אֲנִי מוֹכֵר לְךָ בַּקַּרְקַע שֶׁלִּי בְּכָל מָקוֹם שֶׁאֶרְצֶה:


חציה שבדרום אני מוכר לך – we do not compromise to average all of the field as it is in the first part [of the Mishnah], but rather, we estimate what half of the field in the South is worth, whether good or bad, and corresponding to what one-half of the field is in the South, the seller gives to the purchaser in any direction what he desires from the worst that he has, for the hand of the purchaser is at a disadvantage, and perhaps, this is what he said to him: “What is worth half that is in the South, I sell you from my land in any place that I desire.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 7:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/7:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/7:4:3)


**וּמְקַבֵּל עָלָיו.** הַלּוֹקֵחַ, לָתֵת מָקוֹם בַּחֲצִי שָׂדֶה זֶה שֶׁקָּנָה לַעֲשׂוֹת גָּדֵר, וְחָרִיץ וּבֶן חֲרִיץ, תַּרְוַיְהוּ אֲחוֹרֵי הַגָּדֵר מִבַּחוּץ, כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא תְּהֵא חַיָּה קוֹפֶצֶת וְנִכְנֶסֶת בַּשָּׂדֶה. דְּאִי עֲבִיד חֲרִיץ וְלֹא עֲבִיד בֶּן חֲרִיץ, אַיְדֵי דַּחֲרִיץ רָוַח, נִכְנֶסֶת בְּתוֹכוֹ וְקוֹפֶצֶת לְמַעְלָה עַל שְׂפָתוֹ הַשֵּׁנִי. וְאִי עֲבִיד בֶּן חֲרִיץ וְלֹא עֲבִיד חֲרִיץ, אַיְדֵי דְּקָטִין קַיְמָא אַשְּׂפָתָא וְקָפְצָא. וְכַמָּה בֵּין חֲרִיץ לְבֵין בֶּן חֲרִיץ, טֶפַח:


ומקבל עליו – the purchaser with his one-half field that he purchased to make a fence, and/or a large ditch or a small ditch. Both of them are behind the fence from the outside, in order that no wildlife not jump in and enter the field, for if he made a large ditch, he does not make a small ditch, for since the ditch is wide, it enters into it and jumps above its second rim, but if he made a small ditch and did not make a large ditch, since it is small, there exists garbage and it jumps. And how much is the distance between one small ditch and another small ditch? A handbreadth.



## Chapter 8


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 8:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:1:1)


**יֵשׁ נוֹחֲלִין.** יֵשׁ קְרוֹבִים שֶׁנּוֹחֲלִין אֶת קְרוֹבֵיהֶם כְּשֶׁהֵם מֵתִים. וְאַף כְּשֶׁמֵּתִים הֵם, מַנְחִילִין לָהֶם אֶת מָמוֹנָם:


יש נוחלין – there are relations that inherit their relatives when they die, and even when they themselves die. They bequeath to them their money.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 8:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:1:2)


**הָאָב אֶת הַבָּנִים.** דִּכְתִיב (במדבר כז) אִישׁ כִּי יָמוּת וּבֵן אֵין לוֹ וְהַעֲבַרְתֶּם אֶת נַחֲלָתוֹ לְבִתּוֹ, בִּמְקוֹם בַּת אַתָּה מַעֲבִיר נַחֲלָה מֵאָב, וְאִי אַתָּה מַעֲבִיר נַחֲלָה מִן הָאָב בִּמְקוֹם אַחִין:


האב את הבנים – as it is written (Numbers 27:8): “If a man dies without leaving a son, you shall transfer his property to his daughter.” In place of a daughter, you transfer the inheritance from the father, but you do not transfer inheritance from the father in place of brothers.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 8:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:1:3)


**וְהַבָּנִים אֶת הָאָב.** דִּכְתִיב אִישׁ כִּי יָמוּת וּבֵן אֵין לוֹ, הָא יֵשׁ לוֹ בֵּן, בֵּן קוֹדֵם:


והבנים את האב – as it is written (Numbers 27:8): “If a man dies without leaving a son;” but if he has a son, the son precedes.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 8:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:1:4)


**וְהָאַחִין מִן הָאָב.** דִּכְתִיב (שם) מִמִּשְׁפַּחְתּוֹ וְיָרַשׁ אוֹתָהּ, וּמִשְׁפַּחַת אָב קְרוּיָה מִשְׁפָּחָה, דִּכְתִיב (שם א) לְמִשְׁפְּחוֹתָם לְבֵית אֲבוֹתָם:


והאחין מן האב – as it is written (Numbers 27:10): “in his own clan, and he shall inherit it.” And the family/clan of the father is called a family, as it is written (Numbers 1:2): “by the clans of its ancestral houses.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 8:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:1:5)


**הָאִישׁ אֶת אִמּוֹ.** דִּכְתִיב (במדבר לז) וְכָל בַּת יוֹרֶשֶׁת נַחֲלָה מִמַּטּוֹת בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל, הֵיאַךְ בַּת יוֹרֶשֶׁת שְׁנֵי מַטּוֹת. אֶלָּא זוֹ שֶׁאָבִיהָ מִשֵּׁבֶט אֶחָד וְאִמָּהּ מִשֵּׁבֶט אַחֵר, וִירַשְׁתָּן. וּמִדִּכְתִיב מַטּוֹת, הִקִּישׁ מַטֵּה הָאֵם לְמַטֵּה הָאָב, מַה מַּטֵּה הָאָב בֵּן קוֹדֵם לַבַּת, אַף מַטֵּה הָאֵם בֵּן קוֹדֵם לַבַּת:


והאיש את אמו – as it is written (Numbers 36:8): “ Every daughter among the Israelite tribes who inherits a share [must marry someone from a clan of her father’s tribe, in order that every Israelite may keep his ancestral share],”how a daughter inherits two tribes, but this [is the case] where her father is from one tribe and her mother is from another tribe, "וירשתן"/and she shall inherit them (see Numbers 36:8 and Talmud Bava Batra 111a) – for since it is written (in our verse): "ממטות"/from the tribe, he made an analogy of two verses near each other – the tribe of the mother to the tribe of the father. Just as the tribe of the father, the son precedes the daughter, so too, the tribe of the mother – the son precedes the daughter.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 8:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:1:6)


**וְהָאִישׁ אֶת אִשְׁתּוֹ.** דִּכְתִיב שְׁאֵרוֹ, זוֹ אִשְׁתּוֹ. וְקָרִינַן הָכִי, וּנְתַתֶּם אֶת נַחֲלַת שְׁאֵרוֹ לוֹ, וְגוֹרְעִין וּמוֹסִיפִין וְדוֹרְשִׁין. יָכוֹל אַף הִיא תִּירְשֶׁנּוּ תַּלְמוּד לוֹמַר וְיָרַשׁ אוֹתָהּ, הוּא יוֹרֵשׁ אוֹתָהּ וְאֵין הִיא יוֹרֶשֶׁת אוֹתוֹ:


והאיש את אשתו – as it is written (Numbers 27:11): “[If his father had no brothers, you shall assign his property to] his nearest relative [in his own clan and he shall inherit it].” “the nearest relative” is one’s wife. And we read it this way: “And you shall give the inheritance of his nearest relative to him and deduct or add and we expound, that even she shall inherit him, as the inference teaches us: (Numbers 27: 11): “and he shall inherit it.” He inherits her, but she does not inherit him (see also Talmud Bava Batra 111b).



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 8:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:2:1)


**כָּל הַקּוֹדֵם לַנַּחֲלָה יוֹצְאֵי יְרֵכוֹ קוֹדְמִין.** וְאִם אֵין יוֹצְאֵי יְרֵכוֹ קַיָּמִין, חָזְרָה נַחֲלָתָן אֵצֶל הָאָב. כֵּיצַד, רְאוּבֵן שֶׁמֵּת, חֲנוֹךְ וּפַלּוּא חֶצְרוֹן וְכַרְמִי [בָּנָיו] יוֹרְשִׁין אוֹתוֹ. וְאִם מֵת אֶחָד מִן הַבָּנִים קֹדֶם רְאוּבֵן, וְהַהוּא בֵּן הִנִּיחַ בֵּן אוֹ בַּת [אוֹ בֶּן בֵּן אוֹ בַּת בֵּן] אוֹ בֶּן בַּת אוֹ בַּת בַּת] עַד מֵאָה דּוֹרוֹת, יִירַשׁ בִּמְקוֹם אָבִיו בְּנִכְסֵי אָבִיו רְאוּבֵן, וְיִטֹּל כְּאֶחָד מִבְּנֵי רְאוּבֵן. וְאִם אֵין לִרְאוּבֵן בָּנִים וְלֹא בְּנֵי בָנִים וְאַף לֹא בְּנוֹת בָּנִים, אָז יוֹרְשׁוֹת בְּנוֹת רְאוּבֵן אֶת רְאוּבֵן. וְאִם אֵין לִרְאוּבֵן בָּנוֹת וְלֹא בְּנֵי בָּנוֹת וְלֹא בְּנוֹת בָּנוֹת עַד סוֹף כָּל הַדּוֹרוֹת, אָז יִירַשׁ יַעֲקֹב אֶת רְאוּבֵן בְּנוֹ. וְאִם אֵין יַעֲקֹב קַיָּם, יִירְשׁוּ אֶת רְאוּבֵן אֶחָיו בְּנֵי אָבִיו, שִׁמְעוֹן לֵוִי וִיהוּדָה [יוֹסֵף וּבִנְיָמִין] וְכוּ'. וְאִם אֵינָן קַיָּמִין, יִירְשׁוּ אֶת רְאוּבֵן בְּנֵי שִׁמְעוֹן וְלֵוִי וְכוּ'. וְאִם אֵין לָהֶם בָּנִים וְלֹא בְּנֵי בָנִים, יִירְשׁוּ בְּנוֹתֵיהֶן אוֹ בְּנֵי בְנוֹתֵיהֶן אוֹ בְּנוֹת בְּנוֹתֵיהֶן. דִּבְכָל מָקוֹם הַזָּכָר וְיוֹרְשָׁיו קוֹדְמִין לַנְּקֵבָה. וְאִם מֵתוּ אֶחָיו שֶׁל רְאוּבֵן בְּלֹא זֶרַע אוֹ שֶׁלֹּא הָיָה לוֹ אָח מֵעוֹלָם, תִּירְשֶׁנּוּ אֲחוֹתוֹ דִּינָה בַּת יַעֲקֹב אוֹ בָּנֶיהָ אוֹ בְּנֵי בָנֶיהָ [אוֹ בְּנוֹתֶיהָ וּבְנוֹת בְּנוֹתֶיהָ] עַד סוֹף הָעוֹלָם. וְאִם אֵין לִרְאוּבֵן אָחוֹת וְלֹא זֶרַע אָחוֹת, תַּחֲזֹר יְרֻשָּׁתוֹ לַאֲבִי אָבִיו דְּהַיְנוּ לְיִצְחָק. וְאִם אֵין יִצְחָק קַיָּם, תַּחֲזֹר יְרֻשַּׁת רְאוּבֵן לְעֵשָׂו בֶּן יִצְחָק, שֶׁהוּא אֲחִי אָבִיו שֶׁל רְאוּבֵן. וְאִם אֵין עֵשָׂו קַיָּם, תַּחֲזֹר יְרֻשַּׁת רְאוּבֵן לֶאֱלִיפַז בֶּן עֵשָׂו אוֹ לְבָנָיו וְלִבְנֵי בָנָיו אוֹ לִבְנוֹתָיו וְלִבְנוֹת בְּנוֹתָיו עַד סוֹף כָּל הַדּוֹרוֹת. וְאִם אֵין אַחִים לְאָבִיו שֶׁל רְאוּבֵן וְלֹא מִבְּנֵי בְנֵיהֶן, תַּחֲזֹר יְרֻשַּׁת רְאוּבֵן לַאֲחוֹת אָבִיו אוֹ לְבָנֶיהָ וְלִבְנֵי בָנֶיהָ אוֹ לִבְנוֹתֶיהָ עַל הַסֵּדֶר שֶׁאָמַרְנוּ. וְאִם אֵין לְאָבִיו שֶׁל רְאוּבֵן לֹא אַחִים וְלֹא בְּנֵי אַחִים וְלֹא אָחוֹת וְלֹא בְּנֵי אָחוֹת, תַּחְזֹר יְרֻשַּׁת רְאוּבֵן לְאַבְרָהָם אֲבִי אֲבִי אָבִיו. וְכֵן לְעוֹלָם עַד אָדָם הָרִאשׁוֹן:


כל הקודם לנחלה. יוצאי יריכו קודמין – if his descendants are not living, their inheritance returned with the father. How so? Reuven who died. Hanokh, and Palu, Hezron and Carmi [his sons] inherit him and if one of the sons predeceases Reuven, and that son [who died] left a son or a daughter [or a son of a son or a daughter of a son] or the son of daughter or the daughter of a daughter, until one hundred generations, he shall inherit in place of his father of the property of Reuven his father. And he will take like one of the sons of Reuven and if Reuven doesn’t have any sons, but not sons of sons and even the daughters of sons, for then, the daughters of Reuven would inherit Reuven, but not the daughters of daughters, until all the generations. Then Jacob would inherit his son Reuven. But if Jacob is no longer living, then Reuven’s brothers, the sons of his father (i.e., Jacob) would inherit, Shimon, Levi and Judah [Joseph and Benjamin] [etc. and if they no are not living, the sons of Shimon and Levi, etc. would inherit Reuven]. But if they don’t have sons, nor sons of sons (i.e., grandsons), their daughters would inherit. Or the sons of their daughters or the daughters of their daughters. For in every place, the male and his inheritors precede the female. But if the brothers of Reuven died without progeny, or if he hadn’t ever had a brother, his sister Dinah, the daughter of Jacob would inherit him, or her sons or the sons of her sons [or her daughters and the daughters of her daughters] until the end of the world. But if Reuven does not have a sister nor the progeny of a sister, his inheritance would return to the father of his father, which is Isaac. But if Isaac is not alive, the inheritance would return to Esau the son of Isaac who is the brother of the father of Reuven (i.e., Jacob), and if Esau is not [alive], the inheritance of Reuven would return to Eliphaz the son of Esau, or to his sons, or to his grandsons or to his daughters or to his granddaughters to the end of all generations. But if there are no brothers to the father of Reuven (i.e., Jacob) nor to the sons of his sons, the inheritance of Reuven would return to his father’s sister or to her sons or to the children of her sons or to her daughters according to the order that we stated. But if Reuven’s father does not have either brothers nor grandchildren, nor a sister nor the children of his sister the inheritance of Reuven would return to Abraham, his paternal great-grandfather, and similarly forever until the first human being (i.e., Adam).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 8:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:2:2)


**הָאָב קוֹדֵם.** לְעוֹלָם לַאֲחֵי הָאָב וְלִבְנֵיהֶם, [וַאֲחֵי הָאָב קוֹדְמִים לַאֲחוֹת הָאָב], וַאֲחֵי הָאָב וַאֲחוֹת הָאָב קוֹדְמִים [לַאֲבִי הָאָב. וַאֲבִי הָאָב קוֹדֵם לַאֲחֵי אֲבִי הָאָב. וַאֲחֵי אֲבִי הָאָב וְאַף אֲחוֹתוֹ קוֹדְמִין] לַאֲבִי אֲבִי הָאָב. וְכֵן לְעוֹלָם:


האב קודם – forever to the brothers of the father and to their children [but the brothers of the father precede the sisters of the father] and the brothers of the father and the sisters of the father precede [the father of the father, and the father of the father precedes the brothers of the father’s father and the sisters of the father precede [the father of the father, and the father of the father precedes the brothers of the father’s father, and the brother’s of the father’s father, and even his sisters precede to the father of his father’s father, and similarly forever.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 8:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:3:1)


**נָטְלוּ שְׁלֹשָׁה חֲלָקִים בַּנַּחֲלָה.** בְּנַחֲלַת אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל. וְסָבַר הַאי תַּנָּא לְיוֹצְאֵי מִצְרַיִם נִתְחַלְּקָה הָאָרֶץ, דִּכְתִיב (במדבר כו) לִשְׁמוֹת מַטּוֹת אֲבוֹתָם יִנְחָלוּ. וּצְלָפְחָד וְחֵפֶר אָבִיו שְׁנֵיהֶם נָטְלוּ חֵלֶק בָּאָרֶץ, שֶׁמִּיּוֹצְאֵי מִצְרַיִם הָיוּ שְׁנֵיהֶם. וְנָטְלוּ בְּנוֹת צְלָפְחָד חֵלֶק אֲבִיהֶם שֶׁהָיָה מַגִּיעַ לוֹ בָּאָרֶץ, וְחֵלֶק הַמַּגִּיעַ לוֹ מִירֻשַּׁת חֵפֶר אָבִיו, וְחֵלֶק בְּכוֹרָתוֹ שֶׁהָיָה בְּכוֹר וְנוֹטֵל פִּי שְׁנַיִם. וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁעֲדַיִן לֹא יָרְשׁוּ אֶת הָאָרֶץ וְאֵין הַבְּכוֹר נוֹטֵל פִּי שְׁנַיִם בָּרָאוּי לָבֹא לְאַחַר מִיתָה, אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל מֻחְזֶקֶת הָיְתָה:


נטלו שלשה חלקים בנחלה – in the inheritance of the Land of Israel. And this Tanna/teacher [in the Mishnah] holds that the Land [of Israel] was divided to those who left Egypt, as it is written (Numbers 26:55): “[The land, moreover, is to be apportioned by lot;] and the allotment shall be made according to the listings of their ancestral tribes” (see Talmud Bava Batra 117a). And Tzelophchad and Hefer, his father, took portions in the Land, for both were among those who left Egypt. But the daughters of Tzelophchad took the portion of their father that would become his property in the Land. And the portion that would become his from the inheritance of Hefer his father. And the portion of his first-born share as he was a first-born and he takes a double portion. But even though they had not yet inherited the land, the first-born does not take a double portion as is appropriate to come after death. The Land of Israel was held in possession.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 8:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:4:1)


**אֶחָד הַבֵּן וְאֶחָד הַבַּת בַּנַּחֲלָה.** הָכִי קָאָמַר, אֶחָד הַבֵּן וְאֶחָד הַבַּת שָׁוִין בִּירֻשַּׁת נִכְסֵי הָאֵם כְּבִירֻשַּׁת נִכְסֵי הָאָב, וְאֵין הֶפְרֵשׁ בֵּין יְרֻשַּׁת נִכְסֵי הָאֵם לִירֻשַּׁת נִכְסֵי הָאָב, אֶלָּא שֶׁהַבְּכוֹר נוֹטֵל פִּי שְׁנַיִם בְּנִכְסֵי הָאָב וְאֵינוֹ נוֹטֵל פִּי שְׁנַיִם בְּנִכְסֵי הָאֵם:


אחד הבן ואחד הבת בנחלה – this is what he said: both the son and the daughter are equivalent in the inheritance of the property of the mother, [just as in the inheritance] of the property of the father. And there is no difference between the inheritance of the property of the mother to the inheritance of the property of the father other than that the first born son takes a double portion in the property of the father but does not take a double portion in the property of the mother.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 8:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:5:1)


**לֹא אָמַר כְּלוּם.** וְאֵין יָכוֹל לְסַלְּקוֹ מִן הַיְרֻשָּׁה אֶלָּא עַל יְדֵי שֶׁיִּתֵּן בְּמַתָּנָה נְכָסָיו לִשְׁאָר בָּנָיו:


לא אמר כלום – and he is not able to remove hm from the inheritance, other that via that he would give his property to the rest of his sons through a gift.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 8:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:5:2)


**עַל פִּיו.** מִשּׁוּם דִּמְצַוֶּה מֵחֲמַת מִיתָה, וּשְׁכִיב מְרַע דְּבָרָיו כִּכְתוּבִים וְכִמְסוּרִים וְאֵינוֹ צָרִיךְ קִנְיָן. מִשּׁוּם הָכִי תָּנֵי עַל פִּיו:
