על פיו – because it is a Mitzvah in the consequence of a death and a person on his deathbed, his words are like they were written and transmitted and they don’t require an [act of] acquisition, and because of this it is taught “by his mouth”/על פיו.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 8:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:5:3)


**וְהִשְׁוָה לָהֶן אֶת הַבְּכוֹר.** בִּלְשׁוֹן מַתָּנָה:


והשוה להן את הבכור – in the language of a gift.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 8:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:5:4)


**דְּבָרָיו קַיָּמִין.** וְאֵין כָּאן מַתְנֶה עַל מַה שֶּׁכָּתוּב בַּתּוֹרָה, שֶׁיֵּשׁ כֹּחַ בָּאָדָם לִתֵּן מָמוֹנוֹ בְּמַתָּנָה לְכָל מִי שֶׁיִּרְצֶה:


דבריו קיימין – and we don’t have here [the case] of a person making a condition against what is written in the Torah because it is within the power of an individual to give his money as a gift to whomever he desires.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 8:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:5:5)


**וְאִם אָמַר מִשּׁוּם יְרֻשָּׁה.** וְאִם רִבָּה לְאֶחָד וּמִעֵט לְאֶחָד בְּתוֹרַת יְרֻשָּׁה, שֶׁאָמַר פְּלוֹנִי בְּנִי יִירַשׁ שָׂדֶה בֵּית כּוֹר, וּפְלוֹנִי [בְּנִי] יִירַשׁ שָׂדֶה בֵּית לֶתֶךְ, וְעַל בְּנוֹ בְּכוֹר אָמַר שֶׁיִּירַשׁ כְּשֶׁל חֲבֵרוֹ, לֹא אָמַר כְּלוּם, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁמַּתְנֶה עַל מַה שֶּׁכָּתוּב בַּתּוֹרָה:


ואם אמר משום ירושה – and if he increased [the monies] to one and lessened them to another in the designation of inheritance as he said: “So-and-so my son will a field that is an area requiring a Khor of seed, and so-and-so my son will inherit a field that is in an area requiring a one-half Khor of seed. But regarding his first-born son he said that he would inherit like that of his fellow, he did not say anything, for he made a condition that is against what is written in the Torah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 8:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:5:6)


**כָּתַב בֵּין בַּתְּחִלָּה וְכוּ'.** תִּנָּתֵן שָׂדֶה פְּלוֹנִית לִפְלוֹנִי וְיִירָשֶׁנָּה, זֶהוּ בַּתְּחִלָּה. יִירָשֶׁנָּה וְתִנָּתֵן לוֹ, זוֹ הִיא בַּסּוֹף. יִירַשׁ שָׂדֶה פְּלוֹנִית וְתִנָּתֵן לוֹ וְיִירָשֶׁנָּה, זוֹ הִיא מַתָּנָה בָּאֶמְצַע:


כתב בין בתחלה וכ' – “You will give such-and-such a field to son-and-so, and he will inherit it.” This is at the beginning. “He will inherit it and you will give it to him.” This is at the at the end. “He will inherit such-and-such a field and you will give it to him and he will inherit it. This is a gift in the middle.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 8:5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:5:7)


**אִם אָמַר עַל מִי שֶׁרָאוּי לְיָרְשׁוֹ.** כְּגוֹן עַל בֵּן בֵּין הַבָּנִים, וְעַל בַּת בֵּין הַבָּנוֹת, פְּלוֹנִי יִירָשֵׁנִי:


אם אמר על מי שראוי ליורשו – as for example, regarding a son among the sons, or regarding a daughter among the daughters, so-and-so will inherit me, his words are fulfilled, as it is written (Deuteronomy 21:16):”When he wills his property to his sons [he may not treat as first-born the son of the loved one in disregard of the son of the unloved one who is older].” The Torah gave permission to the father to bequeath to the sons to whomever he desires. And Rabbi Yohanan [Ben Beroka] agrees regarding a brother in place of a daughter, and regarding a daughter in place of a son, he has not said anything for the daughter is not worthy of inheriting in place of a son nor is the brother [worthy of inheriting] in place of a daughter. And similarly, Rabbi Yohanan [ben Beroka] agrees that if he made the first born equivalent to the [other] sons, he has not said anything, as it is written (Deuteronomy 21:16): “he may not treat as first-born the son of the loved one.” And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yohanan ben Beroka.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 8:5:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:5:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:5:8)


**דְּבָרָיו קַיָּמִין.** דִּכְתִיב (דברים כא) וְהָיָה בְּיוֹם הַנְחִילוֹ אֶת בָּנָיו, הַתּוֹרָה נָתְנָה רְשׁוּת לָאָב לְהַנְחִיל לַבָּנִים לְמִי שֶׁיִּרְצֶה. וּמוֹדֶה רַבִּי יוֹחָנָן דְּעַל אָח בִּמְקוֹם בַּת וְעַל בַּת בִּמְקוֹם בֵּן, לֹא אָמַר כְּלוּם, שֶׁאֵין הַבַּת רְאוּיָה לִירַשׁ בִּמְקוֹם בֵּן, וְלֹא הָאָח בִּמְקוֹם בַּת. וְכֵן מוֹדֶה רַבִּי יוֹחָנָן, שֶׁאִם הִשְׁוָה אֶת הַבְּכוֹר לַבָּנִים לֹא אָמַר כְּלוּם, דִּכְתִיב (שם) לֹא יוּכַל לְבַכֵּר וְכוּ'. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹחָנָן בֶּן בְּרוֹקָה:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 8:5:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:5:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:5:9)


**אֵין רוּחַ חֲכָמִים נוֹחָה הֵימֶנּוּ.** אֵין לַחֲכָמִים נַחַת רוּחַ בְּמַעֲשָׂיו, וַאֲפִלּוּ אֵין בָּנָיו נוֹהֲגִים כַּשּׁוּרָה, דִּלְמָא נָפֵיק מִנַּיְהוּ זַרְעָא מַעַלְיָא. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן בֶּן גַּמְלִיאֵל:


אין רוח חכמים נוחה הימנו – The Sages have no pleasure from his actions, and even if his sons did not conduct themselves appropriately. Perhaps, there will arise from them a higher-level seed. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Shimon ben Gamaliel.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 8:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:6:1)


**זֶה בְּנִי נֶאֱמָן.** לְיוֹרְשׁוֹ וְלִפְטֹר אֶת אִשְׁתּוֹ מִן הַיִּבּוּם:


זה בני נאנון – to inherit him and to exempt his wife from levirate marriage.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 8:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:6:2)


**זֶה אָחִי אֵינוֹ נֶאֱמָן.** לְהוֹרִישׁוֹ עִם אֶחָיו, שֶׁהֲרֵי אֵינָן מַכִּירִים אוֹתוֹ:


זה אחי אינו נאמן – to cause him to inherit with his brothers, for they do not recognize him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 8:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:6:3)


**וְנוֹטֵל עִמּוֹ בְּחֶלְקוֹ.** כְּגוֹן אִם הֵם שְׁנֵי אַחִים לְבַד הַסָּפֵק, וְיֵשׁ לָהֶם לְחַלֵּק שָׁלֹשׁ שָׂדוֹת, זֶה נוֹטֵל שָׂדֶה וּמֶחֱצָה וְזֶה נוֹטֵל שָׂדֶה וּמֶחֱצָה, וְזֶה שֶׁמֵּעִיד עַל הַסָּפֵק שֶׁהוּא אָחִיו, נוֹתֵן לוֹ חֲצִי הַשָּׂדֶה, וְהָאַחֵר אֵינוֹ נוֹתֵן לוֹ כְּלוּם, דְּאָמַר לֵיהּ הָבֵא רְאָיָה וּשְׁקוֹל:


ונוטל עמו בחלקו – as for example, if they are two brothers, besides the doubt, and they have to divide three fields, This one takes a field and one-half, and that one takes a field and one-half, and the third one testifies on the doubt that he is their brother, he gives him one-half of the field, but the other does not give him anything, or he says to him: “bring proof and then take.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 8:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:6:4)


**מֵת.** הַסָּפֵק:


מת – [the one about whom there is] doubt.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 8:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:6:5)


**יַחְזְרוּ.** אוֹתָן הַנְּכָסִים שֶׁנָּתַן לוֹ אָחִיו:


יחזרו – those properties that his brothers gave him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 8:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:6:6)


**לִמְקוֹמָן.** כְּלוֹמַר לְמִי שֶׁנְּתָנָם לוֹ. אֲבָל שְׁאָר אַחִים אֵין יוֹרְשִׁים עִמּוֹ:


למקומן – meaning to say, to whomever had given them to him, but the other brothers do not inherit with him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 8:6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:6:7)


**נָפְלוּ לוֹ.** לַסָּפֵק:


נפלו לו – to the one [about whom there is] doubt.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 8:6:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:6:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:6:8)


**נְכָסִים מִמָּקוֹם אַחֵר.** בְּחַיָּיו. אוֹ קָנָה נְכָסִים וְעַכְשָׁיו מֵת:


נכסים ממקום אחר – during his lifetime; or he bought property and now is dead.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 8:6:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:6:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:6:9)


**יִירְשׁוּ אֶחָיו.** שֶׁל זֶה שֶׁהָיָה מֵעִיד שֶׁהוּא אָחִיו:


ירשו אחיו – of the one who testifies that he is their brother.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 8:6:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:6:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:6:10)


**עִמּוֹ.** דְּהָא קָא מוֹדִי לְהוּ שֶׁאֲחִיהֶם הוּא. וְהָנֵי מִלֵּי בִּדְלָא כָּפְרוּ שְׁאָר הָאַחִין לוֹמַר וַדַּאי שֶׁאֵינוֹ אֲחִיהֶן, אֶלָּא טוֹעֲנִים שֶׁאֵינָן מַכִּירִים אוֹתוֹ. אֲבָל אִם כָּפְרוּ בּוֹ שֶׁאֵינוֹ אֲחִיהֶם, לֹא יִירָשֶׁנּוּ אֶלָּא זֶה שֶׁהֵעִיד לוֹ בִּלְבַד:


עמו – for surely he admits to them that he is their brother, but these words [concern] when the rest of the brothers did not deny to state that he definitely is not their brother, but rather claim that they don’t recognize him, but if they deny that he is their brother, they do not inherit him other than the one who testified about him alone.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 8:6:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:6:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:6:11)


**דְּיָתֵיקֵי.** צַוָּאַת שְׁכִיב מְרַע. וּלְשׁוֹן דְּיָתֵיקֵי דָּא תְּהֵא לְמֵיקַם וּלְמֵהֱוֵי:


דיאתיקי – a will of someone on his deathbed. And the language of דיאתיקי is this what shall be established and come to pass.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 8:6:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:6:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:6:12)


**קְשׁוּרָה עַל יְרֵכוֹ.** דְּלֵיכָּא לְמֵימַר אַחֵר כְּתָבָהּ וּנְתָנָהּ שָׁם, אֲפִלּוּ הָכִי הֲרֵי זוֹ אֵינָהּ כְּלוּם, שֶׁהֲרֵי לֹא גָּמַר לְהַקְנוֹת אֶלָּא בְּקַבָּלַת הַשְּׁטָר, וְאֵין שְׁטָר לְאַחַר מִיתָה:


קשורה לו ביריכו – that is not to say that after it was written and placed there, even so it is worthless, for he did not complete to transfer possession other than with the receipt of a document, but there is no document after death.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 8:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:7:1)


**צָרִיךְ שֶׁיִּכְתֹּב מֵהַיּוֹם וּלְאַחַר מִיתָה.** דְּמַשְׁמַע גּוּף הַקַּרְקַע יְהֵא קָנוּי לְךָ מֵהַיּוֹם, וְלֹא תֹאכַל הַפֵּרוֹת עַד לְאַחַר מִיתָה. וְאִם לֹא כָּתַב מֵהַיּוֹם, לֹא נָתַן לוֹ כְּלוּם, דְּאֵין מַתָּנָה לְאַחַר מִיתָה:


צריך שיכתוב מהיום ולאחר מיתה – that implies the body of the land will be acquired by you from today, but you will not eat of the fruits until after death, but if he did not write, “from today,” he did not give him anything for there is no gift after death.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 8:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:7:2)


**אֵינוֹ צָרִיךְ.** לִכְתֹּב מֵהַיּוֹם. דְּכֵיוָן שֶׁכָּתַב בַּשְּׁטָר בְּכָךְ וְכָךְ בְּשַׁבָּת אָמַר לָנוּ פְּלוֹנִי הֲווּ עָלַי עֵדִים, זְמַנּוֹ שֶׁל שְׁטָר מוֹכִיחַ עָלָיו שֶׁמֵּאוֹתוֹ הַיּוֹם הִתְחִילָה הַמַּתָּנָה, דְּאִי לָא תֵימָא הָכִי, זְמַן שֶׁנִּכְתַּב בַּשְּׁטָר לָמָּה נִכְתַּב. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


אינו צריך – [it is not necessary] to write “from today, for since he wrote in the document: “on such-and-such day of the week, so-and-so told us, “be for me witnesses,” the date [and time] of the document proves that from that day, the gift began. But if he did not say this, the time that is written in the document – for what purposes was it written? And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yosi.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 8:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:7:3)


**לְאַחַר מוֹתוֹ.** מֵהַיּוֹם וּלְאַחַר מִיתָה:


לאחר מותו – from today and after death.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 8:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:7:4)


**הָאָב אֵינוֹ יָכוֹל לִמְכֹּר.** בְּלֹא הַבֵּן. שֶׁהַגּוּף קָנוּי לַבֵּן:


האב אינו יכול למכור – without the son, for the body [of the estate] was acquired by the son.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 8:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:7:5)


**וְהַבֵּן אֵינוֹ יָכוֹל לִמְכֹּר.** בְּלֹא הָאָב. שֶׁהַפֵּרוֹת קְנוּיִין לָאָב:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 8:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:7:6)


**מָכַר הָאָב.** סְתָמָא, מְכוּרִים הַפֵּרוֹת לַלּוֹקֵחַ עַד שֶׁיָּמוּת הָאָב:


מכר האב – [the father sold] in an undefined manner the fruits are sold to the purchaser, until the father dies.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 8:7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:7:7)


**מָכַר הַבֵּן.** בְּחַיֵּי הָאָב, אֵין לַלּוֹקֵחַ פֵּרוֹת עַד שֶׁיָּמוּת הָאָב:


מכר הבן – during the lifetime of the father, [the son sold it], the purchaser does not have the fruits until the father dies.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 8:7:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:7:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:7:8)


**הָאָב.** שֶׁכָּתַב נְכָסָיו לִבְנוֹ מֵהַיּוֹם וּלְאַחַר מִיתָה:


האב – who wrote that his property is to go to his son from today and after death, may pluck up and feed the fruit to whomever he desires during his lifetime, but what he left that is attached [is deposed] at the time of death. Even though that it stands to be plucked up, they belong to the son who receives the gift. But he who writes that all of his property is to go to someone else, even what he had left attached to the ground at the time of his death, it belongs to the inheritors, for the intention of a person is closer to his son than to anyone else.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 8:7:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:7:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:7:9)


**תּוֹלֵשׁ וּמַאֲכִיל.** הַפֵּרוֹת לְמִי שֶׁיִּרְצֶה בְּחַיָּיו. אֲבָל מַה שֶּׁהִנִּיחַ בִּמְחֻבָּר בִּשְׁעַת מִיתָה אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁעוֹמֵד לִתָּלֵשׁ הֲרֵי הֵן שֶׁל בְּנוֹ מְקַבֵּל הַמַּתָּנָה. אֲבָל הַכּוֹתֵב נְכָסָיו לְאַחֵר, אֲפִלּוּ מַה שֶּׁהִנִּיחַ בִּמְחֻבָּר לַקַּרְקַע בִּשְׁעַת מִיתָתוֹ הֲרֵי הוּא שֶׁל יוֹרְשִׁים, שֶׁדַּעְתּוֹ שֶׁל אָדָם קְרוֹבָה אֵצֶל בְּנוֹ יוֹתֵר מֵאַחֵר:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 8:7:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:7:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:7:10)


**מִתְפַּרְנְסִין.** לְבוּשׁ וּכְסוּת. לְפִי שֶׁפַּרְנָסַת הַגְּדוֹלִים מְרֻבָּה מִפַּרְנָסַת הַקְּטַנִּים:


מתפרנסין – garments and clothing because the support of the older children is greater than the support of the younger children.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 8:7:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:7:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:7:11)


**נִזּוֹנִים.** אֲכִילָה וּשְׁתִיָּה. לְפִי שֶׁמְּזוֹנוֹת הַקְּטַנִּים מְרֻבִּים מִשֶּׁל גְּדוֹלִים, שֶׁאוֹכְלִים פְּעָמִים רַבּוֹת וּמְפַזְּרִים, לְפִיכָךְ הַקְּטַנִּים מְעַכְּבִים עַל הַגְּדוֹלִים מִלְּהִתְפַּרְנֵס מִתְּפוּסַת הַבַּיִת, וְהַגְּדוֹלִים מְעַכְּבִין עַל הַקְּטַנִּים מִלִּזּוֹן, אֶלָּא יִתְפַּרְנֵס וְיִזּוֹן כָּל אֶחָד מֵחֶלְקוֹ:


נזונים – food and drink, because the food of the younger children is greater than that of the older children, who eat many times and scatter. Therefore, the younger children prevent the older children from supporting themselves from that which belongs to the estate of the house, and the older children prevent the younger children from being fed, but rather, each one is supported and fed from his portion.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 8:7:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:7:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:7:12)


**נָשְׂאוּ גְּדוֹלִים.** וְעָשׂוּ צָרְכֵי חֻפָּה מִתְּפוּסַת הַבַּיִת לְאַחַר מִיתַת אֲבִיהֶם:


נשאו גדולים – they made all the needs of the wedding from the estate of the house after the death of their father, the younger children will also get married with [the support of] the estate of the house.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 8:7:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:7:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:7:13)


**יִשְּׂאוּ גַּם הַקְּטַנִּים.** מִתְּפוּסַת הַבַּיִת:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 8:7:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:7:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:7:14)


**וְאִם אָמְרוּ הַקְּטַנִּים.** לְאַחַר מִיתַת אֲבִיהֶם, הֲרֵי אָנוּ נוֹשְׂאִים כְּדֶרֶךְ שֶׁנְּשָׂאתֶם אַתֶּם בְּחַיֵּי אָבִינוּ:


ואם אמרו הקטנים – behold, we will get married in the manner that you married during the lifetime of our father, we don’t listen to them, but rather, what their father gave them during his lifetime, he gave [them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 8:7:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:7:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:7:15)


**אֵין שׁוֹמְעִין לָהֶם.** אֶלָּא מַה שֶּׁנָּתַן לָהֶם אֲבִיהֶם בְּחַיָּיו, נָתַן:



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 8:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:8:1)


**הִנִּיחַ בָּנוֹת.** וְלֹא בָּנִים:


הניח בנות – but not sons.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 8:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/8:8:2)


**שֶׁהַבָּנוֹת נִזּוֹנוֹת מִן הַבָּנִים.** בִּתְנָאֵי כְּתֻבָּה. וְאֵין בָּנוֹת קְטַנּוֹת נִזּוֹנוֹת מִן הַגְּדוֹלוֹת. דְּבִמְקוֹם שֶׁאֵין בָּנִים יוֹרְשִׁים אֵין הַבָּנוֹת אוֹכְלוֹת בִּתְנַאי כְּתֻבָּה, אֶלָּא חוֹלְקוֹת בְּשָׁוֶה וְכָל אַחַת נִזּוֹנֵת מִשֶּׁלָּהּ:


שהבנות נזונות מן הבנים – according to the conditions of the Jewish marriage contract/Ketubah, for the young daughters are not supported [at the expense of] the older daughters, and in a place where there are no sons who inherit, the daughters do not eat according to the conditions of the Ketubah but rather they divide it equally, and each one is supported from her own [portion] (see Tractate Ketubot, Chapter 6, Mishnah 6 for a comparison).



## Chapter 9


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 9:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:1:1)


**מִי שֶׁמֵּת.** שֶׁהַנְּכָסִים מְרֻבִּים. כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּזּוֹנוּ הַבָּנִים וְהַבָּנוֹת עַד שֶׁיִּבְגְּרוּ הַבָּנוֹת:


מי שמת. שהנכסים מרובים – in order that the sons will be supported and the daughters until they become adults (i.e., the age of twelve and one-half years – or until they are married, after which the balance of the estate goes to the sons).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 9:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:1:2)


**שֶׁהַנְּכָסִים מֻעָטִין.** שֶׁאֵין בָּהֶם כְּשִׁעוּר זֶה:


שהנכסים מועטים – and they don’t have a measure like this.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 9:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:1:3)


**בִּשְׁבִיל שֶׁאֲנִי זָכָר הִפְסַדְתִּי.** בִּתְמִיהָה. אֶלָּא לֹא אַפְסִיד, וְנִזּוֹן כֻּלָּנוּ יַחַד. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּאַדְמוֹן. וְעָשׂוּ אַלְמָנָה אֵצֶל הַבַּת בִּנְכָסִים מֻעָטִים, כְּבַת אֵצֶל הָאַחִין, מַה בַּת אֵצֶל הָאַחִין הַבַּת נִזּוֹנֵת וְהַבָּנִים יִשְׁאֲלוּ עַל הַפְּתָחִים, אַף אַלְמָנָה אֵצֶל הַבַּת הָאַלְמָנָה נִזּוֹנֵת וְהַבַּת תִּשְׁאַל עַל הַפְּתָחִים:


בשביל שאני זכר הפסדתי – in astonishment. Burt rather, I will not lose out and all of us will be supported together. But the Halakha is not according to Admon. The put the widow with the daughter when the property is small, like a daughter with the brothers. Just as the daughter is with the brothers, the daughter is supported whereas the sons go begging at the doors. Even the widow is with the daughter. The widow receives support and the daughter goes begging at the doors.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 9:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:2:1)


**וְטֻמְטוּם.** סָפֵק זָכָר סָפֵק נְקֵבָה:


וטומטום – doubtfully a male and doubtfully a female.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 9:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:2:2)


**הַזְּכָרִים דּוֹחִין אוֹתוֹ אֵצֶל הַנְּקֵבוֹת.** דְּאָמְרֵי לֵיהּ אַיְתֵי רְאָיָה דְּזָכָר אַתְּ וּשְׁקוֹל:


הזכרין דוחין אותו אצל הנקבות – for they say to him, bring proof that you are a male and take [your portion].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 9:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:2:3)


**הַנְּקֵבוֹת דּוֹחוֹת אוֹתוֹ אֵצֶל הַזְּכָרִים.** דְּאָמְרֵי לֵיהּ אַיְתֵי רְאָיָה דִּנְקֵבָה אַתְּ וְתִזּוֹן עִמָּנוּ:


הנקבות דוחים אותו אצל הזכרים – as they say to hm, bring proof that you are a female and you will be supported with us.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 9:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:2:4)


**יָלְדָה זָכָר נוֹטֵל מָנֶה.** וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּקַיְמָא לָן הַמְזַכֶּה לָעֻבָּר לֹא קָנָה, הָכָא בִּשְׁכִיב מְרַע שֶׁזָּכָה לִבְנוֹ קָנָה, שֶׁדַּעְתּוֹ שֶׁל אָדָם קְרוֹבָה אֵצֶל בְּנוֹ:


ילדה זכר נוטל מנה – and even though we hold that one who makes an assignment to a fetus did not acquire it, here we are dealing with someone on his deathbed who made an assignment to his son, he acquired it, for the temperament of a person is closest with his son.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 9:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:2:5)


**יָלְדָה זָכָר וּנְקֵבָה.** תְּאוֹמִים יַחַד:


ילדה זכר ונקבה – twins together.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 9:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:2:6)


**יָלְדָה טֻמְטוּם אֵינוֹ נוֹטֵל.** הַאי מַתְנִיתִין אִדַּחְיָא לַהּ, וְהִלְכָתָא שֶׁטֻּמְטוּם נוֹטֵל כַּפָּחוּת שֶׁבִּשְׁנֵיהֶם:


ילדה טומטום אינו נוטל – this Mishnah is superseded and the Halakha is that a person of indeterminate sex takes the least of the two of them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 9:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:2:7)


**יוֹרֵשׁ אֶת הַכֹּל.** דְּלָא תֵימָא בִּרְיָה בִּפְנֵי עַצְמָהּ הוּא וְלָאו בַּר יְרֻשָּׁה הוּא, קָמַשְׁמַע לָן:


יורש את הכל – so that you don’t say that this is a creature of its own kind, and not worthy of inheritance. This comes to teach us that this is not the case.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 9:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:3:1)


**הִשְׁבִּיחוּ גְּדוֹלִים אֶת הַנְּכָסִים.** בְּעוֹדָן בִּתְפוּסַת הַבַּיִת:


השביחו גדולים את הנכסים – while they are still belonging to the estate (before division among heirs).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 9:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:3:2)


**הִשְׁבִּיחוּ לָאֶמְצַע.** וְהָנֵי מִלֵּי כְּשֶׁשָּׁבְחוּ הַנְּכָסִים מֵחֲמַת נְכָסִים עַצְמָן, שֶׁלֹּא הוֹצִיאוּ מִשֶּׁלָּהֶן כְּלוּם אֶלָּא מִנִּכְסֵי אֲבִיהֶן שָׂכְרוּ פּוֹעֲלִים וְהִשְׁבִּיחוּ הַנְּכָסִים מֵחֲמַת עַצְמָן. אֲבָל אִם חָפְרוּ וְנָטְעוּ וְהוֹצִיאוּ מִשֶּׁלָּהֶן, מַה שֶּׁהִשְׁבִּיחוּ הִשְׁבִּיחוּ לְעַצְמָן:


השביחו לאמצע – and these words refer, when they improved the estate on account of the estate themselves, for they didn’t take out anything of their own, but only from the estate of their father, they hired workers. And they improved the estate on account of themselves. But if they dug and planted and spent their own money, the improvements they made, they made for themselves.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 9:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:3:3)


**וְכֵן הָאִשָּׁה שֶׁהִשְׁבִּיחָה.** בַּגְּמָרָא מוֹקְמִינַן לַהּ בְּאִשָּׁה יוֹרֶשֶׁת. כְּגוֹן שֶׁנָּשָׂא רְאוּבֵן אֶת בַּת שִׁמְעוֹן אָחִיו, וּמֵת בְּלֹא בָנִים, וּבְנוֹת שִׁמְעוֹן אָחִיו יוֹרְשׁוֹת אוֹתוֹ, נִמְצֵאת אִשְׁתּוֹ שֶׁהִיא בַּת שִׁמְעוֹן אָחִיו יוֹרֶשֶׁת אוֹתוֹ עִם שְׁאָר אַחְיוֹתֶיהָ, וְסָלְקָא דַּעְתָּךְ אֲמֵינָא דִּבְהַהִיא הֲנָאָה דְּנָפֵיק עֲלַהּ קָלָא שֶׁשּׁוֹלֶטֶת וּמַשְׁבַּחַת אֲפִלּוּ כִּי אָמְרָה רְאוּ מַה הִנִּיחַ לִי בַּעְלִי וְכוּ'‏ אֲפִלּוּ הָכִי מָחֲלָה וְרוֹצָה שֶׁיִּהְיֶה הַשֶּׁבַח לָאֶמְצַע, קָמַשְׁמַע לָן דְּהִשְׁבִּיחָה לְעַצְמָהּ:


וכן אשה שהשביחה – In the Gemara (Tractate Bava Batra 144a) we establish it with a woman inheriting such as the case of Reuven who married the daughter of Shimon, his brother. But alas, he died without having any children. And the daughters of Shimon, his brother, inherit him. It is found that his wife, who is the daughter of Shimon, his brother, inherits him with the rest of her sisters. And you might say that with this benefit that she derives due to the emerging rumor that she manages [her husband’s property] and improves it, even if she had said, “see what my husband left me etc.,” even so, she renounced it and wants to improve it for the common fund/estate (and divide the profit equally). But it comes to teach it that she improved it for herself.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 9:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:4:1)


**שֶׁנָּפַל אֶחָד מֵהֶן לָאֻמָּנוּת.** לַעֲבוֹדַת הַמֶּלֶךְ. שֶׁכֵּן מִנְהַג הַמֶּלֶךְ לְהַעֲמִיד מִכָּל בָּתֵּי הָעִיר אָדָם אֶחָד לְמוֹכֵס חֹדֶשׁ אֶחָד אוֹ שְׁנֵי חֳדָשִׁים:


שנפל אחד [מהן] לאומנות – to the service of the king, for it is the custom of the king to appoint from all the houses of the city one person [to serve] as custom-collector/publican for one month or two months.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 9:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:4:2)


**נָפַל לָאֶמְצַע.** כָּל הָרֶוַח. מִפְּנֵי שֶׁמֵּחֲמַת אֲבִיהֶם בָּאָת לוֹ. אֲבָל אִם מֵחֲמַת עַצְמוֹ, מִפְּנֵי חֲרִיפוּתוֹ וַחֲשִׁיבוּתוֹ וְחָכְמָתוֹ נִלְקַח לַעֲבוֹדַת הַמֶּלֶךְ, מַה שֶּׁהִרְוִיחַ הִרְוִיחַ לְעַצְמוֹ:


נפל לאמצע – all the profit, because it was on account of his father that it came time, but if was on his account, because of his sharpness and importance and wisdom that he was taken into the service of he king, what he earned, the profit is for himself.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 9:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:4:3)


**חָלָה וְנִתְרַפָּא.** אִם חָלָה בְּאֹנֶס, כְּלוֹמַר שֶׁלֹּא פָּשַׁע הוּא בְּעַצְמוֹ, מִתְרַפֵּא מִן הָאֶמְצַע. אֲבָל אִם חָלָה מִצִּנִּים פַּחִים וְכַיּוֹצֵא בָהֶם, דִּכְתִיב בְּהוּ (משלי כב) שׁוֹמֵר נַפְשׁוֹ יִרְחַק מֵהֶם, בְּהָא אָמְרִינַן בְּמַתְנִיתִין דְּאֵינוֹ מִתְרַפֵּא אֶלָּא מִשֶּׁל עַצְמוֹ:


חלה ונתרפא – if he became sick by accident, meaning to say, that he was not negligent with himself and he is cured from the estate, but if he got sick from blowing cold winds/cold draughts, and similar things, and it is written concerning it (Proverbs 22:5): “Thorns and snares are in the path of the crooked; [He who values his life will keep far from them],” in this we state in our Mishnah that he is not healed other than from what is his.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 9:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:4:4)


**שׁוּשְׁבִינוּת.** דֶּרֶךְ שׁוּשְׁבִינוּת נוֹטֵל סְעוּדָה וְדוֹרוֹן לַחֻפָּה וְאוֹכֵל עִמּוֹ, וְזֶה חוֹזֵר וְעוֹשֶׂה כֵן לָזֶה גַּם כֵּן כְּשֶׁיִּשָּׂא אִשָּׁה. וְאִם שָׁלַח הָאָב שׁוּשְׁבִינוּת בִּסְתָם עַל יַד אֶחָד מִבָּנָיו וְחָזְרָה הַשּׁוּשְׁבִינוּת אַחַר כָּךְ לְאַחַר שֶׁמֵּת הָאָב, חָזְרָה לָאֶמְצַע, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהִיא כְּמִלְוָה, שֶׁהֲרֵי נִגְבֵּית בְּבֵית דִּין, שֶׁיָּכוֹל הַמּוֹלִיךְ שׁוּשְׁבִינוּת לַחֲבֵרוֹ לַחֲזֹר וְלִתְבֹּעַ מִמֶּנּוּ בְּבֵית דִּין שֶׁיִּתֵּן לוֹ שׁוּשְׁבִינוּת כְּדֶרֶךְ שֶׁנָּתַן לוֹ. וְהוּא שֶׁיִּהְיוּ נִשּׂוּאֵי הַשֵּׁנִי כְּנִשּׂוּאֵי הָרִאשׁוֹן, אִם בְּתוּלָה בְּתוּלָה, וְאִם אַלְמָנָה אַלְמָנָה, וְאִם פַרְהֶסְיָא פַרְהֶסְיָא, וְאִם בְּצִנְעָא בְּצִנְעָא. שֶׁיָּכוֹל הַשֵּׁנִי לוֹמַר לָרִאשׁוֹן, אֵינִי עוֹשֶׂה עִמְּךָ אֶלָּא כְּדֶרֶךְ שֶׁעָשִׂיתָ עִמִּי:


שושבינות – it is the manner of wedding gifts (see Talmud Bava Batra 144b) that one takes a meal and a gift to the wedding and eats with him, and the other returns and does the same for that one when he marries a woman. But if the father sent the wedding gift undefined via the hand of one of his sons, and the wedding gift returned after that following the death of the father, it returned to the estate, because it is like a loan, for it can be collected in the Jewish court of law, For a person who brings a wedding gift to his friend can go back and claim the wedding gift from him in a Jewish court, that he should give him the wedding gift in the manner that he had given it to him. And it will be that the second marriage should be like the first marriage: if she is a virgin, she is a virgin, if she is a widow, she will be a widow, and if in public, it will be in public, and if it is private, it will be in private, for the second can say to the first, “I will not do with you other than in the manner that you did for me.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 9:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:4:5)


**אֲבָל הַשּׁוֹלֵחַ לַחֲבֵרוֹ כַּדֵּי יַיִן וְכַדֵּי שֶׁמֶן.** בְּלֹא חֻפָּה. אוֹ אִי נַמִּי בְּחֻפָּה וְאֵינוֹ הוֹלֵךְ לֶאֱכֹל עִמּוֹ, אֵין זוֹ שׁוּשְׁבִינוּת אֶלָּא מַתָּנָה, וְאֵינָהּ נִגְבֵּית בְּבֵית דִּין:


אבל השולח לחבירו כדי יין וכדי שמן – without a wedding, or alternatively, at a wedding, and he doesn’t go to eat with him, this is not wedding gifts, but rather a [personal] gift, and they are not collected in the Jewish court.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 9:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:5:1)


**הַשּׁוֹלֵחַ סִבְלוֹנוֹת.** מִנְהַג חֲתָנִים לְמָחֳרַת הַקִּדּוּשִׁין שׁוֹלְחִין לְבֵית הָאֲרוּסָה תַּכְשִׁיטִים וּמִינֵי מִגְדָּנוֹת וְכַדֵּי יַיִן וְכַדֵּי שֶׁמֶן, וּפְעָמִים הוֹלֵךְ הֶחָתָן וְאוֹכֵל שָׁם:


השולח סבלונות – it is the custom of grows on the morrow of the betrothal that they send to the house of the betrothed ornaments and kinds of precious goods and pitchers of wine and pitchers of oil, and sometimes, the groom goes to eat there.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 9:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:5:2)


**אֵינָן נִגְבִּין.** אִם מֵת הוּא אוֹ מֵתָה הִיא אוֹ שֶׁבָּא לְגָרְשָׁהּ. דְּמֵחֲמַת חִבַּת שִׂמְחַת אֲכִילָה מָחַל. וְדַוְקָא שֶׁאָכַל בְּדִינָר, אֲבָל אָכַל פָּחוֹת מִדִּינָר לֹא מָחַל וְגוֹבֶה הַסִּבְלוֹנוֹת:


אינן נגבין – if he (i.e., the groom) dies or she (i.e., the bride) dies, or he comes to divorce her, for on account of his love and joy of eating he pardoned, and especially when he at a Denar’s worth, but if he at less than a Denar, he did not pardon and he collects the nuptial gifts.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 9:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:5:3)


**שָׁלַח סִבְלוֹנוֹת מְרֻבִּים.** וַאֲפִלּוּ מֻעָטִים, אִם פֵּרֵשׁ עַל מְנָת שֶׁיָּבֹאוּ עִמָּהּ לְבֵית בַּעְלָהּ הֲרֵי אֵלּוּ נִגְבִּין. וְאוֹרְחָא דְּמִלְּתָא נָקַט, שֶׁדֶּרֶךְ הַסִּבְלוֹנוֹת שֶׁשּׁוֹלְחִין כְּדֵי שֶׁיָּבֹאוּ לְבֵית בַּעְלָהּ לִהְיוֹת מְרֻבִּין, וְאוֹתָן שֶׁשּׁוֹלְחִין כְּדֵי שֶׁתִּתְקַשֵּׁט בָּהֶן בְּבֵית אָבִיהָ דַּרְכָּן לִהְיוֹת מֻעָטִין:


שלח סבלונות מרובים – and even a little, if he explained that on the connection that they come with her, to the house of her husband, then these are collected. But it is the way of things that [the Mishnah] took that it is the manner of nuptial gifts that they send them in order that they will come to the house of the husband, to be many. And those which they send in order that she can adorn herself with them in the house of her father, it is the manner to be few.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 9:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:6:1)


**וְשִׁיֵּר קַרְקַע כָּל שֶׁהוּא.** וְהוּא הַדִּין אִם שִׁיֵּר לְעַצְמוֹ מִטַּלְטְלִין, מַתְּנָתוֹ מַתָּנָה, וְאִם עָמַד אֵינוֹ חוֹזֵר. וְהוּא שֶׁקָּנוּ מִיָּדוֹ עַל אוֹתוֹ מַתָּנָה, דְּמַתְּנַת שְׁכִיב מְרַע בְּמִקְצָת בָּעֵי קִנְיָן בֵּין עָמַד בֵּין לֹא עָמַד. וְאִם לֹא קָנוּ מִיָּדוֹ לֹא קָנָה מְקַבֵּל הַמַּתָּנָה אֲפִלּוּ מֵת הַשְּׁכִיב מְרַע. וְדַוְקָא כְּשֶׁבָּא לְהַקְנוֹת הַמַּתָּנָה מֵחַיִּים. אֲבָל אִם אָמַר יִתְּנוּ לִפְלוֹנִי כָּךְ וְכָךְ אַחַר מוֹתוֹ אֵינוֹ צָרִיךְ קִנְיָן, וַאֲפִלּוּ שִׁיֵּר לְעַצְמוֹ. וּשְׁכִיב מְרַע שֶׁבָּא לִתֵּן מַתָּנָה מֵחַיִּים, אִם לֹא שִׁיֵּר לְעַצְמוֹ כְּלוּם וְעָמַד, חוֹזֵר וַאֲפִלּוּ קָנוּ מִיָּדוֹ. וּמְצַוֶּה מֵחֲמַת מִיתָה דִּשְׁמַעְנָא לֵיהּ דְּקָאָמַר וַי לֵיהּ דְּמָיֵת, לְעוֹלָם חוֹזֵר, בֵּין שִׁיֵּר בֵּין לֹא שִׁיֵּר, וַאֲפִלּוּ קָנוּ מִיָּדוֹ. וְאִם מֵת, קָנָה מְקַבֵּל הַמַּתָּנָה וַאֲפִלּוּ בְּלֹא קִנְיָן:


ושייר קרקע כל שהוא – (see Tractate Peah, Chapter 3, Mishnah 7 for a similar statement at the beginning of the Mishnah) and the same law applies if he left to himself movable, his gift is a gift. But if he stood and doesn’t return and he who acquired something from him on this same gift, as the gift of someone on their deathbed for a portion, requires acquisition, whether standing or not standing. But if he did not acquire it from his hand, the recipient did not acquire the gift, even if the person on his deathbed died. And especially, if he came to transfer/give possession the gift while living. But if he said: “Give to so-and-so this and that after his death,” he doesn’t require acquisition even if he left something to himself. And a person on his deathbed who came to give a a gift while living, if he did not leave anything to himself and stood, it goes back and even if they acquired it from his hand. It is a Mitzvah on account of death that he heard him when he said: “Woe is he who dies.” It (i.e., the gift) always returns [to its owner] whether he left [something to himself] or he did not leave [something to himself], and even if they acquired it from his hand. But if he died, the recipient acquired the gift, and even without acquisition.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 9:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:6:2)


**לֹא כָתַב בָּהּ שְׁכִיב מְרַע.** כַּד קָצִיר וְרָמֵי בְּעַרְסֵיהּ. וְאַף לֹא לְשׁוֹן מַתְּנַת בָּרִיא, כַּד הָלַךְ עַל רַגְלוֹהִי בְּשׁוּקָא. וְלֹא שִׁיֵּר כְּלוּם:


לא כתב שכיב מרע – a pitcher of harvest and throws it into his bier, and even if it is not the language of gift of a healthy individual when he walked in the marketplace on his feet and didn’t leave anything.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 9:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:6:3)


**הוּא אוֹמֵר שְׁכִיב מְרַע הָיִיתִי.** וְחוֹזְרַנִי בִּי. וְהַלָּה אוֹמֵר בָּרִיא הָיִיתָ וְלֹא תַחֲזֹר:


הוא אומר שכיב מרע הייתי – and I have retracted, but the other says: “You were healthy and you cannot retract, he has to bring proof that he was on his deathbed, since after the present we follow, since presently, he is healthy. It is upon him to bring proof that he was on his deathbed at the time of the gift.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 9:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:6:4)


**צָרִיךְ לְהָבִיא רְאָיָה שֶׁהָיָה שְׁכִיב מְרַע.** דְּבָתַר הַשְׁתָּא אַזְלִינַן, דְּהָאִידְנָא בָּרִיא הוּא, וְעָלָיו לְהָבִיא רְאָיָה שֶׁהָיָה שְׁכִיב מְרַע בִּשְׁעַת הַמַּתָּנָה:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 9:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:6:5)


**וַחֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים.** לֹא אַזְלִינַן בָּתַר הַשְׁתָּא אֶלָּא אָמְרִינַן הַמּוֹצִיא מֵחֲבֵרוֹ עָלָיו הָרְאָיָה, וּמְקַבֵּל הַמַּתָּנָה שֶׁבָּא לְהוֹצִיא מִיַּד הַנּוֹתֵן שֶׁהוּא מֻחְזָק, עָלָיו לְהָבִיא רְאָיָה וְעֵדִים שֶׁבָּרִיא הָיָה בְּאוֹתָהּ שָׁעָה. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


וחכמים אומרים – we don’t follow after the present but we say: “the claimant must produce evidence” (see Tractate Bava Kamma, Chapter 3, Mishnah 11 as well as the last line of our Mishnah). And the recipient of a gift who comes to remove it from the hand of the giver who is in possession of it, he must bring proof and witnesses that he (i.e., the giver) was healthy at that time, and the Halakha is according to the Sages.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 9:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:7:1)


**רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר אוֹמֵר אֶחָד בָּרִיא וְאֶחָד מְסֻכָּן.** רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר לֵית לֵיהּ דִּבְרֵי שְׁכִיב מְרַע כִּכְתוּבִין וְכִמְסוּרִין דָּמוּ, וַאֲפִלּוּ מְצַוֶּה מֵחֲמַת מִיתָה סְבִירָא לֵיהּ דְּאֵין מַתְּנָתוֹ מַתָּנָה אֶלָּא בְּקִנְיָן, כְּמַתְּנַת בָּרִיא. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר:


ר' אליעזר אומר אחד בריא ואחד מסוכן – Rabbi Eliezer does not hold by [the statement] that the words of a person on his deathbed are as if they are written and transmitted, and even if one issues directives due to [his imminent] death, he holds that his gift is not a gift, other than through an acquisition, like the gift of a health individual. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Eliezer.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 9:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:7:2)


**כְּבִינָתִי.** תַּרְגּוּם רְדִידִים כְּבִנְתָּא:


כבינתי – it is the Aramaic translation of a brooch of hammered metal.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 9:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:7:3)


**תְּקַבְּרֵם אִמָּם.** כְּלוֹמַר אֵין מְבִיאִין רְאָיָה מֵהֶם, שֶׁרְשָׁעִים הָיוּ, וּקְנָסָא קָנְסוּ רַבָּנָן לַבָּנִים לְקַיֵּם לַבַּת מַתְּנַת אִמָּן, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא הָיָה מִן הַדִּין:


תקברם אמם – meaning to say, they don’t bring a proof from them because they were wicked, and the Rabbis fined the sons to fulfill for the daughter the gift of their mother, even though it was not according to the law.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 9:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:7:4)


**אֲבָל לֹא בְחֹל.** אִם חִלֵּק הַשְּׁכִיב מְרַע נְכָסָיו בְּחֹל:


אבל לא החול – if the person on his deathbed distributed his possessions on a weekday.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 9:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:7:5)


**קַל וָחֹמֶר בְּחֹל.** וְכֵן הֲלָכָה דְּבֵין בְּחֹל בֵּין בְּשַׁבָּת דִּבְרֵי שְׁכִיב מְרַע כִּכְתִיבֵי וְכִמְסִירֵי דָּמוּ, וְאֵין צָרִיךְ קִנְיָן. וְאִם שָׁאַל שֶׁיִּקְנוּ מִיָּדוֹ, קוֹנִים מִמֶּנּוּ בֵּין בְּחֹל בֵּין בְּשַׁבָּת, כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא תִּטָּרֵף דַּעְתּוֹ עָלָיו:


קל וחומה בחול – and such is the Halakha, that whether it is a weekday or Shabbat, the words of a person on his deathbed are as if hey are written and transmitted, and there is no need for an acquisition. But if he asked tha they should acquire it from his hand, they acquire it from it, whether on weekdays or on Shabbat in order that his mind should not be troubled by it.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 9:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:8:1)


**יוֹרְשֵׁי הָאָב אוֹמְרִים הַבֵּן מֵת רִאשׁוֹן.** וְאֵין בַּעַל חוֹב יָכוֹל לְהִפָּרַע מִנְּכָסִים אֵלּוּ, שֶׁהֲרֵי לֹא זָכָה בָּהֶן הַבֵּן מֵעוֹלָם:


יורשי האב אומרים הבן מת ראשון – and the creditor cannot collect from these properties for the son never took possession of them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 9:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:8:2)


**וּבַעֲלֵי הַחוֹב אוֹמְרִים הָאָב מֵת רִאשׁוֹן.** וְנָפְלוּ הַנְּכָסִים שָׁעָה אַחַת לִפְנֵי הַבֵּן, וְהֵם מְשֻׁעְבָּדִים לִכְתֻבַּת אִשָּׁה וּבַעֲלֵי חוֹב:


ובעלי החוב אומרים האב מת ראשון – and the properties fell one hour before the son and they are mortgaged to the marriage contract of his wife and the creditors.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 9:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:8:3)


**בֵּית שַׁמַּאי אוֹמְרִים יַחֲלֹקוּ.** דְּקָסָבְרֵי בֵּית שַׁמַּאי שְׁטָר הָעוֹמֵד לִגָּבוֹת כְּגָבוּי דָּמֵי, וְיוֹרְשֵׁי הָאָב וּבַעֲלֵי חוֹב תַּרְוַיְהוּ מֻחְזָקִים הֲווּ, לְפִיכָךְ יַחֲלֹקוּ:


בית שמאי אומרים יחלוקו – the School of Shammai holds that the document that stands to be collected is considered as collected, and the inheritors of the father and the creditors, both of them are in possession. Therefore, they divide it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 9:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:8:4)


**וּבֵית הִלֵּל אוֹמְרִים.** הַנְּכָסִים בְּחֶזְקַת יוֹרְשִׁים וּכְמֻחְזָקִים דָּמוּ וּבַעַל חוֹב בָּא לְהוֹצִיא מִיָּדָם וְעָלָיו לְהָבִיא רְאָיָה שֶׁהָאָב מֵת תְּחִלָּה:


ובית הלל אומרים – the property is in the possession of the inheritors and is seen as possessed, and the creditor comes to take it from their hands, and he has to bring proof that the father died first.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 9:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:9:1)


**הָאִשָּׁה מֵתָה תְּחִלָּה.** וְאֵין לְיוֹרְשֵׁי הָאִשָּׁה כְּלוּם, שֶׁהֲרֵי הַבַּעַל מֵת אַחֲרוֹן וְיָרַשׁ אֶת אִשְׁתּוֹ:


האשה מתה תחלה – and there is nothing for the inheritors of the wife, for the husband died last and inherited his wife.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 9:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:9:2)


**נְכָסִים בְּחֶזְקָתָן.** נִכְסֵי צֹאן בַּרְזֶל בְּחֶזְקָתָן. וְלֹא פֵּרְשׁוּ בֵּית הִלֵּל אִי בְּחֶזְקַת יוֹרְשֵׁי הָאִשָּׁה שֶׁהָיוּ שֶׁלָּהּ, אִי בְּחֶזְקַת יוֹרְשֵׁי הַבַּעַל שֶׁאַחֲרָיוּתָן עָלָיו, הִלְכָּךְ נִכְסֵי צֹאן בַּרְזֶל יַחֲלֹקוּ:


נכסים בחזקתן – mort-main (i.e., wife’s estate held by her husband, which in case of her death or divorce he must restore “in specie,” being responsible with all his landed property for loss or deterioration) belongs to present occupants but the School of Hillel did not explain if it is in the possession of the inheritors of the wife which were hers or in the possession of the inheritors of the husband for their responsibility is upon him. Therefore, the mort-main is to be divided.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 9:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:9:3)


**וּכְתֻבַּת אִשָּׁה.** שֶׁהֵם מָנֶה מָאתַיִם וְתוֹסֶפֶת, נִשְׁאָרִים בְּחֶזְקַת יוֹרְשֵׁי הַבַּעַל:


וכתובת אשה – which are a Maneh (= 100 silver denars) or two hundred (denars) and a supplement which remain in the possession of the inheritors of the husband.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 9:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:9:4)


**וּנְכָסִים הַנִּכְנָסִים וְיוֹצְאִים עִמָּהּ.** הֵם נִכְסֵי מְלוֹג. שֶׁבְּשָׁעָה שֶׁהִיא נִכְנֶסֶת נִכְנָסִים עִמָּהּ וּכְשֶׁהִיא יוֹצֵאת יוֹצְאִים עִמָּהּ, אִם פָּחֲתוּ פָּחֲתוּ לָהּ וְאִם הוֹתִירוּ הוֹתִירוּ לָהּ. אֵלּוּ הֵם בְּחֶזְקַת יוֹרְשֵׁי הָאִשָּׁה. נִמְצְאוּ יוֹרְשֵׁי הָאִשָּׁה נוֹטְלִים כָּל נִכְסֵי מְלוֹג וַחֲצִי נִכְסֵי צֹאן בַּרְזֶל:


ונכסים הנכנסים והיוצאים עמה – these are the usufruct (i.e., the wife’s estate of which the husband has the fruition without responsibility for loss or deterioration) for at the time that she enters [into marriage], they enter with her, and if they lessened, they lessen for her, and if they increased, they increased for her, it is found that the inheritors of the wife take all the usufruct and half of the mort-main.



### Mishnah 10

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 9:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:10:1)


**נָפַל הַבַּיִת עָלָיו וְעַל אִמּוֹ.** וְאֵין לָהּ בֵּן אַחֵר אֶלָּא זֶה. יוֹרְשֵׁי הַבֵּן אוֹמְרִים הָאִשָּׁה מֵתָה תְּחִלָּה וְיָרַשׁ הַבֵּן נְכָסֶיהָ וְאָנוּ יוֹרְשִׁים הַבֵּן. וְיוֹרְשֵׁי הָאִשָּׁה מִמִּשְׁפַּחַת בֵּית אָבִיהָ אוֹמְרִים הַבֵּן מֵת תְּחִלָּה וְאָנוּ יוֹרְשִׁים אֶת הָאִשָּׁה:


נפל הבית עליו ועל אמו – and she has no other son other than this one. The inheritors of the son say that the woman died first and the son inherited her property and we inherit the son. But the inheritors of the woman from the family of the house of her father state that the son died first and that we inherit the woman.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 9:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:10:2)


**אֵלּוּ וָאֵלּוּ מוֹדִים.** בֵּית שַׁמַּאי וּבֵית הִלֵּל: **שֶׁיַּחֲלֹקוּ.** דְּכֵיוָן שֶׁשְּׁנֵיהֶם בָּאִים מִכֹּחַ יְרֻשָּׁה הָוֵי סָפֵק וְסָפֵק, וְיַחְלֹקוּ. וְלֹא דָּמֵי לְנָפַל הַבַּיִת עָלָיו וְעַל אִשְׁתּוֹ, דְּהָתָם יֵשׁ שְׁנֵי מִינֵי נְכָסִים, יֵשׁ שֶׁהַבַּעַל מֻחְזָק וְיֵשׁ שֶׁהָאִשָּׁה מֻחֶזֶקֶת, אֲבָל הָכָא הַכֹּל בְּחֶזְקַת הָאִשָּׁה כֵּיוָן שֶׁהָיְתָה אַלְמָנָה, וּשְׁנֵיהֶם בָּאִים מִכֹּחַ יְרֻשָּׁה לִירַשׁ הַכֹּל, הִלְכָּךְ יַחֲלֹקוּ:


אלו ואלו מודים [ – The School of Shammai and the School of Hillel [agree] that they should divide it. But this is not like the case where the house fell upon him and his wife. For in that case, there are two types of property. There is what the husband is in possession of and there is what the wife is in possession of, but here, everything is in the possession of the woman/wife since she was a widow. And both come from the power of inheritance to inherit everything. Therefore, it is divided.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 9:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:10:3)


**מוֹדֶה אֲנִי בָזֶה.** דִּלְדִבְרֵי בֵּית הִלֵּל הָכָא נַמִּי אָמְרִינַן נְכָסִים בְּחֶזְקָתָן, בְּחֶזְקַת יוֹרְשֶׁיהָ מִן הָאָב, שֶׁהֲרֵי בְּחַיֶּיהָ מִשֶּׁמֵּת בַּעְלָהּ אַחַר שֵׁבֶט אָבִיהָ הִיא מִתְיַחֶסֶת, וּמָמוֹנָהּ נַמִּי בְּחַיֶּיהָ הָיָה בְּחֶזְקַת שֵׁבֶט אָבִיהָ. הִלְכָּךְ יוֹרְשֶׁיהָ מִן הָאָב יוֹרְשִׁים אוֹתָהּ. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא:


מודה אני בזה – for according to the words of the School of Hillel, we say that is so that the property is left in the hands of the possessors, in the possession of her inheritors from the father’s side, for during her lifetime, once her husband died she is a connected through the tribe of her father. And her monies also during her lifetime are in the possession of her father’s tribe. Therefore, her inheritors from the father’s side inherit her, and the Halakha is according to Rabbi Akiba.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 9:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/9:10:4)


**עַל הַחֲלוּקִין אָנוּ מִצְטַעֲרִין.** כְּלוֹמַר, עַל מַחֲלֹקֶת דִּלְעֵיל דְּבֵית שַׁמַּאי וּבֵית הִלֵּל אָנוּ מִצְטַעֲרִים שֶׁלֹּא הִשְׁווּ דַּעְתָּן, וְאַתָּה אוֹמֵר שֶׁגַּם בָּזֶה חֲלוּקִין וּבָאתָ לַחְלֹק עַל תַּנָּא קַמָּא שֶׁאוֹמֵר שֶׁהֵם שָׁוִין:


על חלוקין אנו מצטערין – meaning to say, on the disagreement above of the Schools of Shammai and Hillel. We are saddened that they didn’t come to agreement and you say that also with this they are divided. And you came to dispute on the first Tanna/teacher, that they are both equivalent.



## Chapter 10


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 10:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:1:1)


**גֵּט פָּשׁוּט.** שְׁטָר פָּשׁוּט, כְּעֵין שֶׁלָּנוּ שֶׁאֵינָן תְּפוּרִים וּמְקֻשָּׁרִין. וְכָל שְׁטָר קָרוּי גֵּט:


גט פשוט – a plain document similar to ours which are not sewn or folded; and every document is called a Get.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 10:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:1:2)


**עֵדָיו מִתּוֹכוֹ.** מִבִּפְנִים כְּעֵין שֶׁאָנוּ עוֹשִׂים:


עדיו מתוכו – from the inside, similar to ours that we make.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 10:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:1:3)


**מְקֻשָּׁר מֵאֲחוֹרָיו.** כּוֹתֵב שִׁיטָה אַחַת אוֹ שְׁתַּיִם וְכוֹרְכָן עַל הֶחָלָק וְתוֹפֵר, וְעֵד אֶחָד חוֹתֵם עַל הַכֶּרֶךְ מִבַּחוּץ, וְחוֹזֵר וְכוֹתֵב שְׁנֵי שִׁיטִין אוֹ יוֹתֵר מִבִּפְנִים וְכוֹרְכָן עַל הֶחָלָק, וְחוֹתֵם עֵד שֵׁנִי עַל הַכֶּרֶךְ מִבַּחוּץ, וְכֵן עֵד שְׁלִישִׁי. וְתִקְּנוּ רַבָּנָן גֵּט מְקֻשָּׁר, מִשּׁוּם כֹּהֲנִים קַפְּדָנִים שֶׁהָיוּ כּוֹתְבִים גֵּט פִּתְאוֹם לִנְשׁוֹתֵיהֶם וּמִתְחָרְטִים וְלֹא הָיוּ יְכוֹלִין לְהַחְזִירָן, וְהִתְקִינוּ לָהֶם גֵּט מְקֻשָּׁר שֶׁאֵינוֹ נוֹחַ לִכָּתֵב מְהֵרָה, שֶׁמָּא בְּתוֹךְ כָּךְ יִתְפַּיֵּס. וּכְשֵׁם שֶׁתִּקְּנוּ מְקֻשָּׁר בְּגֵט, כָּךְ תִּקְּנוּ בִּשְׁאָר שְׁטָרוֹת, שֶׁלֹּא חִלְּקוּ חֲכָמִים:


מקושר מאחוריו – he (i.e., the scribe) writes one line or two and wraps them on the blank part and sews it. And one witnesses inscribes on the wrapping from the outside, and he (i.e., the scribe) goes back and writes two lines from the inside and wraps them on the blank part and the second witnesses inscribes on the wrapping from the outside. And similarly, the third witness. But the Rabbis established a folded document because of stringent Kohanim, because they would write a bill-of-divorcement suddenly to their wives and then regret it and they wouldn’t be able to restore them (i.e., because a Kohen cannot marry a divorced woman – see Leviticus 21:7), and the Rabbis ordained for them a folded bill-of-divorcement which is not easy to write quickly, lest in the midst of it, he is pacified. And just as they ordained/instituted a folded document for a Get, so they instituted for other documents that the Sages did not disagree with.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 10:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:1:4)


**שְׁנֵיהֶם פְּסוּלִין.** שֶׁלֹּא נַעֲשׂוּ כְּתִקּוּן חֲכָמִים:


שניהם פסולין – for they did not act as the Sages ordained.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 10:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:1:5)


**שֶׁיָּכוֹל לַעֲשׂוֹתוֹ פָשׁוּט.** שֶׁאִם יִקְרַע הַתְּפִירוֹת וְיִפְתַּח הַשְּׁטָר יִהְיֶה פָּשׁוּט:


שיכול לעשותו פשוט – for if the seams would tear and the Get would open, it would be a plain document.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 10:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:1:6)


**רַבָּן שִׁמְעוֹן בֶּן גַּמְלִיאֵל אוֹמֵר הַכֹּל כְּמִנְהַג הַמְּדִינָה.** פְּלֻגְתָּא דְּתַנָּא קַמָּא וְרַבָּן שִׁמְעוֹן בֶּן גַּמְלִיאֵל בְּאַתְרָא דִּנְהִיגֵי בְּפָשׁוּט וּבִמְקֻשָּׁר, וְאָמַר לֵיהּ עֲבֵד לִי מְקֻשָּׁר, וְאָזִיל וְעָבַד לֵיהּ פָּשׁוּט, אוֹ פָּשׁוּט וְעָבַד לֵיהּ מְקֻשָּׁר, תַּנָּא קַמָּא סָבַר מִקְפַּד קָפֵיד וּפָסוּל. וְרַבָּן שִׁמְעוֹן בֶּן גַּמְלִיאֵל סָבַר הוֹאִיל וּמִנְהַג הַמְּדִינָה בְּתַרְוַיְהוּ, לֹא קָפֵיד וְכָשֵׁר. וַהֲלָכָה כְּתַנָּא קַמָּא:


רשב"ג אומר הכל כמנההג המדינה – there is a dispute between Rabban Shimon ben Gamaliel and the Rabbis in a place where there is the practice of [both] a plain document and a folded document. If someone said to him: “Make for me a folded [document]” and he went and he made for him an ordinary document, or “Make for me an ordinary document” and he made for him a folded document, the first Tanna/teacher [of the Mishnah] holds that he is particular, and it is unfit. But Rabban Shimon ben Gamaliel holds that since with both (i.e., the plain document and folded document), it [depends upon] local custom, he is not strict and it is appropriate/fit, but the Halakha is according to the first Tanna/teacher [of the Mishnah] (see Talmud Bava Batra 165a).



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 10:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:2:1)


**פָּשׁוּט שֶׁכָּתוּב בּוֹ עֵד אֶחָד.** הָכִי קָאָמַר, כְּשֵׁם שֶׁפָּשׁוּט שֶׁכָּתוּב בּוֹ עֵד אֶחָד פָּסוּל מִדְּאוֹרַיְתָא, כָּךְ מְקֻשָּׁר שֶׁכָּתוּב בּוֹ שְׁנֵי עֵדִים פָּסוּל מִדְּאוֹרַיְתָא:


פשוט שכתוב בו עד אחד – this is what he said: just as when a plain document in which only one witness is written in it is unfit from the Torah, so also a folded document in which only two witnesses are written in it is also unfit from the Torah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 10:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:2:2)


**אֵין לוֹ אֶלָּא עֶשְׂרִים.** וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּזוּזִים מֵאָה, חֲמִשָּׁה וְעֶשְׂרִים סְלָעִים הֵם, יַד בַּעַל הַשְּׁטָר עַל הַתַּחְתּוֹנָה. וְהָכִי מְפָרְשִׁינַן לֵיהּ לִשְׁטָרָא, מֵאָה זוּזִים גְּרוּעִים, שֶׁאֵינָם שָׁוִין אֶלָּא עֶשְׂרִים סְלָעִים:


אין לו אלא עשרים – and even though [it states] one-hundred zuzim, they are twenty-five Selaim. The hand of the owner of the document is at a disadvantage. And this is how we interpret for him a document: one hundred inferior Zuzim are not worth other than twenty Selaim.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 10:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:2:3)


**אֵין לוֹ אֶלָּא מָנֶה.** עֶשְׂרִים וַחֲמִשָּׁה סְלָעִים. דְּהָכִי מְפָרְשִׁינַן לִשְׁטָרָא, מֵאָה זוּזִין דְּאִנּוּן תְּלָתִין סְלָעִים קַלִּים וּגְרוּעִים, שֶׁהֵם עֶשְׂרִים וַחֲמִשָּׁה מִן הַטּוֹבִים:


אין לי אלא מנה – twenty five Selaim, this is how we interpret it for a document: One hundred Zuzim which are thirty light and inferior Selaim, which are twenty-five from the good ones.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 10:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:2:4)


**וְנִמְחַק.** שֶׁנִּמְחַק הַמִּנְיָן שֶׁנִּכְתַּב אַחֲרָיו:


ונמחקו – the number that is written afterwards is erased.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 10:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:2:5)


**וּמִלְּמַטָּה מָאתַיִם.** כְּשֶׁכּוֹפֵל אֶת דְּבָרָיו בַּשְּׁטָר:


ומלמטה מאתים – when he doubles his words in the document.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 10:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:2:6)


**הַכֹּל הוֹלֵךְ אַחַר הַתַּחְתּוֹן.** וּבִלְבַד שֶׁלֹּא יִהְיֶה כָּתוּב בְּשִׁיטָה אַחֲרוֹנָה:


הכל הולך אחר התחתון – as long as it will not be written last in the document.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 10:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:2:7)


**לָמָּה כוֹתְבִין אֶת הָעֶלְיוֹן.** הוֹאִיל וּבְסוֹף הַשְּׁטָר חוֹזֵר וְכוֹפֵל, וְאַחֲרָיוּת מָמוֹן זֶה כָּךְ וְכָךְ קִבַּלְתִּי עָלַי:


למה כותבין את העליון – for since at the end, the document repeats and states it again, and the responsibility of this money such-and-such I accepted upon myself (see Talmud Bava Batra 167b).



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 10:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:3:1)


**כּוֹתְבִין גֵּט לָאִישׁ.** וְחוֹתְמִין, וְהוּא יְגָרֵשׁ מָתַי שֶׁיִּרְצֶה, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵין אִשְׁתּוֹ עִמּוֹ, שֶׁהֲרֵי הִיא מִתְגָּרֶשֶׁת בְּעַל כָּרְחָהּ:


כותב גט לאיש – and [the witnesses] inscribe [their names], and he will divorce here when he desires, and even though his wife is not with him, for she is divorced against her will.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 10:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:3:2)


**וְשׁוֹבֵר לָאִשָּׁה.** שֶׁהִיא עוֹשָׂה לְבַעֲלָהּ עַל כְּתֻבָּתָהּ, דְּחוֹב הוּא לָהּ וּזְכוּת לְבַעֲלָהּ, וְזָכִין לָאָדָם שֶׁלֹּא בְּפָנָיו:


ושובר לאשה – which she does for her husband regarding her Jewish marriage settlement/Ketubah, for it is a liability for him to her, and is a benefit for her husband, as we obtain a privilege in behalf of a person in his absence (see Tractate Eruvin, Chapter 7, Mishnah 11).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 10:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:3:3)


**וּבִלְבַד שֶׁיְּהֵא מַכִּירָן.** שֶׁיְּהֵא הַסּוֹפֵר וְהָעֵדִים מַכִּירִין הָאִישׁ וְהָאִשָּׁה, בֵּין בְּגֵט בֵּין בְּשׁוֹבֵר. שֶׁאִם אֵינוֹ מַכִּירָן, שֶׁמָּא יִכְתֹּב גֵּט עַל שֵׁם אֵשֶׁת אִישׁ אַחֵר שֶׁשְּׁמוֹ כִּשְׁמוֹ וְתוֹצִיא אוֹתוֹ הַגֵּט אִשָּׁה שֶׁאֵינָהּ מְגֹרֶשֶׁת בּוֹ. וְכֵן בְּשׁוֹבֵר:


ובלבד שיהא מכירן – that the scribe and the witnesses recognize/know the man and the woman [getting divorced]., whether with a Jewish bill of divorce or a receipt, for if they do not recognize them lest he write a Get/Jewish bill of divorce in the name of another married woman where his name is the same as the other man’s name, and she should take out that same Jewish bill of divorce where she is not divorced by it and similarly for the receipt.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 10:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:3:4)


**וְהַלּוֶֹה נוֹתֵן שָׂכָר.** וַאֲפִלּוּ הִיא עִסְקָא דְּפַלְגָּא מִלְוָה וּפַלְגָּא פִּקָּדוֹן, אֲפִלּוּ הָכִי מְקַבֵּל הָעִסְקָא הוּא נוֹתֵן כָּל הַשָּׂכָר לַסּוֹפֵר:


והלוה נותן שכר – even if it is giving a person goods to trade with another (usually two shares of the profit going to the investor and one to the trader) where half of it is a loan and half of it is a deposit. Nevertheless, the person receiving the business pays the fee to the scribe (see Talmud Bava Batra 168a).



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 10:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:4:1)


**שְׁטָרֵי אֲרִיסוּת.** הַיּוֹרֵד לְקַרְקַע לְעָבְדָהּ וּלְשָׁמְרָהּ לְמֶחֱצָה לִשְׁלִישׁ וְלִרְבִיעַ:


שטרי אריסות – the person who goes down to the land to work it and to guard it for one-half, one-third or one-fourth [of the profit].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 10:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:4:2)


**קַבְּלָנוּת.** בְּכָךְ וְכָךְ כּוֹרִין לְשָׁנָה, בֵּין עָשְׂתָה בֵּין לֹא עָשְׂתָה:


קבלנות – for such-and-such Khorim per year, whether it produced or it didn’t produce.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 10:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:4:3)


**שְׁטָרֵי בֵרוּרִין.** זֶה בּוֹרֵר לוֹ אֶחָד שֶׁיָּדוּן לוֹ וְזֶה בּוֹרֵר לוֹ אֶחָד, וְכוֹתְבִין בִּכְתָב, פְּלוֹנִי בֵּרֵר דַּיָּן פְּלוֹנִי וְטַעֲנוֹתָיו כָּךְ וְכָךְ, כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יִהְיוּ חוֹזְרִים וְטוֹעֲנִים:


שטרי בירורין – this one (i.e. litigant) chooses one judge who will judge for him and that one (i.e. litigant) chooses for himself one [judge] and they write in a document: “so-and-so chose judge so-and-so and his claims are this and that,” in order that they don’t retract and raise a complaint.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 10:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:4:4)


**כּוֹתְבִים שְׁנַיִם.** לְכָל אֶחָד מִבַּעֲלֵי דִּינִים שְׁטָר בִּפְנֵי עַצְמוֹ שֶׁיִּהְיוּ טַעֲנוֹתָיו סְדוּרִים בּוֹ. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבָּן שִׁמְעוֹן בֶּן גַּמְלִיאֵל, אֶלָּא בִּשְׁטָר אֶחָד כּוֹתְבִין טְעָנוֹת שֶׁל שְׁנֵי בַּעֲלֵי דִּינִים, וְשֶׁפְּלוֹנִי בֵּרֵר פְּלוֹנִי דַּיָּן שֶׁיָּדוּן לוֹ וּפְלוֹנִי בֵּרֵר פְּלוֹנִי דַּיָּן אַחֵר שֶׁיָּדוּן לוֹ:


כותבים שנים – to each one of the litigants a separate document in which their claims are arranged in it, but the Halakha is not according to Rabban Shimon ben Gamaliel. But rather, they write one document containing the claims of each of the litigants and that so-and-so (i.e., the litigant) chose so-and-so as a judge that would adjudicate for him and so-and-so (i.e., the other litigant) chosen so-and-so as a judge that would adjudicate for him.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 10:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:5:1)


**וְהִשְׁלִישׁ אֶת שְׁטָרוֹ.** הַמַּלְוֶה וְהַלֹּוֶה מָסְרוּ הַשְּׁטָר בְּיַד שָׁלִישׁ. שֶׁטֹּרַח הָיָה לָהֶם לִכְתֹּב שׁוֹבֵר וְסָמְכוּ עַל הַשָּׁלִישׁ:


והשליש את שטרו – the lender and the borrower transferred the document to the hand of a third-party for it was a trouble for them to write a receipt and they relied upon the third party.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 10:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:5:2)


**רַבִּי יוֹסֵי אוֹמֵר יִתֵּן.** דִּסְבִירָא לֵיהּ אַסְמַכְתָּא קַנְיָא. הַמַּבְטִיחַ לַחֲבֵרוֹ דָּבָר עַל מְנָת שֶׁיַּעֲשֶׂה לוֹ דָּבָר לֶעָתִיד וְסוֹמֵךְ בְּלִבּוֹ בִּשְׁעַת הַתְּנַאי שֶׁיּוּכַל לְקַיֵּם וּכְשֶׁהִגִּיעַ הַזְּמַן אֵינוֹ יָכוֹל לְקַיֵּם, זוֹ קְרוּיָה אַסְמַכְתָּא, וּלְדִבְרֵי רַבִּי יוֹסֵי קַנְיָא. וּלְעִנְיַן פְּסַק הֲלָכָה, אַסְמַכְתָּא לֹא קַנְיָא, אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן קָנוּ מִיָּדוֹ בְּבֵית דִּין חָשׁוּב, וְהִתְפִּיס זְכֻיּוֹתָיו בְּאוֹתוֹ בֵּית דִּין שֶׁמָּסַר שְׁטָרוֹתָיו וּרְאָיוֹתָיו, וְאָמַר אִי לֹא אֲתִינָא מִכָּאן וְעַד שְׁלֹשִׁים יוֹם לִבָּטְלָן זְכֻיּוֹתַי. וְרַבּוֹתַי מְפָרְשִׁים שֶׁכָּל בֵּית דִּין שֶׁבְּקִיאִים וְיוֹדְעִים בְּדִינֵי אַסְמַכְתָּא נִקְרָאִים בֵּית דִּין חָשׁוּב לְעִנְיָן זֶה. אֲבָל הָרַמְבַּ"ם אוֹמֵר שֶׁאֵין בֵּית דִּין חָשׁוּב אֶלָּא בֵּית דִּין הַסָּמוּךְ בְּאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל:


ר' יוסי אומר יתן – for he holds that Asmakhta (i.e., a promise to submit to a forfeiture of pledged property – or equivalent – without having received a sufficient consideration) is a valid legal transfer of property (see Talmud Bava Batra 168a). A person who promises t his fellow something on the condition that he will do something for him in the future, and he relies upon his intention at the time of the condition that he would be able to fulfill it. But when the time comes, he is not able to fulfill it. This is called Asmakhta. But with regard to the Jewish legal decision, Asmakhta does not acquire/gives no title, unless he acquired it from his hand in an important Jewish court. But he caused to take hold of his merits in the same Jewish court where he transferred his documents and his proofs, and he said: “if I don’t bring it from now until thirty days, my benefits will be voided. But my teachers/Rabbis explain that every Jewish court that are specialists and know the laws of Asmakhta are called an important Jewish court in this matter. But Maimonides says that there is no important Jewish court other than a Jewish court ordained in the Land of Israel.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 10:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:6:1)


**מִי שֶׁנִּמְחַק שְׁטַר חוֹבוֹ.** וְיֵשׁ עֵדִים שֶׁרָאוּ כְּשֶׁנִּמְחַק מֵעַצְמוֹ אוֹ נִטַּשְׁטֵשׁ עַל יְדֵי מַיִם:


מי שנמחק שטרו – and there were witnesses who saw when it was blotted out on its own, or smudged by water.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 10:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:6:2)


**מַעֲמִיד עָלָיו עֵדִים.** שֶׁיּוֹדְעִים מֵאֵימָתַי נִכְתַּב וּמֶה הָיָה כָּתוּב בּוֹ, וְהֵן עוֹשִׂין לוֹ קִיּוּם בִּפְנֵי בֵּית דִּין וְכוֹתְבִין לוֹ כָּל מַה שֶּׁהָיָה כָּתוּב בְּאוֹתוֹ שְׁטָר:


מעמיד עליו עדים – that know from when it was written and what was written in it, and they make for him an attestation before a Jewish court and they write for him everything that had been written in that document.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 10:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:6:3)


**יַחֲלִיף.** יִקְרַע אוֹתוֹ שְׁטָר וְיִכְתְּבוּ לוֹ אַחֵר כְּפִי הַחֶשְׁבּוֹן הַנִּשְׁאָר:


יחליף – he should tear the document and write another according to the remaining balance.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 10:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:6:4)


**נִמְצָא זֶה.** הַלֹּוֶה צָרִיךְ לִשְׁמֹר שׁוֹבְרוֹ מִן הָעַכְבָּרִים, שֶׁאִם יֹאבַד, יִגְבֶּה הַמַּלְוֶה אֶת כָּל חוֹבוֹ:


נמצא זה – the borrower needs to guard his receipt from the mice, for if he loses it, the creditor will collect all of his liability.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 10:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:6:5)


**אָמַר רַבִּי יוֹסֵי וְכֵן יָפֶה לוֹ.** לַמַּלְוֶה, שֶׁיְּהֵא הַלֹּוֶה צָרִיךְ לִשְׁמֹר שׁוֹבְרוֹ וִימַהֵר לִפְרֹעַ לוֹ, וְלֹא יֵרַע כֹּחוֹ שֶׁל מַלְוֶה לִכְתֹּב שְׁטָר אַחֵר, שֶׁהָיָה זְמַן חוֹבוֹ מֻקְדָּם וְיָשׁוּב עַתָּה מְאֻחָר וְלֹא יוּכַל לִטְרֹף לָקוֹחוֹת אֶלָּא מִזְּמַן הַשְּׁטָר הַשֵּׁנִי. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי שֶׁכּוֹתְבִין שׁוֹבֵר:


אמר רבי יוסי וכן יפה לו – to the creditor, for the borrower must guard his receipt and hurry to pay him back, and no harm will come to the creditor to write another document that had an advanced time for the liability [to be paid off] and that he returns now and he would not be able to seize the sold property unlawfully other than from the time of the second document. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yosi that we write a receipt.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 10:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:7:1)


**קַח לְךָ עֲבָדִים.** שֶׁיְּחַמְּמוּ לְךָ הַמֶּרְחָץ. דִּכְמוֹ שֶׁהִנִּיחָהּ אָבִינוּ כֵּן תִּהְיֶה לְעוֹלָם. וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּאִית לָן בְּפֶרֶק קַמָּא דִּבְמִידֵי דְּלֵית בָּהּ דִּין חֲלֻקָּה יָכוֹל לְמֵימַר גּוֹד אוֹ אֱגוֹד, כְּלוֹמַר מְכֹר לִי חֶלְקְךָ אוֹ אֲנִי אֶמְכֹּר לְךָ חֶלְקִי, שָׁאנֵי הָכָא, שֶׁאֵין הֶעָנִי יָכוֹל לוֹמַר אֱגוֹד, שֶׁאֵין לוֹ בַּמֶּה לִקְנוֹת:


קח לך עבדים – that they should warm up for you the bathhouse that it is like what our father left us, so it shall be forever, and even though that we have in the first chapter (Tractate Bava Batra, Chapter 1, Mishnayot 1 and 6), concerning something where there isn’t the law of division. One can say, “I will make a wall or I will divide it,” meaning to say, “sell me your part or I will sell [to you] my part. But it is different here because the poor person cannot say, “I will divide it” because he has nothing with which to buy.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 10:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:7:2)


**אֵינָן יְכוֹלִין לְהוֹצִיא שְׁטָר חוֹב זֶה עַל זֶה.** דְּכָל אֶחָד מֵהֶם יָכוֹל לִטְעֹן זֶה הַשְּׁטָר שֶׁבְּיָדְךָ אֲנִי הֶחֱזַרְתִּי לְךָ כְּשֶׁפָּרַעְתָּ לִי הַמָּעוֹת שֶׁהִלְוִיתִיךָ:


אינן יכולין להוציא שטר חוב זה על זה – because each one can claim that this document that is in your hands, I returned to you when I paid you repaid me the monies that I lent to you.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 10:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:7:3)


**וְלֹא אַחֵר יָכוֹל לְהוֹצִיא שְׁטָר חוֹב עֲלֵיהֶם.** דְּכָל אֶחָד מָצֵי מַדְּחֵי לֵיהּ לְגַבֵּי חַבְרֵיהּ:


ולא אחד יכול להוציא שטר חוב עליהם – because each one can supersede him regarding his fellow.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 10:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:7:4)


**יְשַׁלֵּשׁוּ.** יִכְתֹּב שֵׁם אֲבִי אָבִיו:


ישלשו – he will write the name of his father’s father.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 10:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:7:5)


**וְאִם הָיוּ מְשֻׁלָּשִׁים.** שֶׁשְּׁמוֹתָן וּשְׁמוֹת אֲבוֹתֵיהֶן וַאֲבוֹת אֲבוֹתֵיהֶן שָׁוִין:


ואם היו משולשים – that their names and the names of their fathers and the names of their father’s fathers are the same.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 10:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:7:6)


**יִכְתְּבוּ סִימָן.** פְּלוֹנִי, שֶׁהוּא גִּיחוֹר אוֹ אָרֹךְ אוֹ גּוּץ. וְאִם הֵן שָׁוִין בַּסִּימָנִין, יִכְתְּבוּ כֹּהֵן. אִם אֶחָד כֹּהֵן וְאֶחָד יִשְׂרָאֵל:


יכתבו סימן – a certain [sign] that this is red-spotted or long or short and if their signs are the same, they should write, “Kohen,” if one is a Kohen and the other is an Israelite.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 10:7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:7:7)


**נִמְצָא.** לְלֹוֶה אֶחָד שָׁם שְׁנֵי שְׁטָרוֹת מִשְּׁתֵּי הַלְוָאוֹת שֶׁלָּוָה מִמֶּנּוּ:


נמצא – to one borrower there two documents from two loans that he borrowed from him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 10:7:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:7:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:7:8)


**הַקָּטָן אֵינוֹ פָרוּעַ.** דִּשְׁטָר אֶחָד בֵּין שְׁטָרוֹתָיו קָאָמַר, וְלֹא שְׁנַיִם:


הקטן אינו פרוע – that one document among his documents is spoken of and not two.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 10:7:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:7:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:7:9)


**לֹא יִפָּרַע מִן הֶעָרֵב.** תְּחִלָּה, עַד שֶׁיִּתְבַּע אֶת הַלֹּוֶה לַדִּין וִיחַיְּבוּ אוֹתוֹ בֵּית דִּין, וְאִם אֵין לוֹ מַה לְּשַׁלֵּם אָז יִפָּרַע מִן הֶעָרֵב:


לא יפרע מן הערב – first, until the borrower is brought to court, and the Jewish court makes him liable [to pay it off]; and if he doesn’t have wherewith to pay, then he can collect from the guarantor.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bava Batra 10:7:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:7:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bava%20Batra/r/10:7:10)


**רַבָּן שִׁמְעוֹן בֶּן גַּמְלִיאֵל אוֹמֵר אִם יֵשׁ נְכָסִים לַלֹּוֶה.** לֹא יִפָּרַע מִן הֶעָרֵב. לַאו מִכְּלָל דְּתַנָּא קַמָּא סָבַר אֲפִלּוּ אִם יֵשׁ נְכָסִים לַלֹּוֶה יִפָּרַע מִן הֶעָרֵב, אֶלָּא מַתְנִיתִין חֲסוּרֵי מִחַסְּרָא וְהָכִי קָתָנֵי, הַמַּלְוֶה אֶת חֲבֵרוֹ עַל יְדֵי עָרֵב לֹא יִפָּרַע מִן הֶעָרֵב, וְאִם אָמַר עַל מְנָת שֶׁאֶפָּרַע מִמִּי שֶׁאֶרְצֶה, יִפָּרַע מִן הֶעָרֵב. בַּמֶּה דְּבָרִים אֲמוּרִים, כְּשֶׁאֵין נְכָסִים לַלֹּוֶה, אֲבָל יֵשׁ נְכָסִים לַלֹּוֶה לֹא יִפָּרַע מִן הֶעָרֵב. וְקַבְּלָן, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁיֵּשׁ נְכָסִים לַלֹּוֶה יִפָּרַע מִן הַקַּבְּלָן. רַבָּן שִׁמְעוֹן בֶּן גַּמְלִיאֵל אוֹמֵר, אֶחָד עָרֵב וְאֶחָד קַבְּלָן, אִם יֵשׁ נְכָסִים לַלֹּוֶה לֹא יִפָּרַע מֵהֶן. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבָּן שִׁמְעוֹן בֶּן גַּמְלִיאֵל. עָרֵב, הוּא שֶׁאוֹמֵר תֵּן לוֹ וַאֲנִי עָרֵב. קַבְּלָן, הוּא שֶׁאוֹמֵר תֵּן לוֹ וַאֲנִי נוֹתֵן לְךָ:


רבן שמעון בן גמליאל אומר אם יש נכסים ללוה – he should not collect payment from the guarantor, not from the fact that the first Tanna/teacher holds that even if the borrower has property, he should collect payment from the guarantor, but rather, because our Mishnah is deficient, and this is how it should be taught: A person who loans his fellow through a guarantor should not collect payment from the guarantor, but if he said, “on condition that I can collect payment from whomever I desire,” he can collect payment from the guarantor. When is this said? When the borrower lacks property, but if the borrower has property, he should collect payment from the guarantor, or from a person who assumes the other man’s obligations unconditionally (see Talmud Bava Batra 173b and 174a), even though the borrower has property, he should collect from the person who assumes the other man’s obligations unconditionally. Rabban Shimon ben Gamaliel says that both the guarantor and the man who assumes the other person’s obligations unconditionally are the same: if the borrower has property, he (i.e., the creditor) may not collect from them.
